ACOLYTE 
Items: 
●​
Proselytize 
●​
Question your belief 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Fervor 
Whenever you roll at least three successes on a roll to intimidate or shame, you may regain one psyche burst. 
●​
Apostasy 
At the end of a hunt, you may select one exorcist whose actions rattled your beliefs, and give them 1 xp. Until the end of the next hunt, 
with their consent you may swap one of your bolded agenda items with one of theirs. 
●​
Atonement 
You gain +1 rest dice if you are at or above ½ of your sin cap. 
●​
Banishment 
Once per hunt, if a sin has taken any slashes on its talisman you may take 1D3 sin and forcibly fling it back to its palace, which it cannot 
leave or exert influence beyond until the next scene. 
●​
Confession 
You gain +1 maximum stress and heal 1 stress when you answer a trauma question correctly. This bonus resets when the hunt ends. 
 
Passive: Devout 
Until you advance out of this agenda, you are incapable of questioning the morals or ethics of CAIN, including your own treatment. 
 
THUNDERHOOF 
Items: 
●​
Move before you think 
●​
Slow down and think 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Rumination 
You may choose to not use up to 2 dice when you rest. These unused dice are saved, and you may once per scene choose to add one 
saved dice to a roll made by you or your allies. Unspent dice are lost at the start of the next rest. 
●​
Fleet Of Foot 
If any roll you make would be affected negatively by difficult terrain or environmental hazards, you may gain +1D on that roll by taking 1 
nonlethal stress. 
●​
Twitch 
Once per rest you may move faster than/dodge a projectile of your choice for one action. If the projectile is something extremely fast, like a 
bullet or laser, you cannot use this again this mission. 
●​
Prey Instinct 
You are vaguely aware of the directions of all beings below your CAT that are watching or stalking you within a long distance, but not 
where or what they are. 
●​
Rolling Stone 
When you would mark your last slash on a hook, roll 1D3. On a 3, you don't slash the hook. Once you successfully negate a slash this 
way, this ability cannot be used until the start of the next rest. 
 
ADVOCATE 
Items: 
●​
Protest injustice 
●​
Abandon the cause 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Closed Shop 
Guidelines on personal items, clothing styles, curfews, and self styling are more lenient for your allied exorcists. All exorcists in your group 
start with comfortable shoes and casual wear, and once per hunt a kit item each ally pulls out costs 1 less KP to pull out, minimum of 0, 
and refunds 1 scrip if it was a purchased consumable that cost at least 1 scrip. 
●​
Fellows In Arms 
Once per hunt, you may use an action to forsake your turn (not triggering a reaction) and grant an ally of your choice a second action that 
does not trigger a reaction. This action must be taken before the end of the round. 
●​
Rally 
Once per hunt, when an allied exorcist within reach of you rolls a 6 on a dice, you may take 1 stress and allow them to roll another dice. 
This ability refreshes after you rest. 
●​
Bellows 
Your voice can easily carry an unnatural distance equal to your CAT, and can raise to a volume loud enough to make ears ring. 
●​
Thunder Under Earth 
All actions related to mending, forming, or strengthening relations with the working class of the area gain +1D. Police, government, and 
military employees inherently distrust you. 
 
Passive: Iconoclast 
If you end a hunt by sparing the sin, earn 2 extra scrip. If you end a hunt by executing the sin, take 1D3 sin. 
 
 


 
BLANK 
Items: 
●​
Scare someone to death 
●​
Comfort the terrified 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Who’s There? 
Once per rest if you are hiding from a target, you can be considered invisible for the duration of a single action if the target turns around 
and would see you. 
●​
Tapping In The Walls 
Gain +1D to any roll made to create a distraction or guide someone’s attention if they cannot see you or you are within moderate 
darkness. 
●​
Wail 
You gain the ability to accurately recreate the voice of anyone you have heard in the same mission, and throw it up to a short distance. 
Once used, you cannot use this ability again until the next rest. 
●​
Cheshire 
Once per hunt, you may flash an eerie smile at a target you can see within long range. Their attention is immediately snapped to your 
direction and their train of thought is fully interrupted. This ability refreshes if you kill the target. 
●​
Subdermal 
Whenever you would deal enough slashes on a talisman to kill a mundane creature, you may instead put it to sleep harmlessly until the 
next rest. While they are asleep in this way, checks made to impersonate them by you or any ally gain +1D. 
 
Passive: Uncanny Valley 
At first glance people will always assume you are wearing a mask or fake face of some kind. You aren’t. This can easily influence witness reports 
and people trying to identify you. 
 
WARDOG 
Items: 
●​
Distance yourself 
●​
Take someone under your wing 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Shellshock 
Once per hunt you may take an injury when you suffer sin overflow to negate it and lose all use of your psychic abilities until the next rest. 
●​
Battle Scars 
This ability can only be taken if you have suffered grievous physical injury, lost a limb, or have been heavily scarred. Your stress cap 
reduces by one, but the threshold for instant death increases to 9 stress in one instance. 
●​
Yellow Paint 
Once per rest you may break a wooden barrel or box within close distance to yourself and flip a coin. You find: 1. Ammo (your next 
successful attack with a mundane weapon deals 1 more slash) or 2. Herb (consume to heal 2 stress)  
These last until the next rest. 
●​
Slacked Leash 
Once per session, receiving praise or gratitude from a person with higher hierarchical or official standing than yourself grants +1D to one 
action of your choice within the same scene. 
●​
Top Brass 
Military and law officers inherently trust you. Gain +1D when deceiving or persuading them.  
 
JUNKIE 
Items: 
●​
Risk it all 
●​
Exercise caution 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Crash Out 
When you activate Divine Agony, you may double the Divine Agony dice but take stress equal to twice the number of failures on the roll. 
This may go beyond the bonus dice cap. 
●​
Kicks 
You possess some form of skating gear, your choice of roller skates, a longboard, or heelies, that costs 0 Kit Points to pull out. You gain 
+1D on all movement across roads and on concrete surfaces as long as you are using your gear.  
●​
Nectar 
1/rest when you indulge in a vice of your choice (alcohol, nicotine, drugs, etc), you may regain 1 psyche burst.  
●​
Wild Abandon 
You may remove difficulty from a roll as part of an attack action, but you take +1d3 stress from all sources for the rest of the round. 
●​
The Mindkiller 
You lose the ability to feel fear or danger entirely. 1/scene on a 1 on any risk roll, erase 1 sin and 1d3 stress.  
 
 


 
FIELDMOUSE 
Items: 
●​
Run away like a coward.  
●​
Be brave. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Yellowbelly 
You receive +1D on actions relating to abandoning or sabotaging others to die while you escape or flee. 
●​
The Smallest Person 
1/rest you may fully ignore the effects of a non-attack risk roll by actively betraying a person who trusts you. 
●​
Doormat 
Twice a hunt, you can pathetically fawn at someone’s feet instead of rolling to persuade them. Within reason they will take pity on you, and 
you automatically succeed. 
●​
Path of Least Resistance 
You can spend 2 scrip to add +1D to a check and convince a civilian to do your work for you instead. 
●​
Lionkiller 
You can effortlessly claim responsibility for things you haven’t actually done. Gain +1D when lying through your teeth about other’s 
accomplishments being your own, and if you succeed others cannot be convinced otherwise. 
 
HARLEQUIN 
Items: 
●​
Laugh it off like always. 
●​
Take things seriously for once. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Punchline 
1/rest your charm takes on an almost supernatural twist. When attempting to talk to mundane humans using humor, Flip a coin to activate 
this ability:  
○​
Win: Laugh Track. You and any allies gain +1D on the next CAT actions interacting with the target. 
○​
Lose: Tough Crowd. Receive -1D to the action. If the dice pool drops to 0, roll a risk die. 
●​
The Best Medicine 
1/mission when you gain an affliction, you may take 1d3 sin. If you do, the affliction only lasts for a scene. 
●​
Absurdity 
Once after gaining this ability, failing to Defy Fate does not turn you into an Imago, instead bringing out a sadistic bend in your personality. 
Permanently add to your agenda: enjoy watching someone suffer. If there is no space, replace one bolded item. Once triggered, replace 
this ability with another Harlequin ability. You cannot take this ability again. 
●​
Practical Joker 
1/mission you can describe a silly trap to cause someone following or chasing you to suffer a comical mishap, rendering them stunned for 
the duration of a reaction. This ability refreshes after a rest or you expend 2 KP to refresh it. 
●​
Divine Comedy 
You become fundamentally unable to experience anything except mirth. Your sin cap increases by 2, and at the start of a hunt choose one 
ally to gain the same benefit, which lasts until the hunt ends.  
 
STRAY 
Items: 
●​
Cry about it. 
●​
Get over yourself. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Waterfall 
Whenever you fail a check to convince or distract someone, you can take 1d3 non-injuring stress to hold their attention for enough time for 
your allies to take one action. 
●​
Gloom 
1/rest you can take the negative energy in the room entirely for yourself, erasing up to 1d3 sin from another ally and transferring it to 
yourself. 
●​
God I’m The Worst 
Once per mission you can take responsibility for the poor outcomes of another person’s poor choices, taking all of the consequences of a 
risk roll. This can include taking their place in a severe attack and flinging them to safety. 
●​
World’s Smallest Violin 
Humans will instinctively and awkwardly avoid eye contact and being around you whenever you are visibly crying or dejected. Gain +1D 
when trying to pass unnoticed or cause a distraction in this way. 
●​
Pity The Poor Fool 
You are entirely immune to effects that would manipulate your emotions and desires, at the cost of being fundamentally unable to be truly 
happy. 
 
 


 
 
LONGHORN 
Items: 
●​
Take your pain out on others. 
●​
Spare others your fury. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Red Coals 
1/scene when someone attempts to rectify a check you failed and succeeds, you can take 1 stress to gain +1D on your next attempt to 
one-up them. 
●​
Eruption 
1/mission you may explode into a storm of suppressed anger, instantly killing one human or NPC exorcist of your choice without needing 
to roll, regardless of their talisman or abilities, as long as you can reach them. 
●​
Liquid Fire 
1/mission you can spend 2 KP to summon a vice of your choice and imbibe it, taking -1D to two mental skills but gaining +1D to two 
physical skills, or vice versa. This effect lasts until the end of the scene, as you throw up and take 1d3+1 sin. 
●​
Fuck You In Particular 
You gain +1D to roll harm against a target if they have harmed you within the round, and -1D if they have not. 
●​
Seethe 
1/mission when you are categorically beaten in an argument or disproven in a debate by someone that is not an ally, your next roll to harm 
something (including objects) cannot be hard. This ability refreshes after a rest. 
 
Passive: China Shop 
Nuance is not your thing. Take a -2 to social skill rolls when using them for anything other than coercion and intimidation. 
 
PARAGON 
Items: 
●​
Prove you’re the greatest. 
●​
Give someone else credit. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Golden God 
1/mission when you would take stress, reduce it by 3. If it goes negative, you gain temporary “shield” stress equal to the negative amount 
left over. 
●​
Absolute Majesty 
1/rest you can suppress the effects of any affliction affecting you for the duration of exactly one scene. It then suddenly triggers at the start 
of the next scene. 
●​
Never Send A Human 
To do an exorcist’s job. Gain +1D when trying to take over an activity from a mundane human, even if you don’t know how to actually do it. 
●​
Built Different 
Whenever you succeed a hard roll with at least two 6s, heal 1d3 stress and regain 1d3 psyche bursts. 
●​
You’re Welcome 
1/mission when an ally fails a check you may butt in rudely and take over, rerolling for them. If you succeed, they have to thank you. If you 
fail, it’s their fault. 
 
FOOL 
ItemsL 
●​
Save the day heroically. 
●​
Get a reality check. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Onward!​
Your movement cannot be impeded by crowds of people or hordes of enemies, and 1/rest you may nominate one other ally to gain this 
effect for a scene. 
●​
Hero’s Journey​
You gain +1xp at the end of every hunt. Other players may spend your xp as their own with your permission. 
●​
The Heart​
1/rest when you take an injury, you can shout a cheesy catchphrase to let all allies erase an amount of stress equal to the number of 
exorcists in your team (divided how they wish). 
●​
Dungeon Delve​
1/hunt when you enter a palace or psychically distorted area, you may force it to assume a procedural, sensible dungeon layout. This does 
not alter its appearance or nature, only the shape of its rooms and hallways 
●​
Loot Goblin​
You may choose one handheld mundane item you found during the hunt to keep with you at the end of the mission. You can store up to 
CAT of these items at your dorm, and bring one stored item on a hunt. 
 
 


 
SHAMAN 
Items: 
●​
Keep your secrets. 
●​
Share your knowledge. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Bookwyrm 
1/rest when picturing a face or full name in your head, you immediately learn the location of any book or print that includes a picture of 
them or their name on a page within the same room. If nothing turns up, you regain this ability at the end of the scene. 
●​
Dogeared 
You may pull out any book or piece of print you have seen at least once before for 1 KP. This is a copy, and does not steal the original. 
●​
Antiquarian 
You gain +1D when making an action pertaining to or using information, places, or people more than 75 years older than you are.  
●​
Know It All 
1/pressure if you Set Up an ally on a non-attack action and they use a skill that is lower than yours, they may use your number in that skill 
instead. 
●​
The Letterbox 
1/mission you may receive a suspicious, embossed and well-calligraphed letter at the edge of your resting place after a rest. It contains 
one small piece of evidence or small object that could be used to answer a trauma question, but you do not learn the context nor which 
question it answers. 
 
FRAGILE 
Items: 
●​
Break down and lose it. 
●​
Lock the fuck in. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
We’re All Gonna Die 
The first time any exorcist takes an injury or an affliction, you may take the same effect and prevent one instance of an injury or affliction 
from occurring before the end of the mission. 
●​
It’s a Trap! 
Gain +1D on the first 2 actions you take if you walk into a trap or ambush. 
●​
Worried To Death 
Whenever you slash a hook, you may erase 1d2-1 stress. 
●​
Ode to Capgras 
1/scene, you may sense the presence of any impostor or impersonator within short range. You know of their presence, but not who they 
are pretending to be, where they are, or their true identity. 
●​
Under Pressure 
If one or more of your allies is on the brink of death or dead, you gain -1 sin cap and +2 stress cap for each one. 
 
Passive: When you take this agenda, gain a crippling feeling of anxiety at all times. Your rolls gain -1D when trying to intimidate someone or 
appear confident. 
 
DAYDREAMER 
Items: 
●​
Cling to the past if it kills you 
●​
Let what you love go. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Twist the Knife 
This ability may only be taken if you have watched at least one allied exorcist die and not be resurrected. 1/rest you may catch a glimpse 
of a person that reminds you of them, rolling a rest dice immediately and adding one to the result. You roll one less die the next time you 
actually rest. 
●​
Rose Tinted Glasses 
You may gain +1D on rolls made to manipulate humans via reminiscing and muddling around in the past. You are unaware you are doing 
so, and are often not sober during the process. 
●​
Noli Oblivisci  
You remember the name, face, and story of every ally you have ever had, and no amount of memory manipulation, resetting, or temporal 
warping can erase your knowledge of them. You are required to take notes about this, not the GM. 
●​
Heartstrings 
When you suffer sin overflow, all allies that can see you may erase 1d3 sin. When you die (and do not resurrect) all allies may 
permanently increase their sin and stress caps by 2; an exorcist can only benefit from this effect once in their life. 
●​
The Modern Orpheus 
Choose one deceased allied exorcist. You may utilize one of their agenda abilities or blasphemy powers once per mission as long as you 
have this agenda ability. 
 
 


 
EEL 
Items: 
●​
Swindle something in your favour. 
●​
Give someone a good deal. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Faustian Bargain 
Whenever you attempt to make a deal with a mundane human, roll 1d6. On a 5+, you gain a loophole you can exploit to effortlessly void 
the deal if necessary, without rolling to convince them to drop the deal. 
●​
Salivation 
You can roll to steal objects that normally couldn’t be stolen easily, such as signposts and holstered items, without it being hard.  
●​
Honeypot Plot 
1/scene you can spend a number of scrip equal to the level of suspicion an NPC has towards you to negate it and let you perform one 
action they will ignore:  
○​
1. mild 
○​
2. moderate 
○​
3. extreme 
○​
4. severe 
●​
Flash Sale 
You may pawn off any non-consumable non-weapon kit item you have pulled out to regain equivalent KP by rolling for it. If you do so, you 
cannot pull out that item again. 
●​
Flea Bag 
You can barter an object at up to twice its actual value before mundane humans begin to suspect a scam. 
 
RETAINER 
Items: 
●​
Defend your honor. 
●​
Betray your oath. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Crown of Thorns 
1/scene you may take 1d3 stress to defend your liege without taking your action. 
●​
Writ in Blood 
Take -1 stress from mundane attacks while within short range of your liege. 
●​
Avenger 
You gain +1D on any actions against a creature that has attacked your liege this round. 
●​
Honor Maketh Man 
At the end of a session, you may address a character that you feel has benefitted your liege at the cost of their own safety, clearing 1d2 
sin from them. A character can only benefit from this effect once if targeted by multiple instances. 
●​
Gentleman’s Duel 
When you attack a creature, you may divert their focus entirely to you. They will only attack you that round unless another ally attacks 
them, breaking the attention. 
 
Passive: When you take this agenda, either choose or create an NPC, or with a player's permission choose their character. Doing something that 
would put them in direct risk grants -1D, and you must aid them during a severe attack if possible, regardless of your health. 
 
LAYABOUT 
Items: 
●​
Avoid responsibility at all costs. 
●​
Take on more work than you’re given. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Toke 
When you rest, you may spend 2 KP to add 1 to all of your or an ally’s rolls to relieve stress. 
●​
Passivity 
When drawn into an argument against your will, you can claim total neutrality, gaining +1D to talk down the situation. The moment you 
stop playing devil’s advocate for both sides, they converge against you. 
●​
Dear Future Me 
You may negate up to 6 stress and push it off until later. You take all negated stress at the end of the next rest, or at the end of the mission 
(whichever comes first). 
●​
Five More Minutes 
During a rest, you may choose to reroll up to 3 rest dice, keeping the higher result. If you do, you must remain asleep for a number of 
scenes after the rest equal to the number of dice rerolled minus 1 (minimum of a few minutes). 
●​
Gather Moss 
If you do not make any significant action rolls for an entire scene in which you are present, gain +1D on your next roll and it cannot be 
hard. 
 
 


 
AIRHEAD 
Items: 
●​
Don’t think too hard. 
●​
Really mull it over. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Cogwheels Turning 
When you fail a roll to analyze or investigate, you may take 1d3 stress to have the correct answer come to you in a vague way in the next 
scene. 
●​
Bluescreen 
When an effect would try to invade or take over your mind, you may Bluescreen, negating the attempt, but treating all rolls made to 
investigate, analyze, or perform complex thought hard until the next rest. 
●​
The Best Idea 
When you make a plan, you can declare it Foolproof. If all people involved play their part correctly, you must choose to make one 
important roll have two Risk Dice, turning it into an immediate success. On a 1 on either die, the plan suddenly goes spectacularly wrong 
at the last moment. 
●​
Slip the Mind 
1/mission if a piece of evidence you need to investigate would be destroyed or made inaccessible, you may declare “oh yeah, I forgot i 
had this” and pull out a piece of evidence from your pocket that replaces the need for it. You don’t remember where you found it. 
●​
Dunning Kruger 
You gain either +1D or a reduction in difficulty when speaking on things you have absolutely zero actual knowledge or understanding of. 
 
ARCHIVIST 
Items: 
●​
Keep track of it all. 
●​
Don’t sweat the small stuff. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Nanomanagement 
Whenever an exorcist follows your orders obediently, you may both take 1d2-1 sin to clear 1 stress. 
●​
Due Process 
You may forbid the execution of a sin until all trauma questions are answered, ticking back Pressure by the number of unanswered 
questions. This cannot reverse deaths or destruction, but may reduce ambient effects and the sin’s talisman. 
●​
Egg Basket 
At the start of the mission all allied exorcists may agree to give you all of the group’s KP, giving you KP equal to the total +2. Allied 
exorcists may spend your KP as their own with permission as long as they are within the same scene. 
●​
Checklist 
Gain a consumable “free set up” free action when one of the following happens: an exorcist sin overflows, pressure reaches 6, an exorcist 
suffers instant death or enters brink of death, mass civilian casualties, mass property destruction. You may only benefit from each item on 
the checklist triggering once per mission. 
●​
Chickenscratch  
You may easily write onto any surface, regardless of if it is normally receptive to ink. 1/rest gain +1D when utilizing information you have 
recorded and written down yourself. 
 
TOWER 
Items: 
●​
Keep up the act. 
●​
Let the walls down. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Calloused  
You can punch into solid surfaces such as metal, stone, and wood without injuring yourself with enough force to blow through walls. Your 
fists count as a service weapon you can upgrade with scrip. 
●​
Brick Wall 
Stress is now represented by attacks simply pinging off of your iron skin. Injuries are the only time you take actual physical damage, which 
can affect complications and narrative effects. Take -1 stress from the first attack you receive each scene. 
●​
Loom 
1/rest you may approach behind someone an ally is occupying without them noticing, regardless of your height or footsteps.  
●​
Poker Face 
Nothing can read your intentions or emotions via your facial expression or mannerisms alone. You almost always look pensive, apathetic, 
and/or bored. 
●​
Pedestal 
Gain +1D on actions when you have a steady, perched, birds eye view of the situation. 
 
 


 
WIZARD 
Items:  
●​
Retreat into technology. 
●​
Touch grass. Metaphorically. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Spiderweb 
1/rest while using a device such as a laptop or desk computer, you may use it to become aware of any other technological device within 
short range, including hidden cameras, bugged mics, and devices in secret compartments. Gain +1D the next time you or an ally utilizes 
this information. 
●​
Backend 
You can take 1d3 stress to access the deep code of any electronic device, bringing up deleted files, cleared browser history, and opening 
encrypted files.  
●​
Ectoplasm 
Whenever you use a blasphemy with a range of short or longer, you may originate it through a monitor from the source of the camera or 
vice versa as long as you can see it within short range. 
●​
Doomscroll 
1/mission during a rest you may take 1d3+1 sin to count your rest dice as if they had all rolled their maximum. 
●​
Profile 
If you know someone’s real full name and face, you can easily find a social media profile if they have one without rolling, as well as up to 
1d3-1 secret accounts if they have them. 
 
Passive: You start each mission with an actively monitored burner phone with limited internet capabilities. 
 
TOURIST 
Items: 
●​
Take the road less travelled. 
●​
Take the easy way out. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Compass Needle 
You have a perfect sense of polar direction, like a mental compass. You cannot get lost in wilderness environments by your own doing. 
●​
Back of My Hand 
1/mission when you and a target you are chasing are taking the same route, you may take a shortcut once during the chase and close half 
of the distance instantly (slashing half of any chase talisman if necessary). 
●​
Turn Left in Three Miles 
You start every hunt with a simple map or blueprint of the investigation area, including points where prominent shops, restaurants, hotels, 
and gas stations are located. Once per mission you may “find” a secret room on the map that isn’t visible in normal view. 
●​
Culture Shock 
1/rest when you fail a roll to socialize or connect with people of a different culture than yourself, you may have them take a form of pity on 
you and disregard it as foreigner ignorance, and you succeed to a minor degree. 
●​
Designated Driver 
You gain the ability to travel while resting by hijacking a car or casually travelling. Your allies may rest as normal while you navigate, but 
you roll 1 less rest dice.  
 
CUCKOO 
Items: 
●​
Stay above those lower than you. 
●​
Step off of the pedestal. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Glass House 
Gain +1D when interacting with the rich, the politically powerful, and the corporate elite. The working lower class inherently distrusts you. 
●​
Legacy 
You gain +2 scrip at the end of every successful hunt as long as you are not docked pay, and have not died or sin overflowed. 
●​
Silk Palanquin 
You gain the ability to rest whilst your fellow exorcists continue on. While resting, you must relax for 2 full scenes in a row where you are 
present uninterrupted. As long as you are not disturbed, you gain the full benefits of a rest without advancing pressure. 
●​
The Silver Platter 
You may spend 1 KP in addition to a consumable’s cost to take one less stress or sin from it, and heal one more stress or sin from it. 
●​
Whitelist 
Once per mission you may be granted entry to any restricted, prestigious, or invitation-only event or area as if you had been personally 
invited. You may choose one other ally to gain this effect. 
 
Passive: You have a Patron among the ranks of CAIN who is responsible for your special treatment. Displeasing them may have dire 
consequences. They may call you during the mission to voice displeasure if you act inappropriately. 
 
 
 


 
WHEEL 
Items: 
●​
Be the martyr you’re meant to be. 
●​
Fight back, god damn it. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Old Wounds 
1/mission when you take an injury, or take damage on the Brink of Death, you may choose to purposefully sacrifice a limb to negate any 
stress taken entirely. You lose this ability if you have no more limbs to sacrifice. 
●​
No!!! 
If an allied exorcist enters the Brink of Death or dies due to a risk roll from your action, you may immediately step in the way, immediately 
entering the Brink of Death in their place and restoring 1d3 stress to them. 
●​
Nihilism 
1/mission as a reaction to taking an injury or entering the Brink of Death, you may “give up” and immediately be treated as unconscious, 
losing all will to live. Any character that acts to save you may immediately gain 2xp at the cost of 2 stress. 
●​
Erosion 
Every time you answer a trauma question, you may instead target the sin’s traces, taking the stress the sin normally would and 
permanently reducing the traces maximum talisman size by 2 or all current and future instances of them. If reduced to 0, they die in a 
single hit, mundane or not. 
●​
Not Your Time 
1/rest you may choose one civilian or non-hostile NPC. While chosen, that NPC gains a 6-slash execution talisman and you may take any 
amount of stress in their place as long as you are within reach of them. 
 
DRAGON 
Items: 
●​
Revel in the blood. 
●​
Pursue peace. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Absolute Mess 
1/scene after attacking an enemy in a brutal fashion, you may cause a complication that makes the ground in a short distance around you 
slippery and hard to navigate for all non-allied beings. You regain a use of this ability if you brutalize an unarmed or incapacitated enemy. 
●​
White Knuckle 
Mundane humans can never cause you to lose your grip, escape your grapple, or otherwise knock things from your grasp as long as you 
are conscious. 
●​
Sheep’s Clothing 
1/mission you may force an enemy or hostile NPC to underestimate you, feinting some sort of defeat or surrender. You gain +1D in the 
next action taken to subvert or escape them. 
●​
Drink Deep 
You may consume the fresh blood of any being to immediately ascertain whether or not they are supernatural and/or affected by 
supernatural effects. This also dispels any illusion or control the source of blood has over you. 
●​
Pulse 
You are acutely aware of the position and vague health of every being within a short distance that is afraid of or hiding from you. 
 
Passive: While you possess any ability from this agenda, you are utterly incapable of caring for humans or considering the lives of anyone that is 
not an allied exorcist as worth anything. You may choose to abandon any abilities from this agenda when you move on to another, choosing 
abilities from the new one to replace every one lost. 
 
DESERTER 
Any exorcist that is lost to becoming an imago does not take the Departed agenda and instead takes this one. You gain this agenda automatically. 
●​
I thought I knew you better. 
Once a session, the lost character may force the admin to reroll any number of dice in a roll, including risk, fate, and severe attacks. If the 
net result was better than the original roll, this ability cannot be used again. If the net result was worse than the original roll, all exorcists 
gain 2 extra xp at the end of the hunt. 
 
A character can only take this agenda if they are irreversibly lost to Grace corruption. You get its ability for free. 
 
 
 
 


 
HALCYON 
Items: 
●​
Remain pure. 
●​
Let yourself desire. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Inner Peace 
While resting, if you gain additional resting die or lose resting die from any source, increase that amount by 1. 
●​
Cleanliness Before Godliness 
You are immune to complications related to intense emotion, filth, desire, and toxins. 
●​
White Noise 
1/rest you may completely turn away your mind from your body, dropping into an altered state of consciousness. While in this state, your 
senses’ or reflexes’ CAT increase by PSYCHE, but whichever option you do not choose becomes greatly reduced until the start of the 
next scene. 
●​
Ignorant Bliss 
1/mission you may completely suppress your grace for a scene. You become unable to gain sin, unable to use abilities that require sin or 
psyche bursts to activate, and unable to see or physically interact with grace or grace constructs. 
●​
Nèidān Shù 
When you would gain sin, gain 1 additional sin. When you would clear sin, instead clear half of your current sin. 
 
Passive: Whenever you show any intense emotion, you must either take 1d3 sin or lose the effect of and access to all Halcyon abilities you 
possess until the start of the next scene. When you lose access to any Halcyon ability, you gain +1D on the next action related to the strong 
emotion you felt. 
 
SUN 
Items: 
●​
Empathize with others. 
●​
Close yourself off. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Bleeding Heart 
You gain +1D on rolls to connect emotionally with a being if it would place you in definite and obvious danger. 
●​
Open Book 
If you are being genuine and emotional about telling the truth, rolling to convince a mundane human can never be hard or impossible. 
●​
Linchpin 
1/scene when you take stress, the next action an ally takes gains +1D or a reduction in difficulty. This ability refreshes early if you take an 
injury. 
●​
The Lost Child 
Humans and certain kinds of animalistic supernatural beings are instinctively protective of you, similar to a parental instinct. Gain +1D 
when taking advantage of this effect.  
●​
You’re Not Alone 
You may take 1d6 stress to roll an additional die and keep the highest roll when utilizing a trauma question. If you do, you can no longer 
partake in execution of the sin, and must vehemently defend them. 
 
SPECTATOR 
Items 
●​
Fade away 
●​
Make a name for yourself 
 
Abilities

John Smith 
You start every mission with a set of standard local civilian clothing. While in this outfit you are completely indistinguishable from a 
mundane civilian, even to supernatural means of inspection. 
●​
Wallflower 
Rolls to remain hidden while you are already hidden can never be hard. 
●​
Mediocrity 
1/scene you may forgo rolling a non-PSYCHE roll to flip a d2. On a failure, all dice count as 3s. On a success, all dice count as 4s. If this 
causes you to succeed on an action you do it in a bland and ineffective way. 
●​
Unassuming 
Once per mission per creature that receives reports of your actions, you may cause them to immediately doubt the validity of the reports 
upon seeing you for the first time. 
●​
Beige 
Literally nothing about you is exciting. No creature can have anything beyond a mild opinion of you, positive or negative, while you have 
this ability. 
 
 


 
WRENCH 
Items 
●​
Take it on the chin. 
●​
Take time to heal. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Bandaid 
You can never take mechanical stress from punches, kicks, or other hand-to-hand combat that is not supernatural in power as long as you 
do not react or fight back. You may still appear and feel normally injured. 
●​
Other Side Of The Glass 
You are diegetically aware of the sin cap and current sin levels of all allied exorcists. You may take any amount of sin in an ally’s place, 
which they do not have to agree to. If multiple characters with this ability use it at once, roll randomly to decide who takes it. 
●​
The Great Divide 
Supernatural beings will never consider you a threat until proven wrong, and mundane beings will always consider you a threat until 
proven wrong. 
●​
Clockwork Heart 
1/scene if you immediately obey a command of an authority or enforcement figure with a higher standing than yourself, they will drop all 
suspicion of you (and only you) regardless of circumstance. 
●​
I’m Sorry 
1/mission when invoking a trauma question to deal slashes to a sin’s talisman, you may choose to deal no damage and instead pacify the 
sin completely for the duration of a scene or until someone speaks, after which it flees until pressure increases. 
 
CLOUD 
Items 
●​
Let others in. 
●​
Close yourself off. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Rosebloom 
1/mission when you take an injury, you may have the injury appear horrifically mortal at the end of the scene (such as being shot or 
stabbed near your heart, or fully impaled) and immediately fall unconscious until the end of the next scene. All allies who witness your fall 
immediately regain 1 psyche burst, which can exceed their maximum. You recover with a gruesome scar at the end of the next rest. 
●​
Soft Gaze 
1/rest when faced with a violent or outraged individual, you may ask the Admin the true cause of their emotions as a one-word answer. 
You then gain +1D while attempting to reason with or calm the individual for the rest of the scene. 
●​
Killdeer 
You are always able to roll to save any number of children or young adults from danger. If you fail the roll, you may take 2d3 stress in 
addition to any risk roll to automatically succeed with 1 success. 
●​
Tourniquet 
You are always aware of how many people a person you are conversing with is close with, and roughly how close. If they have no close 
friends or family, you gain +1D on any social rolls towards them. If they are truly alone or isolated, this becomes +2D. 
●​
Nest 
You have a child either within or outside of CAIN, and a photo to commemorate them. By showing the photo or talking about them, most 
children will answer your questions honestly, after a few tries if they are hesitant, and in general trust you more than most people your age. 
 
SHRIKE 
Items 
●​
Put them in their place. 
●​
Show some introspection. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Blood In The Water 
You always know the direction of the nearest emotionally vulnerable human within short range, and can sense emotional breakdowns at 
long range. Gain +1D on social rolls if you are needlessly cruel. 
●​
Stepping Stones 
Mundane humans in more servant-like roles like butlers, hotel staff, and clerks will always believe you are in the right place. You may 
spend 1 scrip to automatically obtain a high-end hotel room, though only for one night per mission. 
●​
Diva Beam 
Whenever you spend Kit Points on a purely aesthetic item (such as lipstick, a hand mirror, a brush or comb, a wig), reduce the KP cost by 
1. You gain a +1D to intimidate, coerce, or demoralize humans whose sexuality includes your gender as long as you are clean and 
made-up. 
●​
Crab Bucket 
Whenever you discover disloyalty or plans to escape CAIN (either within your allies or a third party) you may report it to CAIN at the end of 
the mission for 5 additional scrip and a free voucher for 1 night in a comfortable bed. 
●​
The Tallest Poppy 
Whenever an ally fulfills an agenda item in a way that grants them attention or admiration, gain 1 sin. Whenever you do so, relieve 1d3-1 
sin. 
 
 


 
ROADKILL 
Items 
●​
Fuck the system. 
●​
Flaunt some privilege. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Diehard 
You gain +1D on actions regarding information about obscure/niche bands, brands, and pop culture figures. 
●​
Frying Bacon 
You deal +1 slashes against all forms of law enforcement or authority, and gain +1D on social rolls to decry, slander, or demoralize them. 
●​
Boombox Orthodoxy 
1/rest you may put on a set of headphones to enter The Zone for a scene, becoming completely deaf and blind to the outside world. For 
the duration, everything you do matches perfectly to a beat, and you can effectively perceive the world around you despite being blind and 
deaf, ignoring hazards related to vision and hearing. 
●​
Sandlot Native 
You are always familiar with the local slang and subversive culture of the investigation area. You can immediately establish moderate 
rapport with a prominent figure over the course of a scene, but they will immediately turn to hate you if you associate with law enforcement 
at all. 
●​
The Concrete Canvas 
You gain the ability to read and write hidden messages inside graffiti tags, and can create incredible graffiti in a fraction of the time it 
normally takes. You can choose to finish a tag without any risk of being spotted, but in exchange will always be spotted at the end of it. 
 
Passive: I Hate You, Let’s Die 
Once per mission you may declare a mundane human in a position of legal authority your “enemy”. Both you and your target deal +1 stress or 
slashes against each other, and gain +1D in rolls to slander, frame, or blackmail each other. 
 
HEEL 
Items 
●​
Be the bad guy you’re meant to be. 
●​
Be the good guy you wanna be. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Ragebait 
Diverting an enemy’s attention by provoking or enraging them is never hard, but is always risky. 
●​
Silver Tongue 
When you successfully enrage or provoke a mundane human, you may cause them to take 1 nonlethal slash on their talisman from the 
straining to contain their anger, which increases to 2 nonlethal slashes if they react violently. 
●​
They All Want Me 
1/rest when you meet an NPC for the first time, you may have them immediately despise you in exchange for asking the Admin “what 
makes them the most angry?” targeting the NPC. Gain +1D when you act on the answers for the rest of the scene. 
●​
Chickenshit 
Whenever you engage in any sort of official or formal fight (wrestling, boxing, street fights, honor duels) you may pay up to 3 scrip to roll 
an equal amount of dice. If at least one is a success, you may rig the fight in your favour in a nasty way without consequence; if you roll no 
successes the fight is rigged against you. 
●​
Pretty Little Princess 
Once per rest, pick one ally or human NPC. As long as you act awful and irritating, especially if you harass your target, mundane humans 
will always respect your target completely, instead anchoring all of their hate towards you. 
 
LOSER 
Items 
●​
Prove you’re the victim in everything. 
●​
Accept responsibility. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Keyboard Warrior 
1/mission as long as you have access to some form of anonymous online forum or an anonymous burner account, you may spread any 
form of viral rumor, no matter how outlandish, against one mundane NPC of your choice. Every time pressure increases afterwards, 10% 
of the investigation area begins to believe the rumour based on flimsy and circumstantial evidence. 
●​
Study The Blade 
Whenever you witness someone wielding what you consider to be an “exotic” weapon (a fancy foreign gun model, a foreign blade type, 
foreign martial arts), rolls to attempt to one-up them wielding the same item can never be hard. 
●​
Whinge 
No one will ever take you seriously as long as you pathetically complain about something; you gain +1D on attempts to mask the truth by 
engaging in reverse psychology. 
●​
Knight Of The Corkboard 
You gain +1D on any roll to unearth, remember, or discover cryptid stories and conspiracy theories, which increases to +2D if the 
conspiracy ostracizes a single individual. 
●​
Eligible Bachelor 
When you meet someone, you can ALWAYS find out whether they are actively dating or otherwise romantically involved with someone. 
1/rest you may, as a hard roll, attempt to discover anyone they are longing for or have a crush on without needing any form of evidence. 
 
 


 
ORPHAN 
Items 
●​
Desperately dig up the past. 
●​
Move on with your life. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Memorabilia 
Objects with an intense emotional connection or baggage surrounding them almost physically catch your attention, causing you to lock 
your eyes onto them and hesitate if you are moving. You gain no special insight into the nature of the connection, only its presence. 
●​
Cage And Key 
You have a single token from your backstory that you may gaze at to activate a single, consistent flashback. 1/mission, you may lend it to 
an ally as a good luck charm that allows them to fully reroll a single roll. 
●​
God Rays 
1/rest when the Admin or Player you chose to write your backstory deem it relevant to your backstory, they may force a longer Flashback 
targeting a single object in your line of sight. Afterwards, you may either gain perfect proficiency with the object, or incredibly specific 
knowledge about the object, down to things such as the screw type it uses or where a commonly loose panel is. 
●​
Neuroplasticity 
1/rest when you slash a hook relating to mental afflictions or complications, you may erase one slash of another hook. 
●​
Muscle Memory 
Whenever you end a rest, roll 1D, or 2D if you have taken an injury since the last rest. On a success, you have already been awake for 
several minutes. You may immediately summon an up to 2KP cost item from any kit for free; you have no idea where it came from, or why 
you know how to use it. 
 
Passive: Glass Cage 
You awoke in a CAIN custody dormitory with no recollection of your past life or self. With your permission, the Admin and one other Player of your 
choice may write a backstory that you remain unaware of. Make sure to have clear talks about boundaries and what elements you are/are not 
willing to engage with. Whenever you gain sin, you gain brief Flashbacks of a suppressed memory that grants -1D to any social rolls for the rest of 
the scene. 
 
LOTUS 
Items 
●​
Inflate your ego. 
●​
Show humility. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Ethereal Grace 
You take -1 stress from falling or tumbling, and you always land on your feet. 
●​
Unseelie 
You may roll to intimidate, coerce, or scare a being without making any noticeable actions as long as you are at or above half of your sin 
cap. If you have sin overflowed this mission, this roll cannot be hard. 
●​
Glitter 
While in any form of formalwear (such as a gown, suit, designer clothes, or couture fashion), most humans are immediately stunned silent 
and dazzled by your appearance. Clothing kit expansions cost 1 less scrip, to a minimum of 1 scrip. 
●​
Delicate Flower 
You take +1 stress from all sources of damage, but also regain +2 stress from all sources of healing.  
●​
Preen 
You may perfectly clean blood and other evidence of violence from your clothes over the course of a minute, and can perfectly clean 
evidence on the environment of a murder or death within the duration of a scene.

PUZZLE 
 
The pieces fall into place in the palm of your hand. 
 
FACT: The only known way to counteract Puzzle exorcists messing with CAIN dormitory infrastructure is Black Rock insulation. Allegations that the 
spun insulation is carcinogenic is unfounded, and has been extensively disproven by high-ranking TEMERITY pseudoscientists. 
 
Passive: Mosaic 
●​
Whenever you use a piece of evidence to answer a trauma question, you may graft it into yourself. For the duration of the hunt, the sin 
deals -1 stress to you, and you deal -1 slashes to the sin. You can have up to ½ CAT of these inlays at a time. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Splinter Until Rest, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
You gain the ability to draw ½ CAT area lines around and onto objects, cutting away at the space up to CAT feet deep seamlessly. 
You can generate up to four of these cuts, which last until the end of the scene.  
 
For the duration you may place any cut objects in the place of other objects to permanently swap their places and weld them 
together, or place them in their original place to restore them flawlessly. 
●​
Weld Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Target up to CAT objects within range, gaining the ability to phase them into and out of surfaces and each other in a seamless, 
unbreakable connection. You may attempt to weld creatures instead by rolling PSYCHE if they are unwilling, only spending a 
psyche burst on at least one success. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally 
takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Shuffle Instant, Transmute, Long Range 
○​
As an action, target up to CAT+1 short-distance sized bubbles within range. All bubbles and any creatures within them randomly 
swap places, determined by rolling dice or some other means of random assignment. You can take 1d3 stress to cause a bubble 
to swap to a specific location up to CAT times. After the swap is complete, the GM asks the following questions: 
■​
Were you inside a bubble? 
■​
Were all bubbles within short distance of each other? 
For each “no”, the GM rolls 1d6. On a 1, the GM may teleport you a long distance away once before the next rest. 
●​
Overlay Scene, Transmute, CAT+1 Range 
○​
As an action, target two CAT sized areas within range. You overlap their realities on top of each other haphazardly, and any 
creature inside counts as being in both places at once. Any creature can enter and exit from either of the two locations’ actual 
locations. This can target different planes of existence such as palaces, as long as you are aware of their location. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Tile Scene, Curse, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Target up to CAT creatures within range, touching your hand somewhere on their heads. If the target is unwilling, roll PSYCHE, 
only spending a psyche burst on a success. On a success, you completely shuffle the chronology and subjects of your target’s 
memories, turning their perception of past and present into a muddled mess. The target instantly loses any strong will they 
possess as they struggle to reconcile their scrambled thoughts, and any attempt at psychic connection to the target deals 3 stress 
or slashes to whoever attempts to do so. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
CONFINE 
 
Being a god is the loneliest achievement of them all. 
 
FACT: DO NOT INTERACT WITH CONFINE USERS UNDER ANY CIRCUMSTANCE BEYOND DEPLOYMENT. 
 
Passive: Singular 
●​
You become unable to benefit from Teamwork or Set Up actions. Whenever you use a power with the Curse or Charm tag, you gain a 
Token which you can expend to give yourself the benefit of Set Up. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Detached Scene, Curse, Short Range 
○​
As an action, select a target within range. For the duration, the target always seems to be too far away no matter how close other 
creatures get to them. For the duration they are always considered at CAT distance away from all other creatures regardless of 
how close they actually are physically, and never seem to reach a destination they try to go to, looping around or simply jumping 
back without noticing. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. 
●​
Dungeon Scene, Curse, Touch 
○​
As an action, trap yourself and a target you can touch in a dark, demiplanar maze inside of your own soul. You and the target may 
run and hide, seek each other out, or simply wait. If you and the target ever touch, you are immediately transported back to the 
real world in random distances and directions. 
●​
Solitary Scene, Curse, Short Range 
○​
As an action, choose a target within range. The target loses the ability to speak directly to others, make eye contact, make 
gestures, or directly communicate in any way or form; writing on surfaces is their only way of communication. For the duration, 
effects that would require communication, including telepathic connection, speech, or sight/eye contact automatically fail when 
used by or on the target. 
●​
Introspect Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
As an action, strip away the confidence from a target within range, rolling PSYCHE if it is a supernatural creature. They 
immediately consider every bond they have to be obsolete or fraudulent, somehow invalidated by some chilling revelation. They 
become incredibly receptive to any sort of validation or connection. Gain +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this 
effect. The effect wears off slowly over the next CAT days. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Island Scene, Transmute, Long 
○​
Roll PSYCHE and target a creature within range, completely isolating them from others. On a success, the target is now 
fundamentally unable to consider beings other than itself, and becomes almost entirely unable to notice other beings moving 
around it. For every success, the GM chooses one outgoing bond or control the target has with another being, and severs it. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
COMPRESS 
 
Perspective is an illusion. Yours is the only true vision. 
 
FACT: President ████ was killed via the rapid expansion of ████████ within his bloodstream when a secret service member spontaneously 
developed a Compress sinseed. ████ ██████ has been chosen as an appropriate coverup scapegoat. 
 
Passive: Pack 
●​
You gain the ability to modify the density of your own body at will for a scene. This can be as light as 1/4 your normal weight or as heavy 
as 4x your normal weight. 1/mission you may gain +1D when you take advantage of this effect, regaining a use of this bonus after you 
rest. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Palm Scene, Transmute, Extreme 
○​
You pluck a distant, visually small object from your view. Target an object up to CAT+1 size in any dimension and it appears in 
your hand the same size as it appears due to distance; the object and anything it contains is virtually indestructible and frozen in 
time while in this state. You can end this effect at will, depositing it in any space large enough and it appears an equal distance 
away from you. If it is not placed by the end of the scene, it returns to where you originally palmed it from. 
●​
Stretch Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
You haphazardly warp an object up to CAT in size in up to two axes, stretching its form. This can easily push or pin beings if the 
object is grounded or stabilized in some way. You can stretch an object up to ½CAT steps up or down in the chosen axis/axes. 
Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. The object returns to normal at the end of the 
scene, or whenever you choose to end the effect earlier. 
●​
Pop Instant, Curse, Short 
○​
You cause the internals of a creature or object in range to rapidly expand or shrink, resulting in massive hemorrhaging and 
damage. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE for damage and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ 
answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on success. 
■​
Can you make skin contact with your target? 
■​
Is your target made of flesh and blood? 
On at least one yes, one part of the target’s body inflates or collapses lopsidedly, making movement difficult and painful for them 
for the rest of the scene. If this kills the target or destroys the targeted object, it explodes in a shower of shrapnel, dealing 1d2-1 
slashes to two additional targets of your choice within a short distance of them. 
●​
Collapse Scene, Transmute, Curse, Long 
○​
You compress the spatial footprint of an object or creature up to CAT size you can see within range. If you targeted an unwilling 
creature as part of this action, roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. You completely flatten the 
target into a two-dimensional plane, though it is not weakened by being “thinner”. You may choose to end this effect at any time, 
but beings affected by this power may willingly revert to normal after 3 rounds. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to 
take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Lattice Instant, Transmute, Short 
○​
As an action, target an object or surface up to CAT+1 in size. You alter the object’s material density beyond what it normally 
should be. Choose one of the following options: 
■​
You reduce the density of the object drastically, rendering all of its materials ½ as dense as they normally would be. It 
becomes incredibly fragile and lightweight. Any amount of impact can damage it greatly. 
■​
You increase the density of the object immensely, doubling its effective weight and making it incredibly durable. Most 
mundane weaponry is completely ineffective in damaging it. 
 
 


 
MARCH 
 
You’re in charge. Act like it. 
 
FACT: March sinseed hosts are the most likely to develop superiority complexes out of any observed living thing. Constant degradation is 
recommended to prevent unwanted growth in confidence and independence. 
 
Passive: Bark 
●​
Your telekinetic abilities supplement the need for actual physical strength. 1/rest you can effortlessly lift loose objects you can touch up to 
PSYCHE in size for a scene but cannot take any other actions until you throw or drop them. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Command Scene, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
As an action, target objects with a combined size up to CAT and begin levitating them in the air. They can be moved at CAT0 
speed within range, and you can easily tear apart objects this way.  
 
This takes your full concentration; any other sustained power you casted ends when casting this power, and casting any other 
power or taking an injury immediately ends this power. You take -2D to checks made to perceive your surroundings or be aware of 
things around you while concentrating.  
 
Gain +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this. 
●​
Charge Scene, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
Imbue a massive amount of telekinetic force into an object or creature you can see within range, rolling PSYCHE if it is unwilling 
and only spending a PSYCHE burst on at least one success. Choose one of the following: 
■​
While the target is moving, anything that touches it experiences an impact PSYCHE+1 times greater than normal 
■​
While the target is moving, it bounces perfectly off of surfaces, does not experience air resistance or friction, and does not 
lose momentum. 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Pin Instant, Curse, Long Range  
○​
You grasp a creature you can see within range and crush them with psychic power. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE for 
damage and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each no answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on success. 
■​
Can your target hold themselves onto the ground or a wall? 
■​
Are you unable to anchor yourself? 
On at least one “no”, you can move them a short distance within range in any direction. If this kills a creature, they remain floating 
in the air until the scene ends. 
●​
Fire Scene, Self 
○​
You telekinetically lift CAT dozen small objects into the air, firing them like pin missiles in all directions. Roll PSYCHE, dealing 
slashes equal to the number of successes, always at least one slash. They easily pierce through most mundane armor, and deal 
+1 stress to talismans that represent large groups of enemies. Using this again before a rest costs 1d3 nonlethal stress. 
●​
Attention Scene, CAT Range 
○​
As an action, you force one type of weapon present in range (guns, blunt, blades) to orient around a targeted creature or object 
you can see within range, including ones lying on the ground or locked in place. Choose one of the following: 
■​
The weapons attempt to point directly towards the target and are pulled towards the target with an almost magnetic force. 
Actions made with the chosen weapon type that do not attack the target are hard. 
■​
The weapons attempt to point directly away from the target and are pushed away with an almost magnetic force. Attacks 
against the target with the chosen weapon type are always hard or deal half stress. The effect ends early if the target 
takes an injury. 
Mundane humans must roll a Force roll (0d for average human, 1d for a very strong human, 2d for trained military) to attempt to 
deal stress with the chosen weapon type in the first place. 
 
 


 
COBBLE 
 
Improvise, Adapt, Overcome, Annihilate. 
 
FACT: All Cobble users are under constant supervision to prevent unwanted theft of TEMERITY proprietary property. 
 
Passive: Poach 
●​
Any handheld object you are able to touch with your flesh, even just the tip of your finger, may be instantly transported into your 
possession where you choose (such as into a pouch, your mouth, under your clothes, or into your hand etc). You can take 1d3 stress to 
increase this to short range for the duration of one action as long as there is a space the object can fit through between you and the 
object. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Improv Scene, Transmute, Touch  
○​
As an action you can convert one object up to CAT size you can see within range into an entirely new basic object of your choice 
with the same rough size as the original. Roll PSYCHE, choosing modifications equal to the number of successes +1: 
■​
The form is complex, it can have fine details and shapes, including patterns. 
■​
The form is functional, it can have hinges, motors, and/or gears. 
■​
The form is durable, and can take a hit or two. 
■​
The form is CAT-1 sizes bigger or smaller than its original form. 
This object can be anything at all, like turning a stapler into a sword, turning a briefcase into a bowling ball, turning a car into a 
guitar, and vice versa. This change reverts at the end of the scene or if the object would be destroyed. Gain or grant +1D when 
you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Hoard Scene, Transmute, Long range 
○​
As an action, target an object or creature within range. All loose objects that are CAT sized or smaller within a long range of the 
target are drawn to it regardless of weight, with the strength of the attraction becoming stronger the closer they are to it. Once an 
object enters this field once, it is drawn to the target regardless of the distance. At the end of the scene, this effect ends and 
causes the accumulated sphere to collapse. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect.  
●​
Repossess Instant, Charm, Touch 
○​
As an action, you may touch and designate a loose object up to CAT in size. Until the end of the hunt, you may cause this object 
to instantaneously appear in your hand if it is small enough or adjacent to you otherwise, regardless of distance or plane. You may 
do this up to ½ CAT times, at which point this effect ends. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of 
this effect. 
●​
Snatch Instant, Transmute 
○​
1/scene you may cause any thievery caused by this blasphemy’s passive ability to go completely unnoticed by any non-allied 
creature. This includes taking objects that would otherwise be extremely noticeable if missing, such as a gun from someone’s 
hands, or a computer from someone’s desk as they are using it. The minds of creatures affected by this power make up a 
narrative to explain the absence of the object as if it was never there. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant 
+1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Title Scene, Self, Charm 
○​
Target an object or area up to CAT size within range, and roll PSYCHE. On at least one success, you may cause a number of 
creatures equal to CAT to wholeheartedly believe that you are the owner. As part of this power you may provide altered physical 
evidence to verify your ownership if necessary. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
 
If this power is used before the end of the next rest, you can only persuade a single creature this way. 
 
 


 
BREAK 
 
Violent irreverence to the natural order. 
 
FACT: CAIN has had to falsify materials sciences and chemical lifetime tests conducted by researchers to account for the rising abundance of 
Break users in the general populace degrading infrastructure. 
 
Passive: Antithesis 
●​
Surfaces directly touched by your bare skin decay at hundreds of millions of times the speed they normally do. This can destroy rope and 
paper, crack glass, rust and weaken metal, and blister or foul organic matter almost instantaneously. 
 
You start every mission with a pair of black synthetic rubber gloves that are immune to this effect. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Hiss Instant, short 
○​
Spray caustic bile that coats surfaces like a cancer. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE targeting a being or location within 
range and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘no’ answer. 
■​
Is the target unobstructed and in clear line of sight? 
■​
Is the target wearing plastic or rubber clothes? 
​
All objects and creatures in an up to CAT sized cone around your target are covered in the bile. It feels cold and viscous, and 
writhes like twitching skin. Mundane material is instantly dissolved into a foul-smelling goop. If an enemy does not use a reaction 
to clear the slime from themselves, they take 1 slash on their talisman for each turn they are covered. 
●​
Sacrilege Until rest, adjacent, transmute 
○​
Insult objects to cause them to collapse or fail. As an action, choose an object up to CAT size, and spew insulting energies 
towards it, conditioning it to hate humans. It will now go out of its way to fail, operate incorrectly, or actively terrorise any humans 
or exorcists that come near it, including you and any allies. Doors jam, bullets detonate in chambers, trees drop branches, 
concrete trips feet, and engines explode in thick, black smoke etc. ​
​
This effect lasts until the end of the next rest. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Degrade Scene, Self 
○​
As an action, release a chilling, noxious fume that chokes sight and air from a CAT sized area. Sensitive or brittle materials like 
film, thin glass, and exposed metal melt, shatter and rust upon contact. The gas instantly incapacitates non-graced humans that 
do not have breathing filters, and lasts for the duration or until you dismiss it as an action. ​
​
Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Contempt Scene, Transmute, Self 
○​
Your ability to break and destroy extends beyond the physical. You can now cut away at, shatter, or melt non-physical or otherwise 
irregular things such as light, air, water or electricity for the duration. The broken source of the target does not generate new 
material to replace the broken shards. (For example, shattering a chunk out of a stream of water cuts a hole the flow pretends 
doesn’t exist).​
​
Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Crumble Scene, self 
○​
Emit an acidic plague that eats away at health itself in a field around you. Everything in a CAT area around you begins rapidly and 
violently experiencing symptoms similar to rot and aging, as if sped up by a thousand times. Healthy non-graced humans under 
the ages of 35-40 affected in this way typically recover in 2-3 weeks with no aftereffects given proper medical care. Choose 
PSYCHE of the following effects, and you or any ally gains +1D the next time they take advantage of it. 
■​
Trees rot and collapse, plants wither and die, grass browns and shrivels. 
■​
Food spoils, water blooms with algae, and the air becomes thick with cough-inducing spores. 
■​
Exposed skin experiences rot and boils, muscles atrophy, hair grows grey and white, and minds become foggy. 
■​
The ground and walls crack and crumble, glass fogs and shatters, and wooden boards collapse and rot. 
■​
Social bonds wither and vanish, opinions sour, distrust blooms, and memories fade. 
 
Pure polyethylene plastics and synthetic latex are uniquely and entirely immune to Break powers. 
 
 


 
SHIMMER 
 
All that doesn’t glitter, you shall make gold. 
 
Fact: The most common form of death for Shimmer users is repeated crystallization of internal organs. 
 
Passive: Bling 
●​
You always seem to have some sort of cash on you. You can easily pay any expense equal to or less than a small meal at a fast food 
restaurant, via some unknown means. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Inclusion Scene, Transmute, CAT-1 Range 
○​
Target and foster the growth of deposits of minerals within mundane surfaces in a CAT-1 range. When you use this power, roll 
PSYCHE and target up to CAT creatures in the line of effect, only spending a psyche burst on a success. Crystal spines 
appropriate to the composition of the surface blast outwards, and you may “ride” or run along these crystals as they form to grant 
yourself up to CAT velocity. ​
​
Structures created this way crumble to mineral powder at the end of the scene. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts 
to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Bruting Scene, Charm, Touch 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst as part of a Setup action to trigger this power. The targeted ally’s attack now deals lethal damage 
to any mundane human as an atomically sharp monoedge encrusts their service weapon for the duration, easily cutting and 
piercing through thick body armor and metal plating like they were soft butter. ​
​
The affected service weapon deals an extra +PSYCHE slashes on all attacks. Against supernatural beings, this bonus decreases 
by 1 after an attack resolves, and once this bonus decreases to 0 the charm ends. 
●​
Kaleidoscope Scene, Transmute, Long 
○​
You rapidly shift a structure or material into a mutable crystalline matrix. While this power is active you may perform PSYCHE of 
the following as an action: 
■​
Open a hole up to ½CAT in size through a solid surface, up to ½CAT distance in depth. 
■​
Summon an obstacle or formation up to ½CAT in size within range, which must originate from a solid surface. 
You can have up to CAT alterations active at once, but can dismiss one alteration as a free action before or after you take an 
action.​
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Polish Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
As an action, touch a continuous surface up to CAT in area. For the duration, the surface becomes sparklingly smooth and 
polished, and has fundamentally zero light absorption of any kind. Any supernatural effect, physical or not, reflects off of it entirely, 
but mundane means can still crack and damage it. Allies can use this effect to bank shots that would otherwise require line of 
sight by bouncing it at an angle off of the surface, making the roll hard by choice.​
​
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Carat Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
As an action you may touch and convert an entire object or area, up to CAT, into a singular solid piece of mineral or metal from the 
following list: 
■​
Plain Gray Stone 
■​
Crystal Clear Glass 
■​
Solid Lead 
■​
Rough-Wrought Iron 
■​
Mirror-Polished Silver 
■​
Rich Gold 
This includes all of the parts the object is made of, including any fluids contained within it, panes of glass, ropes/fabrics, and any 
paint or similar adhesives stuck to it. This change reverts at the end of the scene, and any broken or cut away pieces act as 
normal.​
​
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
REGRESS 
 
The forests are older than bones. 
 
Fact: The 1995 ██████ were a direct result of unchecked Regress usage, totaling nearly ████ civilians being lost as they 
instantaneously ███████████████████ from lack of ██████. 
 
Passive: Return 
●​
Your footsteps do not disturb rubble or debris where you walk, such as gravel, twigs, leaves, puddles of water, or dust. Roll +1D on 
all rolls to sneak or avoid detection while within wild vegetation or wilderness landscapes. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Rebuke Instant, Curse, Extreme range 
○​
As an action, target a being within range you can see. This being gains a special 3-slash Resentment talisman and 
becomes a prime enemy of nature, and everything wild seeks its downfall. While they have this talisman, animals will 
become violent on sight, insects will swarm them, grass and brambles will trip and tangle their feet, and trees will attempt to 
fall on top of them. ​
​
As an action, if the target is within range you may slash the Resentment talisman and instantly mark one slash on their 
execution talisman as nature besieges them, which instantly kills most mundane creatures. Or if they are within short range 
you can slash the Resentment talisman during a reaction to reduce any stress they deal with that reaction by 1.  
●​
Recede Scene, Self, Transmute 
○​
While this power is active you are able to hide or dodge perfectly behind any object bigger than CAT0 regardless of 
silhouette, position, or size, and can move invisibly between such hiding spots, as long as they are within short range of 
each other and no one is looking directly at you, as an action. This power may easily affect the parameters of rolls, such as 
difficulty and risk. 
●​
Reclaim Instant, Transmute, Adjacent 
○​
Touch the ground and revert an up to CAT area to the way it was before the stench of human presence soiled it. Trees grow 
instantly to replace old, felled trees, the soil warps to reform paved hills, and human structures crumble and grow choked 
with vines as nature retakes it. After this effect finishes the area looks as if hundreds of years had passed, as if abandoned. 
Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Refute Scene, Curse 
○​
As an action, speak aloud an official law or widespread social norm that you know to be true. All humans within a CAT area 
of you begin to act antithetical to that social norm or law, including law enforcement and those whose professions relate to 
it. This can include things such as Speed Limits, No Shoplifting, Free Speech (or lack of), Beauty Standards, Social 
Etiquette, Sanitation Procedures, No Trespassing, etc etc etc. All humans retain memories of this event, and may become 
confused or disoriented when the effect ends.​
​
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Recall Instant, Curse, Long 
○​
As an action, cause the wilds to thicken and the earth to become rich and hungry. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE 
for damage and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on a 
success. 
■​
Is your target in substantial darkness or shadow? 
■​
Is your target standing in a wooded area, in grass, or on bare soil? 
​
If this would reduce a being’s talisman to 1 or less, mark one more slash which cannot be reduced in any way. Any being 
killed or incapacitated by this effect immediately erupts into a fully grown tree in a direction of your choice. This growth does 
not cause stress to other creatures, but can easily blow through decently thick concrete. 
 
 


 
ROIL 
 
The sky screams in fury. You are its weapon. 
 
FACT: Lightning does not strike twice unless it is feeling particularly spiteful. 
 
Passive: Bristle 
●​
You have the ability to shut down or permanently fry any minor electrical device you can see in short range, including cell phones, 
electronic locks, computers, cameras, and radios. You constantly give people shocks as if charged with static electricity. 
Abilities: 
●​
Chariot Self, Transmute, Scene 
○​
Your mundane speed increases to CAT+1, and your steps leave behind glowing-red trails and scorch marks. At high enough 
speeds this can allow you to bypass gravity and run along walls or ceilings. Failing a roll made with acrobatics or precise 
movement CAT times while this power is active causes you to tumble out of control and take 1d6 stress, ending the effect. This 
can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Flash Instant, Curse, Short range 
○​
As an action, emit a blinding, deafening flash of lightning and thunder. Any enemy that can see or hear within range is stunned 
and blasted back a ½ CAT distance, which does not cause stress. Rolls for the next round stop being risky, but as soon as any 
enemy stunned this way takes stress or slashes on its talisman, it re-enters the conflict scene and rolls are risky again. 
●​
Nerve Instant, Touch 
○​
You gain the ability to transmute into electricity, travelling along a wire or metal surface of your choice, up to CAT distance. The gm 
then asks the following questions, rolling for each “no”: 
■​
Was the conduit insulated, directional, and intended for transfer of electricity? 
■​
Was the conduit free from large amounts of other electricity? 
■​
Was the conduit uninterrupted, unharmed, and/or undamaged while you travelled? 
On at least one 1, you are incomplete when you emerge, reducing all of your physical skills by 1 until the next scene. On two or 
more 1s, this lasts until the next rest.  
●​
Crackle Instant, Short 
○​
You gain minor control over the flow of electricity in the area around you, allowing you to perform basic functions. Choose 
PSYCHE of the following effects: 
■​
Turn a certain number of electrical components on or off regardless of its power source. 
■​
Restore or disable power in a CAT area for a scene regardless of fuel or status. 
■​
Fry electrical components permanently within range. 
■​
Strongly magnetize metal objects with a combined CAT equal to yours regardless of if the metal is capable of magnetism. 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Charge Until Rest, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Choose a creature you can see within range. You strip away electrons from their atoms, building up a lethal negative charge in 
their body. Any time they touch a metallic surface or object before the effect ends you may trigger the curse to instantly roll 
PSYCHE for damage, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. These dice explode on a 6, but you take 1 stress 
each time they do, up to CAT explosions. Once triggered, this curse ends. 
 
 


 
MASK 
 
The truth is what you make it. Make it fun. 
 
FACT: Handlers are required to ingest Schedule 3 Anti-Amnestics prior to interaction with any form of Mask user. 
 
Passive: White Lie 
●​
1/mission when you fail a roll to deceive, distract, or mislead, you may take 1d3 sin to instead make it automatically successful and now 
believe it wholeheartedly, as if you weren't aware you were lying. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Doubt Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
As an action, choose one belief that a creature within range you can see has expressly stated or demonstrated to you, and 
choose one of the following: 
■​
Their belief switches from positive to negative, such as flipping their belief in a fact from being true to believing it is false, 
or their opinion on something being love turned to hatred. They believe this to have always been the case. 
■​
The previous, but they are actively aware of this shift but treat it as a "sudden realization" or revelation free of outside 
influence. 
At the end of the scene, roll PSYCHE. On at least two successes, this change in belief becomes permanent. This can easily affect 
the parameters of rolls, and grants +1D to you or an ally the next time you take advantage of it. 
●​
Charade Scene, Curse, Touch 
○​
As an action, you may choose one of the following: 
■​
Choose two targets. Hide behind the first one as if cowering or suffer a fake mishap, and the first target now believes the 
second target is hostile towards you or caused the mishap and will attempt to protect you like an ally or start a fight. 
■​
Choose two targets. The first target treats you as a close friend from the past and introduces you to the second target, 
whose emotions are manipulated to be amiable towards you. 
■​
Choose two targets. The first target becomes incredibly interested in the second target (either attracted, alarmed, or 
curious) and will wholeheartedly believe the first rumor you tell them that aligns with their newfound interest. 
Once the scene ends, the target retains the altered perception but becomes aware that it is unusual or unnatural in some way. 
Gain or grant +1D to you or an ally the next time you take advantage of it. 
●​
Facade Scene, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
As an action, designate an up to CAT area within range that humans instinctively avoid. Humans will always gloss over it as if it 
doesn't exist and are unable to look directly at it. They will naturally take paths to avoid crossing the space even if they don't 
realise they are doing so. 
 
Even if a human is aware of its existence and is trying to find it, it will result in them realizing they have absentmindedly walked
