past it, etc, etc. If you bring a human within the radius they are immune to its effect until pushed back outside the radius. 
●​
Corrupt ???, ???, ??? 
○​
This power does not require a psyche burst, but instead forces you to consume a piece of evidence that would normally be used 
to answer a Trauma Question. 1/mission you may destroy an aforementioned piece of evidence, rendering it unusable and 
rendering the trauma question unanswerable. But as a result, you may feed the sin host's soul itself a convincing lie and have the 
GM choose a domain they would like to get rid of. After they choose, the sin permanently loses that domain's effect including any 
afflictions, effects dealt to exorcists, or traces which demanifest immediately. You must replace it with another domain of your 
choice, which you gain +1D on rolls against for the duration of the hunt. 
●​
Scam Until Rest, Curse, Short Range 
○​
When you make a deal with a creature, you may spend a psyche burst anytime before the next rest to gain the following benefits: 
■​
The creature respects the deal over almost everything else aside from their wellbeing and core emotional ties. 
■​
You may "retroactively" alter one aspect of your offer for the deal, such as a monetary payment or details of a favor. The 
target believes this alteration to have always been the case. 
■​
Once the deal is "complete", the target becomes aware of their mind alteration at the start of the next scene.  
Gain or grant +1D to you or an ally the next time you take advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
COMPASSION 
 
A golden heart, beating bloody on your sleeve. 
 
FACT: The almost universal adoration of Compassion users within the ranks of CASTLE suggests constant monitoring to reduce harassment as 
much as possible, lest a riot evolve as a result of “avenging” them. 
 
Passive: Empathy 
●​
Mundane humans cannot start out with a negative attitude towards you, regardless of domains and other effects. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Beloved Until Rest, Charm, CAT Range 
○​
Designate a human you have seen within the last scene. For the duration, you are constantly aware of their health status, vague 
location as long as they are within range, and current mood. If they die or take stress, you take 1d3-1 stress from the worry, which 
cannot cause an injury. 
●​
Embrace Instant, Touch 
○​
As an action, target a creature you can touch. Instantly deal lethal damage without rolling to the target if they have ½ CAT or less 
slashes on their talisman, by hugging or embracing them gently. They may take a reaction to do whatever they wish including 
dealing stress to you before they die painlessly and peacefully. Take a stacking 1d3 stress for every time you use this ability in a 
mission beyond the first (2nd use deals 1d3, 3rd use deals 2d3, etc). 
●​
Complete Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Choose another willing exorcist within range. You both meld into a hybrid form, merging all of your powers and capabilities. 
Record your current health on a separate note, then add together your injury cap, psyche bursts, and sin cap, and increase your 
stress cap by CAT. You now count as one creature in initiative. In addition, choose one of the following effects: 
■​
You gain access to 1 more blasphemy power of your choice symbolic of your merging, which you can use once for free. 
■​
You gain access to 1 more agenda ability of your choice symbolic of your merging. 
■​
You may 1/scene do two actions at once while still triggering only 1 reaction. 
Keep track of all stress you take on a separate sheet. After the merge is over, split all stress, injuries, and sin taken evenly 
between the two of you and apply it to the health you had before the merge. If the split would be uneven, you take the higher 
amount. Leaving a merge imparts an immense sense of dysphoria. 
●​
Compatriot Scene, Charm, Long Range 
○​
Designate a creature within range you can see (which can include yourself), and roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at 
least on success. You gain the following effects: 
■​
Each treats the other as their closest, most cherished soulmate (romantic or platonic), and will favor them over even their 
fellow allies and other relationships. 
■​
If one of you takes an injury or dies, the other gains 1d3 sin (an npc gaining sin instead deals 1d3 nonlethal stress). Even 
after the effect ends one will grieve the other’s death immensely. 
Once this effect ends, both lose complete memory of each other as if they were complete strangers until you use this on them 
again, and are left with an immense sense of heartbreak. 
●​
Destiny Instant, Charm, Short 
○​
Target an ally within range. If willing, you become irrevocably bound by destiny. Both of you become constantly aware of the status 
of the other, and choose the following effects: 
■​
The first time after being bound one of you would suffer death/instant death or sin overflow, the other may take an injury or 
2d3 sin respectively instead to nullify the event. After which, this bond ends. 
■​
The first time after being bound one of you would be affected by a 1 on a risk die, including a severe attack, the other may 
take their place, taking CAT less stress and ignoring up to one hook or affliction gained. After which, this bond ends. 
 
 


 
HORDE 
 
We are many, we are one, we are all. 
 
FACT: Horde users are the most unusually unremarkable out of any blasphemy resonance profile. It is theorised this is because their abilities 
express themselves in ways that are not quantifiable by current scientific instruments. 
 
Passive: Recursion 
●​
Your consciousness is spreadly evenly between all allied exorcists and your “host body”. While within touch range of a willing exorcist you 
can see, you may swap bodies with them. This swaps all of your mental attributes (Interfacing, Authority, Investigation, Negotiation, 
Connection) and blasphemy powers, but retains physical attributes (Force, Conditioning, Coordination, Covert, Surveillance), current 
stress and sin cap, injuries, and afflictions/hooks. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Hotswap Scene, Curse, Long Range 
○​
As an action, target up to ½ CAT+1 creatures. For unwilling creatures, roll PSYCHE (this roll is hard if targeting a trace). On at 
least one success, or if all targets are willing, you may swap the minds of all targets however you wish.  
■​
If a mundane human is swapped with an exorcist or trace, they spontaneously develop a weak CAT1 sinseed to 
accommodate their new graced mind, which will kill them by the end of the next rest. 
 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Chain Scene, Charm, Long Range 
○​
As an action, choose up to 2 willing allies (which can include yourself). For the duration, they are fundamentally the same 
consciousness, sharing all memories, knowledge and sensations across their two bodies. Whenever they roll, use the highest skill 
between the two of them. This merge lasts until the duration ends, one of them leaves the range, or one of them suffers instant 
death, death, or sin overflow. 
 
When this effect ends, each target chooses 6-CAT core memories. These memories are muddled and/or swapped as they both 
leave a part of themselves behind, each taking 1d2 sin. 
●​
Uplink Until Rest, Charm, Touch 
○​
As an action, you may cement yourself into a body and grant an ally the benefit of your passive instead. While altered this way, 
they gain +1D on one skill of their choice for the duration as their knowledge mixes with your residual influence. 
●​
Ghosting Scene, Transmute, Self, Extreme Range 
○​
For the duration, you gain the ability to take 1 stress and emerge from the body of any allied exorcist within range, stepping out 
from behind them. This leaves behind your previous body as you generate a new one, which then discards the new form for a 
newer one if you warp again etc. At the end of the scene, all discarded bodies melt into a swirling multicolored sludge. 
●​
Facet Unique 
○​
1/mission when you suffer sin overflow, you may splinter off that fragment of your consciousness, suppressing it and negating the 
overflow entirely. Gain the Bubbling hook, clearing all sin. When the hook fills, you take half of your maximum sin immediately and 
your personality is replaced by another, representative of the fragment you sacrificed to contain the overflow. This change is 
semi-permanent and requires counseling for the cost of 1 scrip to dredge up the sunken personality, but all fragments remain. 
 
 


 
WEAVE 
 
Razor-sharp, wire-thin, blood-red. 
 
FACT: Weave users are ██% more unlikely to form meaningful interpersonal relationships than any other blasphemy. 
 
Passive: Needle 
●​
You gain the ability to produce an effectively infinite amount of strong, sticky, psychic thread that can hold roughly CATx100 lbs of weight. 
You may spend a burst to be able to eject it at a high velocity for a scene. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Noose Scene, Curse, Long 
○​
Thin, invisible wires of pure force extend from your fingertips, tangling targets with a combined CAT equal to yours within range, 
up to CAT+1 number of targets. As a part of this action roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On a 
success, all targets within range are immediately restrained, glued in position as the wires dig into their skin and lock their bones 
in place. All movement for all targets becomes impossible. In addition, choose a number of the following effects equal to the 
number of successes rolled: 
■​
The targets cannot make any sort of noise or reaction as the wires gag them or sew their mouth shut. 
■​
The targets each take 1D3 stress or slashes on their talisman as the wires bite into their skin. 
■​
The targets are cocooned and dragged a short distance in any direction of your choice, including suspension in midair. 
​
This may easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Tether Scene, Curse, Self, CAT range. 
○​
Target two creatures you can see within range, which can include yourself. While this effect is active, a string of pure psionic 
energy tethers the targets together, making it impossible for either target to move farther than up to CAT distance away from each 
other. If an effect would cause them to be unavoidably pushed or pulled apart, both targets experience force equal to their own 
weight in the direction of the tether. This effect cannot cause stress (via sudden stops, impacts, or pulling someone through a 
small space etc). You may choose to end this effect early at any time.​
​
Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Net Scene, Long 
○​
As an action, send out a thick, up to CAT area sized matted net of wires to catch up to CAT targets within range. The target’s 
speed immediately drops to 0m/s without any form of stress, and they are stuck to the net like a flytrap. Depending on where you 
cast the net, they may be suspended in midair. You may retract the net to pull any target caught in the net to yourself as an action 
as long as you are within range.​
​
If the target is unwilling, they may take 1d2 slashes on their talisman at the end of the round to instantly escape the net.  
●​
Choke Instant, Curse, Transmute 
○​
As an action, target a creature or object up to CAT in size and wrap a net of atomically sharp razor wires around it, slashing it to 
bits.  
■​
If the target was an object, the next time you or an ally touches it it collapses into any number of slices in a direction of 
your choice: horizontal, vertical, or diagonal, down to a centimeter in thickness. It re-assembles itself at the end of the 
scene. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
■​
If the target was a creature, roll PSYCHE for damage and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘no’ 
answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on at least one success. 
●​
Can your target see you? 
●​
Is your target wearing any form of thick armor? 
If this effect would kill or incapacitate a creature, you can choose to either wrap them up harmlessly in a cocoon of force, 
rendering them imobile and incapacitated until the next rest, or slice them to ribbons. 
●​
Tripwire Instant, CAT area, Scene 
○​
You stretch a thin, fragile, and sensitive psychic web of wires across an up to CAT sized area you can see. You are alerted to the 
presence, identity (type of being, whether it is graced or not, basic age/physical info), and point of entry/exit of any corporeal 
creature that enters or leaves the area. The effect fades early if you or an ally casts another blasphemy power in its vicinity. 
 
 


 
CRYPTID 
 
The boogeyman isn’t just a bedtime story. 
 
FACT: CAIN’s counterinformation department is composed almost entirely of former Cryptid users. 
 
Passive: Glimpse 
●​
Once per mission, you may instantaneously appear inside a large shadow within close distance of a being you have seen at least 
once, so long as they are in darkness and are not within a palace or other plane.  
 
Abilities: 
●​
Rumour CAT Scenes, Curse 
○​
For the duration, nobody believes observations or evidence of your existence or presence unless they see you with their own eyes 
when it happens. Beings respond to first hand reports of your existence with extreme doubt and mockery, and will not believe 
things such as recording or photos. This effect ends early if you rest. 
●​
Grin Instant, Curse, Long range 
○​
As an action, roll PSYCHE and choose a creature you can see within range, only spending a psyche burst on at least one 
success. On at least one success the target locks eyes with you and its muscles seize solid as primordial fear floods its system. 
You can hold this stare for a number of minutes equal to twice the amount of successes. While frozen their vision is filled with 
nothing but a warped and horrifying version of your face. Once the effect ends, all memories of the time immediately before and 
after the effect are lost to the horrifying memory of the visage. 
●​
Chimaera Scene, Transmute, Self 
○​
As an action, target an animal or corpse you can see within short range, mutilating it instantly, and noting it down on a special 
Chimaera list. You can forgo choosing a target when using this ability and instead choose up to CAT creatures from your 
Chimaera list. You morph CAT corresponding parts of your body into parts of the chosen creatures, such as turning your neck into 
shark gills, mouth into alligator jaws, arm into the claws of a sin’s trace, or legs into grasshopper limbs. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next ½ CAT times you or an ally takes advantage of this 
effect. 
●​
Impostor Instant 
○​
Once per hunt, you may appear anywhere in any scene within the hunt, replacing any NPC. You shed the skin of their 
appearance, revealing yourself covered in a thin layer of mucus. The real NPC will either be found bound and gagged within a 
cocoon a long distance away, or burst into the room immediately after, depending on the Handler’s discretion. 
●​
Hackles Self, Transmute 
○​
This ability does NOT use a psyche burst. Once per scene you may spend a point of pathos to cause up to CAT humans you can 
see within range to experience a deep, chilling dread. They will immediately seek out the nearest source of shelter or bright light 
they can find, and will mistake any sound they cannot clearly see the source of for the presence of a creature or monster. 
 
As part of choosing this power, you gain the following passive effect: 
■​
Whenever you kill a creature whose last emotion was terror, gain 1 pathos. 
 
 


 
SHAMBLE 
 
Things have learned to walk, that ought be dead. 
 
FACT: Do not, under any circumstance, allow a Shamble user to make skin contact. For detailed warnings and further incident reports, see 
███████████████ 
 
Passive: Restless 
●​
You become entirely unable to suffer instant death unless you are on the brink of death, and must always choose to Defy Fate rather than 
die. While you are on the brink of death, all attacks against you deal -½ CAT stress, but all rolls are hard. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Raise CAT Minutes, Curse 
○​
Once per mission, you may fully raise a single human or exorcist from the dead for up to CAT minutes. They are fully aware of the 
nature of their existence, and will act accordingly. If used on a mundane human, you may reduce yourself to the brink of death to 
instead infect them with your own curse, turning them into another Shamble user with a CAT 1 lower than your own. 
●​
Blaspheme Scene, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
You suffuse a vast area of land with undeath, tainting life with rot and halting growth. You gain CAT-sectioned Hallowed clock, 
which you may slash to perform two of the following actions (you can select the same option multiple times): 
■​
Cause a corpse you can see in short range to get up or teleport and perform a single, simple action, such as grabbing 
someone’s leg or throwing someone. If there is no corpse within range, you may summon an incomplete one. 
■​
Cause phantom-like spirits to emerge and harass a target of your choice with chilling touch and haunting laughter. 
■​
Lock, unlock, close and open doors and windows, flicker or turn lights on or off, summon the sound of footsteps or 
thumping from anywhere in range. 
Once the duration ends, the undeath leeches back into your body, dealing stress equal to the amount of slashes in the clock. This 
can easily affect the parameter of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Maggots Scene, Curse, Touch 
○​
Mark a target for death, and answer the following questions: 
■​
Are they severely injured? 
■​
Are you surrounded by rampant death of some kind? 
For every “yes”, roll +1D and take 1 stress. The target takes slashes on their talisman equal to the number of successes, and if 
they die within the duration they cannot be revived or resist death by any means as their soul is withdrawn into the abyss. You 
may transfer your rotted brain into this new corpse during a rest, letting you replace one of your blasphemy powers with another of 
your choice for the rest of the hunt. 
●​
Screech Instant, Curse, CAT Range 
○​
You emit a deafening cursed shriek, rattling the souls of any creature within range. Any human not wearing complete ear 
protection is immediately rendered insensate and unconscious as they freeze and begin to experience vivid hallucinations of 
horrors. Protected humans and sin traces instead take nonlethal stress equal to CAT-(CAT of your distance from them). Any 
creature with a sense of self preservation will avoid the area where the shriek originated as if occupied by a lethal hazard. This 
can easily affect the parameter of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Scatter Self, Short Range 
○​
Your skin opens up, releasing a swarm of decomposers and carrion-scavenging creatures with a talisman equal to your CAT+2. 
Your skin falls limp and empty as you take full control of the swarm as a hive mind, and you may re-enter your skin as an action as 
long as you can touch it. If the swarm is killed while you are outside of your skin, your allies may bring your skin with them during a 
rest, wherein a new swarm of pests fills it up and you regain control of your body, taking one injury. Gain or grant +1D the next 
time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
GARDEN 
Blood and bones, slumbering in the tilled meat. Grow, and grow wildly. 
 
FACT: While the flesh of Garden users demonstrates remarkable resilience and regeneration, the lack of a soul in cloned samples leads to rapid 
deterioration. 
 
Passive: Till 
●​
Whenever you use a Garden power you may take the Overgrowth hook to target another being, targeting their body rather than your own, 
rolling PSYCHE and only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. If there are no successes, both you and the target take 1D3 
stress and/or slashes on your talismans. Otherwise, they cast the power and you control how. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Sow Until Rest, Transmute, Short 
○​
As an action, your body splits open and envelops objects with a combined size equal to your CAT, up to CAT items, within range. 
While enveloped, this object is stored in a compressed space inside your flesh, and you may cause parts of it to extrude from, 
move around on, or retract from your skin at any time as an action. You may target one singular unwilling creature of CAT0 instead 
of multiple objects, or a creature with a CAT equal to or less than yours by taking the Overgrowth hook. 
 
Willing guests count as CAT0 objects. Welcome. 
●​
Trim Scene, Transmute, Self, CAT+1 Range 
○​
You can detach up to CAT parts of your body, at any size, and manipulate them from a distance. You can merge detached parts 
into one “construct” if you wish, merging their talismans together. You receive all sensory effects from the detached part as normal, 
including pain. You can reattach a part at any time, taking stress equal to any number of slashes it took on its talisman while 
detached, but if a part is destroyed while disconnected you take the phantom stress listed in its statblock. 
■​
Seed, talisman 2, 1D3 phantom stress 
●​
Eye, hand or a small lump of flesh. Can climb walls and move at a speed equal to CAT1. 
■​
Sprout, talisman 4, 1D6 phantom stress 
●​
Leg, or arm, or patches of skin. Can interact with larger objects and carry up to CAT x 5 lbs. 
■​
Stalk, talisman 6, 2D6 phantom stress 
●​
Torso, waist, or head. If one half is destroyed while disconnected, you return to the other half, which returns to the 
normal stress/injury tracking and automatically takes 2d6 stress which cannot be reduced in any way.  
●​
Pleach Instant, Transmute, Self 
○​
This ability only works if you are able to make full contact with your palm, and if you use the Overgrowth hook to affect another 
being you must make contact with your full palm. You may warp your body to an extent based on your CAT, including all of the 
options below it: 
■​
1-2: Warp, snap, extrude, or compact limbs, disfigure features, manipulate skin and hair. 
■​
3-4: Nonlethally warp and reassemble your flesh and bones, such as forcing yourself into the shape of a spider but 
keeping limbs and faces intact, or compressing into a flesh orb. 
■​
5: Manipulate, Add, and Remove flesh and bone in any way you see fit, up to CAT in size and down to the size of a 
marble. Achieve perfection. 
If this attack targets an unwilling creature, roll PSYCHE for damage. If this kills the target, you automatically gain access to the 5 
effect. All effects revert to normal extremely painfully during the next rest, or after you dismiss them as an action. 
●​
Weed Scene, Self, CAT+1 Area 
○​
As an action, you cause the materials of your body to undergo explosive growth, carpeting an up to CAT+1 area in your flesh and 
bones to attack a creature within range. You may drastically alter the layout of the area by forming bridges and walls, and breaking 
holes through thick surfaces. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE for damage and answer the following question, gaining +1D 
for a ‘yes’ answer. 
■​
Are you in agonizing pain right now? 
■​
Do you disregard your own personal safety? 
​
If any answer is yes, or you use this power more than once before a rest, take the Overgrowth hook. 
●​
Branch Instant, Transmute, Self 
○​
Whenever you would lose a limb, lose your head, or suffer catastrophic injury, you may spend a psyche burst to intentionally 
sacrifice the injured part, reducing the triggering stress by 2 and regrowing the lost flesh over the course of the next round. 
Outside of conflict regenerating body parts does not cost a psyche burst but may take up to an hour depending on how large the 
portion lost was. While regenerating your head or brain you are considered unconscious. 
 
Overgrowth: 
Your body can only stretch so far. If this hook fills up, your cells undergo cancerous duplication, and you immediately take 1d6 stress. If this 
causes an injury, roll a d4: 
●​
A facial feature completely sloughs off of your face, such as your jaw, your eyes, or half of your skin. Until the end of the next rest, all of 
your rolls to engage with mundane humans receive -2D6.  
●​
A limb withers and crumbles away, rendering use of it impossible. Until the end of the next rest, all of your physical rolls using the lost limb 
receive -2D6. 
●​
Your organs warp and bloat, causing immense pain. Until the end of the next rest, all of your mental rolls receive -2D6, and complex 
thought becomes incredibly difficult to focus on. 
●​
Your bones crack and fuse, turning your joints and muscles into ossified tumors. Until the end of the next rest, all of your rolls made to 
move in any capacity receive -2D6 as your manual strength and dexterity withers. 
 
 


 
GLUTTON 
 
The sweet, succulent savor of oblivion is intoxicating. 
 
FACT: The food plan reductions are nearly singlehandedly implemented due to the vast drain Glutton users have on cafeteria resources. 
 
Passive: Devour 
●​
You possess an additional large mouth spanning a limb or your torso, which may be opened 1/rest for a scene to use as a mundane 
service weapon. Whenever you use a consumable item, you may duplicate its effects to one other person as long as they let you consume 
their blood, dealing 1d3 stress to them. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Liquor Scene, Curse, Short Range 
○​
As an action, designate a type of consumable thing you can see within range. For the duration, all humans in a long distance 
around you will flock to that type of item as if it was incredibly addictive and expensive, squabbling over it and searching for it. If 
you hold one of the selected things, it will be treated as the most alluring.​
​
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Feast Scene, Transmute, Self 
○​
This power does not consume a psyche burst. By taking the Starving hook you may gain the ability to chew and consume literally 
any mundane material for a scene with no harm as your stomach hardens and your stomach acid intensifies to melt even 
ceramics and steel. You become entirely immune to diseases, toxins, sharp needles or blades, and other such hazards that you 
eat. You may at any time store and regurgitate one item within your stomach without digesting it until the next rest. 
●​
Maw Scene, Transmute, Self 
○​
For the rest of the scene, your jaws become a short range mundane weapon that can easily bite through steel and concrete and 
deal lethal damage. Once, as an action, you may take the Starving hook and take a bite out of reality itself, rolling PSYCHE and 
punching a clean hole up to CAT in size into any physical material. Enemies caught in the bite have a limb of limbs of your choice 
severed off cleanly if they are dropped below half their talisman maximum, or were already. If this kills a creature, heal 1 stress. 
●​
Digest Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
After you kill a creature, you may expend a psyche burst and take the Starving hook to consume their corpse, erasing the memory 
of their existence from all beings within the investigation area other than yourself and your allies. This process is irreversible. 
Affected creatures will fill in gaps in their memory with fake stories to placate their doubts. 
 
1/mission if this targets a creature that is the answer to a trauma question, you may roll PSYCHE to damage the sin (even if it is 
not present) and permanently reduce its talisman by the number rolled. 
 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Cuisine During Rest, Transmute 
○​
After a battle, you may prepare the corpse of a supernatural creature within range into a lavish meal, and present it to your allies 
during a rest. Describe what you cook, and how you cook it. 
 
Anyone who partakes in the feast takes the Starving hook as they hunger for more of the unnaturally delicious meal, but regains 
1d3 extra stress and psyche bursts during that rest, which can put them beyond their maximum. 
 
Starving Hook: 
When this hook fills, the primal desire to consume overtakes you. You must either immediately attempt to attack and consume the next non-allied 
creature you can see within the scene, or take 1d6 stress. The target can be either a recently deceased corpse or a living creature. You may flip 
1d2 as part of this effect; on a success the creature is just an animal. 
●​
If you were the one that killed the target, you become immune to the starving hook until the end of the next rest. 
●​
If the target is a sin or trace, clear 1 sin. 
●​
If the target is a mundane creature, clear 1 stress. 
 
 


 
TWIRL 
 
Stretched thin, like skin over too many bones. 
 
FACT: Overexertion of Spiral powers invariably leads to arthritis and increased risk of dislocation. 
 
Passive: Contort 
●​
You take -1 stress from effects that would bend, twist, or stretch you, and all of your joints have 360 degrees of flexibility, including your 
neck. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Plié Scene, Transmute, Self 
○​
Your body becomes incredibly malleable, reducing to a pliable gel. You gain the ability to squeeze into spaces as small as 3 
inches in thickness or diameter, and gain +1D to checks made to grapple, wrap around, or restrain a target you can touch. You 
can take 1d3 stress to increase this dissolution into a watery liquid, becoming able to separate and scatter as long as you regather 
yourself at the end of the duration. 
●​
Pointe  Instant, Long Range 
○​
You wind up a limb into a thick coil, then explode outwards in a burst of force. Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one 
success. On a success, You can target any creature in a straight line from you within range, and your strike can break through the 
following materials in a straight line based on your CAT: 
■​
1: Wood, thin walls, a window. 
■​
2-3: Thick walls, thin metal, a foot or two of solid stone. 
■​
4-5: Thick metal walls, bulkheads, several feet of concrete. 
■​
6: Any non-paranatural material. 
You can choose to stop at any target in the line and drag it directly to you instead of continuing farther, as long as it is CAT size or 
lower. 
●​
Arabesque Instant, Transmute, Touch 
○​
When you would suffer damage from an extreme impact, falling, or other means of blunt impact with the environment, you may 
spend a psyche burst to reduce the stress taken by PSYCHE and travel a ½ CAT line of your choice as you bounce. 
●​
Barre Instant, Short Range 
○​
You wind your body into a loaded spring, and unleash a blow against a target within range. As part of this action, roll PSYCHE for 
damage, only spending a burst on at least one success, then choose one of the following effects: 
■​
Trebuchet: You spike a target into the ground, making movement difficult until ½ CAT rounds pass to free themselves.  
■​
Ballista: You launch a target into the air, launching them a CAT distance in any direction away from you (which can 
potentially cause stress if thrown into hazardous things). They are dazed and deal -1 stress for the next round on all their 
attacks. 
●​
Assemblé Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
The body of a creature or a CAT sized area within range becomes incredibly stretchy, able to stretch up to ½ CAT distance easily 
and up to ½ CAT in size by flattening or inflating itself. It becomes incredibly bouncy while this effect is active, and it takes -2 
stress or slashes on its talisman from all blunt or crushing sources of damage. If it is a creature, it can stretch farther, but all rolls 
made while stretched beyond ½ CAT are hard by default. 
 
 


 
LADDER 
 
To whom it may concern: ████████████████████ ████████████ ████████████████ ███ ███████ ████ █████████ 
██████████████ ███████████████████████ ████████████. 
 
FACT: Ladder users are not a part of CAIN admin, despite any credentials they show you. 
 
Passive: Manager 
●​
1/rest when an ally uses a blasphemy power, you may have it originate from any ally you can see (including yourself) as long as they are 
within long range. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Forward Instant, Charm 
○​
When you are the target of an order or accusation, you may effortlessly shift the blame or responsibility to someone else in one of 
the following occupations, as long as you have seen them at least once: 
■​
Food Service 
■​
Customer Service 
■​
Retail 
The person who issued the order or accusation will now seek out and accost the newly appointed victim. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time your or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Micromanage Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
You may issue a concise order to a target within range. If the target is a willing ally, they gain +1D when obeying your command 
but -1D on any other action, and if completed do so with a remarkable degree of quality or efficiency. If the target is an enemy, roll 
PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. For the next ½ CAT rounds the target must waste a number of its 
reactions per round equal to the amount of successes rolled, attempting to pursue your command. 
●​
Buzzword Scene, Charm 
○​
You may place exceptional importance on a particular word or phrase, imbuing it with a supernatural ability to persuade. Humans 
who hear it will treat it as some sort of secret code word or clever phrasing, and will react accordingly.  
 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. If used on someone of a high corporate standing, 
instead of +1D add one success to the final result. 
●​
Investment Unique, Whole Mission 
○​
As an action, target a willing exorcist you can touch (including yourself) and they may choose one of the following: 
■​
Slash an injury and take 1d6 stress. 
■​
Spend 2 psyche bursts (Including the one you used to cast this ability, if used on yourself). 
 
The target gains a special Investment clock with 4 sections. Every time tension increases, they may roll a number of dice equal to 
PSYCHE. On at least one success, they fill a section of the Investment clock. When they fill the Investment clock completely, they 
immediately choose one of the following based on their original choice: 
■​
Erase two injuries and regain 1d6 stress, which can go above their maximum stress as “temporary” shield stress. 
■​
Regain 3 psyche bursts, which can go above their maximum. 
 
The target can either trigger this bonus immediately, or as a free action on their turn anytime before the end of the hunt. 
 
If the target suffers instant death or sin overflow before the Investment clock is filled, they lose it and you cannot use this power 
again this hunt. 
●​
Currency Instant, Transmute, Long Range 
○​
As an action, target up to CAT creatures within range. The next PSYCHE times they go to reach for something in a holster, pocket, 
bag, or sling, you may have them find it has vanished and in its place is its equivalent value in CAIN Approved Scriptocurrency, 
determined by the GM. Until the end of the scene, you may spend an equivalent amount of scrip to have it materialize in your 
hand, otherwise it simply vanishes. 
 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
BEND 
Bend the rules until they break you. 
 
FACT: CAIN has not, as of yet, verified the source or reason for Bend powers. While grace, its unique signature suggests it is a refined, 
pre-digested form of power, like honey is to pollen. 
 
Passive: By the way, I know everything. 
●​
You become vaguely aware of the fourth wall, and may once per scene force a scene jump, treating a number of required actions as 
automatically successful equal to CAT as exposition is skipped and increasing tension by 1. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Soliloquy Instant, Charm, Self 
○​
Tear down the fourth wall and address the Admin or a player other than yourself directly. Time completely freezes, including your 
body but not your mind, and you may choose one of the following options: 
■​
You may bargain with the Admin directly in-character, and may make and take more drastic deals such as sacrificing your 
lifespan or mass destruction as a payment if they agree. After the pact is made you forget everything, including the deal 
and even activating this power in the first place. 
■​
You may ask the Admin or player a single yes or no question of your choosing. The reality of the encounter strains your 
limited mind. After the encounter ends you forget everything, including activating this power in the first place, and are left 
with the answer to your question as an intrusive thought in your mind.  
No matter which choice you pick, take the Comeuppance hook. 
●​
Narration Scene, Charm, Short 
○​
Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on a success. You may cause your Admin’s narration to become diegetic for a scene, 
granting you the ability to act on any information you hear constantly even if you are not present in the scene. Any being that 
psychically touches your mind also gains the ability to comprehend the narration. 
 
On a failure, take the Comeuppance hook. 
●​
Plagiarist ???, ???, ??? 
○​
At the start of every hunt, replace this power with a random power from a blasphemy of your choice or choose a power an ally 
possesses to copy for yourself until the end of the hunt. You may use one power you gain this way once per rest without 
expending a psyche burst by treating it as CAT-1. You may also use the power by expending a psyche burst normally or by taking 
the Comeuppance hook 1/scene. If the core function of the power relies on a passive, you are considered to have the required 
passive for the duration of that power.​
​
This blasphemy power can be taken any number of times. 
●​
Exposition Instant, Transmute, Extreme 
○​
At the start of a scene, you may change, remove, move, or add a single set piece of a scene within range (the weather, the time, a 
telephone booth, a shack, a policewoman’s car, a public park, an office, a set of stairs, the words on a sign, etc). You cannot 
create or remove anything larger than CAT+1, and any buildings and objects generated are nonfunctional, bland, and brutalist in 
aesthetic. This change lasts until the end of the scene, at which point it changes back to its intended design. NPCs will accept the 
change as if it was always there, but become noticeably disoriented, wary, or distressed if the difference is too severe. Take the 
Comeuppance hook. 
​
This power may easily affect the parameters of rolls, such as difficulty and risk. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts 
to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Serendipity Instant, Charm/Curse, CAT+1 range 
○​
Seamlessly swap places with a willing exorcist or any npc “retroactively” in a scene within CAT+1 distance, with all NPCs 
regarding it as if it didn’t happen. NPCs will become noticeably concerned or confused if the difference is severe. If this power is 
used again before a rest you must either take the Comeuppance hook, or take 1D3 stress and gain extreme vertigo. You were 
always there.  
 
Comeuppance: 
Many of your powers give you this hook. If this hook fills up, the stretched bowstring of the narrative snaps back into place around your neck. You 
lose use of all Grace abilities until the next scene. Additionally, roll 1D6: 
1.​ Once before the end of the mission, the Admin may replace one sentence you speak with a sentence of their choosing. 
2.​ Once before the end of the mission, the Admin may cause an important open passageway in front of you to suddenly warp and become 
nonexistent. 
3.​ Once before the end of the mission, the Admin may choose to outright lie to you on a successful roll to collect information. Attempts by 
other exorcists to acquire the same information do not reveal the lie. 
4.​ Once before the end of the mission, the Admin may choose to sour a single NPC’s opinion of you permanently by giving them a false 
memory. 
5.​ Once before the end of the mission, the Admin may choose to add a risk die to a roll which you have just succeeded. The consequences 
can only affect you and cannot trigger a severe attack. 
6.​ Once before the end of the mission, the Admin may cause you to trip on a nonexistent step and scuff your forehead on the pavement even 
if there isn’t any around, halting your movement entirely for an action. 
 
 


 
SHUTTER 
Picture perfect. 
 
FACT: The most common form of decommissioning for Shutter users is accidentally trapping themselves within their own captures. It is unknown if 
destroying the capture kills the exorcist inside. 
 
Passive: Captures 
●​
Many of your abilities use this term. Whenever anything is stored in or creates a Capture, it generates a CAT-slash talisman object in one 
of the following mediums of your choice, which cannot be changed: 
■​
Drawing/painting. Creates a single piece. 
■​
Writing/script. Creates a single page. 
■​
Photography. Creates a single polaroid photo. 
■​
Sculpture/carving. Creates a palm-sized figure or relief. 
​
You can have up to CAT+1 Captures active at any given time, and may choose which one to destroy when making a new one if they are 
full. ​
​
A Capture lasts until a rest or its talisman is filled, at which point it either ejects its contents adjacent to the object (in the case of Portrait) 
or its contents fade away (in the case of Aperture or a featureless Capture). You can extend the lifetime of the capture by rolling a number 
of dice equal to your PSYCHE during a rest individually for each Capture you want to sustain. On at least one success, that capture is 
sustained. (You may spend 1 KP per Capture that has endured to the end of a mission to preserve the results for TEMERITY analysis 
purposes. You will be rewarded for your efforts. Gain 1 scrip per Capture, or 3 if it is of a supernatural entity.) 
​
You may use an action to take a Capture of an object, environment, or being with a size up to CAT with no other unique abilities other than 
existing as a Capture. This does not expend a psyche burst but does take up a Capture slot. These Captures cannot be cashed in for 
scrip. 
 
Abilities:  
●​
Portrait Instant, Curse, Short 
○​
Roll PSYCHE and target a creature or object with a CAT equal to or less than yours +1, only spending a psyche burst on a 
success. You may increase the CAT limit to yours +2 by making the PSYCHE roll hard. On at least one success, the target 
becomes trapped in a Special Capture, even if it was connected to a previous structure such as a wall or the ground. ​
​
Once per mission, a supernatural being within a Capture can use a reaction on a risk roll of 1 to deal 1d2 slashes to the talisman. 
Sins slash all of the talisman’s maximum size with this reaction, regardless of CAT. This attempt refreshes when pressure 
increases.  
●​
Aperture Instant, Transmute 
○​
Take a snapshot of a static 3D moment within a CAT area around you, storing it in a Special Capture. Everything within a ½ CAT 
distance is in crystal clear detail, but anything beyond that is blurry and things like writing and faces become illegible. You or any 
ally can mark one slash on its talisman and mentally enter this capture as an action to move around inside and inspect it, but your 
body freezes and remains insensate until you leave. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this 
effect. 
●​
Exposure Scene, Long 
○​
Create an illusory duplicate of anything you can see within range up to CAT size, or an object or creature you have a Capture of 
by marking one slash on the Capture’s talisman. The effect can be animated as you wish, and is perfectly lifelike and visually 
convincing but cannot produce any other sensory effect such as sound, touch, or smell. Mundane humans can see whatever it 
portrays regardless of supernatural nature. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Develop Scene, Transmute 
○​
As an action, destroy a Capture of an environment or other object of the same medium as your Captures. You may instantly 
overlap the scene it contains with reality in a CAT area bubble around you. Everything within it is interactable and follows the rules 
of whatever fiction it contains. This can include weather, light, and other effects, but cannot create non-plant living beings of any 
kind such as humans, animals, or graced beings. 
​
The real world underneath the overlap is effectively erased, aside from living beings which are deposited safely within the overlap 
in a space nearest to their original position. The same happens when the overlap ends as reality returns to its previous shape. Any 
objects that originate from the overlap blink out of existence instantly when it ends. 
●​
Postcard Instant, Curse, Unique 
○​
1/mission you may destroy a Capture of an environment you possess and instantly warp up to CAT willing targets to the exact 
viewpoint the capture was taken at. As a part of this action, roll PSYCHE, and choose an effect equal to the amount of successes: 
■​
(0) The transition went wrong somehow. All targets immediately take 1D3 stress or slashes on their talisman, and one 
feature of their face or body is visually distorted, rendering using it impossible until the end of the scene. 
■​
(1) The cut is slightly choppy. All targets become physically ill from disorientation, and all rolls are hard by default until the 
end of the scene. 
■​
(2) The scene smears together, but all targets hold their ground against the flow. Their next roll is hard by default. 
■​
(3+) The transition is seamless, and all targets suffer no ill effects. 
 
 


 
VOID 
 
The stars are as innumerable as your sins. Snuff them out. 
 
FACT: Void users are to be stored in windowless sub-level containment dormitories until deployment. Under no circumstance should they be able 
to view the sky via image or directly at any time. Constant supervision is advised. 
 
Passive: Empty 
●​
You may cause the corpse of any creature you kill to instantaneously collapse inwards and cease existing before any on-death effects 
trigger.  
 
1/rest you may take 1d3 stress to increase this to a target you can touch with PSYCHE slashes left on their talisman. Every time you do 
so, you must sacrifice a finger. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Paradox Scene, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
You speak an unutterable word, the definition of █̷͚̤̹̖͔̖̒̃̀́̃̿̎͛̂̕͝█̷̧̡̨̡͈̮̱͚̝͚̲̥̺̱̭̈́̿̿̀̀̄̓͒̐̄̉̒͋̕͝͝█̷̧͉͙͕̟̤̠̟̺̟͚̰̹̽̊̎̄̄͑̏͌͜█̷͖͕̻̹̝̀̄█̵̢̖͔̼͈̜̀̍̓́̈́̃̐̆͒͋̋̽͋͒█̸̫͍̝̪̘͔̼̘̣͗̒͂͂̿̄́̚͜█̸̡̥̮̺̳̔̈̃́̒. Any human within a CAT area that can hear you is struck by either a 
(flip a d2) nihilistic epiphany or a divine acceptance of absurdity. They will begin to act violently in accordance to their new belief, 
separating into divisive factions if you affect enough people.   
 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Silence Until Rest, Transmute, Investigation Area 
○​
You may instantly snap the light out of all stars in the sky within the investigation area, including the sun and light pollution, turning 
the sky into a solid, singular color. All wind and natural noises cease, including animal calls. 
 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Mass Instant, ½ CAT size 
○​
Gravity in a ½ CAT sized bubble around you is affected in one of the following ways: 
■​
Completely ceases to exist, causing objects and yourself to float as if in deep space. Creatures other than yourself and 
allies treat movement as nearly impossible unless they have exhaustive propulsion. 
■​
Increases drastically, causing items to be flattened, pulled towards, and/or arc around each other. Creatures other than 
yourself and allies treat movement as nearly impossible unless they have incredible strength. 
●​
Aurora Scene, Transmute, Curse 
○​
Designate an up to CAT sized area anywhere within the investigation area. For the duration, any mundane human that looks 
directly at the sky over that area is awestruck, losing track of their surroundings, dropping what they are carrying, and seeking out 
the closest spot to the phenomenon. 
■​
Any time you or an ally looks directly at the phenomenon, even through photos or representations, roll a number of dice 
equal to PSYCHE. On a failure, the exorcist becomes entranced as well, slipping away from the group and losing 1 KP as 
they stumble a long distance before regaining their senses.  
●​
Wound Scene, Summon, Short Range 
○​
You summon a skull-sized silent sphere of force that moves in an unending straight line. All loose objects within CAT range are 
drawn towards it, even on the other side of walls, and CAT size or lower objects are sucked in and annihilated entirely. Any being 
that touches it instantly takes PSYCHE slashes before being thrown out a safe distance away, and beings killed by this are sucked 
in and annihilated. The sphere disappears early if it deals 3 instances of damage to a creature, or travels a CAT distance. This 
sphere can harm both you and allies. 
 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
PITCH 
 
Bright eyes in the long, dark hallway. 
 
FACT: Overuse of Pitch abilities results in noticeable drops in skin pigmentation and increased sensitivity to UV light. 
 
Passive: Passenger 
●​
Your cast shadow is sentient, able to act on its own, communicate via gestures, and ignore light sources to appear on surfaces it normally 
could not. While within full darkness it blankets you like a second skin, granting +1D to all rolls made to sneak, assassinate, or hide. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Blot Instant, Self 
○​
All light in an up to CAT sized sphere around you ceases to exist, including supernatural sources of light. You can control the 
rough shape of this effect, such as constraining it to within a building, but cannot change the shape, location, or size once it is 
summoned. You and up to CAT allies can see clearly as if everything was lit by a blacklight. Gain or grant +1D when you or any 
ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. This effect fades at the end of the scene or when you dismiss it as an action. 
●​
Stain Until Rest, Curse, Long range 
○​
Choose a creature you can see within range, bathing them in psychic residue. For the duration, the target feels a heightened 
sense of paranoia and fear, and gains a minor phobia of darkness if they did not have it before. You and any ally can see their trail 
of movement and any place their body touched within the past CAT×4 hours as if they were soaked in black ink. This can easily 
affect the parameters of a roll. The traces slowly start fading away once they are CAT or more hours old. 
●​
Tar Scene, Transmute, CAT area 
○​
As an action, designate an up to CAT area surface that is covered in darkness or shadow. For the duration, the surface sucks 
everything loose into the shadow. Mundane objects not fixed or ingrained into the surface are sucked in and destroyed this way, 
and any enemy that does not stay within a source of bright light takes 1d2 slashes on their talisman for each round they remain in 
the shadow. As an action, you can take 1D3-1 stress to dive into the shadow and emerge anywhere within a long distance on the 
same surface.​
​
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D when you or any ally next acts to take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Snip Instant, Curse, Long range 
○​
You sever the link between a creature and its shadow within range, rolling PSYCHE and only spending a psyche burst on at least 
one success. The target takes stress or slashes on its talisman equal to the number of successes, and cannot heal that amount 
while the shadow is severed. If this kills the target, the Shadow takes -1 to its talisman as it withers.​
​
The Shadow becomes a sentient being with a talisman equal to the number of successes, and its attitude depends on the nature 
of the target it was separated from. It can move along any connected continuous surface and interact with the shadows of objects 
to puppet them in a poltergeist-like manner. The shadow can still harm and attack exorcists or enemies via their shadows. 
​
Shadow (???): Execution talisman determined during creation.​
Inflict stress: Any melee attack the former host had access to. (1/2) 2 stress, (3/4) 1 stress, (5+) 0 stress. 
Or: You may re-sew a Shadow to its former host as an action.​
​
If the shadow is destroyed while severed, the target permanently loses their shadow and their maximum stress or talisman size 
permanently reduces. If an exorcist or supernatural being would suffer this effect, this only lasts until the next rest, as their shadow 
regenerates. 
●​
Project Scene, Transmute, Self 
○​
For the duration, you flatten yourself into your own shadow. You are effectively infinitely flat, can slither through any width of space 
that is not vacuum sealed, and your mundane speed counts as 1 CAT higher than normal. If the surface you are plastered onto is 
destroyed, or if your shadow is suddenly illuminated with bright light, you take 2d6 stress and are crudely ejected with abrasions 
covering most of your body. 
 
 


 
FLICKER 
 
The brightest light casts the darkest shadows. 
 
FACT: Flicker users often experience “burnout syndrome” when overusing their powers. Psychoactive and hallucinogenic substances are shown to 
greatly repair their performance. 
 
Passive: Lumen 
●​
You may cause any object you hold in your hand to emit light up to the strength of a medium lightbulb. As an action, you may cause this 
light to spark into an equivalent of a flash grenade, disabling your use of this ability until the next rest and gaining or granting  +1D the next 
time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Bathe Scene, Curse, Long Range 
○​
You intensify any light source within range of yourself into a blinding floodlight no matter the size of the source. The brighter the 
light, the hotter it feels against skin. This automatically deals PSYCHE slashes to all beings in extreme range that are in LOS of 
the source of the light, once. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Gleam Scene, Long Distance 
○​
You can project your effective point of view anywhere you can see within range, and can look around and move around as if you 
were standing there. As long as you do not go beyond a short distance from yourself, and look for no more than CATx5 seconds 
per scene, this power does not cost a Psyche burst. ​
​
If you spent a psyche burst on this power, gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect.  
●​
Beacon Scene, Extreme Distance 
○​
As an action, name a creature you either have met, or have extensive knowledge of. A beacon of light immediately erupts from 
their body, shining through solid matter within range. Only you and allies can see this beacon, but you may, as an action, make the 
beacon visible to all humans for the duration of a single round, ending the effect early. The effect also ends if you come within a 
short distance of the target, as it becomes too blinding to see at that close of a range.​
​
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect.  
●​
Corona Until Rest, Investigation Area 
○​
As an action, cause the sun or moon to seemingly increase in brightness, bathing the area in blinding light. For the duration, all 
creatures in the investigation area treat sight as incredibly difficult unless they possess some form of eye protection. Shadows 
seemingly don’t exist unless they are inside, as the light seems to come from all directions of the pure white sky. 
 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Blink Instant, CAT Range 
○​
You momentarily surpass the speed of light, sending a ½ CAT sized or smaller object or willing creature within short range 
rocketing towards another target within CAT range. Roll PSYCHE, dealing stress or slashes equal to the number of successes 
rolled, and double if the target is an object. If this kills or destroys the target, it is evaporated, leaving behind a shadow that is 
burned into any surface behind it. The accelerated object or creature takes no damage from this movement. 
 
 


 
ODYSSEY 
 
The path to hell is paved with the best of intentions. 
 
FACT: All known Odyssey users are to be fitted with Schedule 1 blackstone spatial anchor anklets while within CAIN facilities. For more 
information, see █████████████. 
 
Passive: Road 
●​
You are immune to terrain and complications related to terrain that would slow or hinder your movement, such as mud, earthquakes, 
rubble, ice, and the like. You are unable to sit down, lie down, or recline in any way. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Sanpo Instant, Self 
○​
You may form minute barriers near your feet, essentially temporary steps, in midair. You may take up to CAT+1 steps this way per 
scene for free, and you may spend a psyche burst to allow you to step infinitely in any direction until the scene ends. These steps 
do not disappear until you leave them, which can allow you to “stand” in midair. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Stroll Scene, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
You and up to CAT willing creatures gain the ability to effortlessly walk on walls, ceilings, and other continuous surfaces as if your 
gravity is always pointing directly down relative to you. This effect also extends to any object the targets touch until they are farther 
than an arms length from them. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes 
advantage of this effect. 
●​
Meander Instant, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature you can see within range. If the target is unwilling, roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one 
success. Choose one of the following: 
■​
Warp the target back to their position during their previous turn, essentially undoing their movement. 
■​
Warp the target forward to their position during their next turn, essentially spending their next movement ahead of time.  
You may trigger this effect PSYCHE times this scene, and may choose a different option each time. 
●​
Journey Until Rest, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Target a creature you can see within range. If the target is unwilling, roll PSYCHE. On at least one success, you may designate 
one location in the investigation area you have been to at least once. The target is now compelled to treat reaching that 
destination as their greatest priority, and will travel there on foot as fast as possible. If the target is an allied exorcist or NPC, they 
gain a boost of speed that allows them to reach their destination in an illogically fast manner, halving the travel time. Gain or grant 
+1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Passage Scene, Transmute, Touch, Extreme Range 
○​
As an action, touch a door, trapdoor, or other such entrance. Then target another door within range and ask the following 
questions, rolling 1d6 for each “no” answer: 
■​
Is the second door the same rough size and shape as the first? 
■​
Have you walked through the second door at least once before? 
■​
Is the second door within long range of the first? 
If any dice is a 1, the first door is instead connected to a random door within a long distance of the second target, which you are 
not aware of until you open it. The door is now linked to the new location, causing any being that enters from one side of the first 
door to exit into the other side of the second door, acting as a two-faced portal.  
 
 


 
BYGONE 
 
The world marches ever onwards. Stand your ground. 
 
FACT: If a user of Bygone is mourning you, do not attempt to correct them unless you are absolutely sure you are real. 
 
Passive: Nostalgia 
●​
You may speak to a hallucinatory psychic duplicate of anyone you care about at will. This Friend can appear to interact with their 
environment from your perspective, but nothing actually happens from the perspective of others. Your Friend only acts and remembers 
their life as you know them, and cannot bring up information you are not at least subconsciously aware of. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Reminisce Scene, Curse, Long 
○​
Target the person your Friend is currently replicating. Your Friend and the target effectively swap places for the duration. While in 
this state, the target cannot be harmed and follows the same hallucinatory restrictions as your Friend normally would, and your 
Friend gains a physical body. 
 
If your Friend dies while in this state, the target immediately pops back into the real world after they vanish and your Friend can no 
longer replicate the target. 
●​
Pedestal Scene, Charm 
○​
Target a person you have met before, raising their memory to greater heights. Anyone who hears you speak about them this way 
will automatically treat them as an important or significant figure in their lives as well. This can easily affect the parameters of a 
roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
Roll PSYCHE. On a success, the target of this power may choose to act as the caster’s idealized version of themselves, 
potentially altering their personality for the duration. If they do so, they immediately gain 1 xp. Each exorcist may only gain this xp 
once per hunt. 
●​
Funeral Until Rest, Curse, Extreme 
○​
Target a person within range that you have met before. They are now considered dead. All beings (including fellow exorcists) that 
are aware of their existence know that they are dead, though they cannot remember when they learned this or what the nature of 
the death was. All effects that would be aware of living things (such as a heartbeat sensor or aura reading) do not detect them. 
While in this state, they can be glimpsed up to CAT+1 times by a being who knows them before this effect ends. 
 
Roll PSYCHE. On a success, the Admin may choose a number of beings whose memories were altered by this power equal to the 
number of successes rolled. The chosen beings do not regain their normal memories, and fully believe that the target returned 
from true death. 
●​
Homage Instant, Summon, Self 
○​
Remember a deceased person you had bonded with. Your Friend may use this power to cast a blasphemy power they had as if it 
was your own. If they had no blasphemy powers, you may instead use one that is symbolic of them, which cannot be changed. 
While the power is active, your Friend becomes visible to all other beings. 
●​
Legacy Instant, Summon, Curse 
○​
Remember a deceased person you had bonded with. This can include both NPCs and deceased exorcists. During the next scene, 
a functionally perfect replica of that person appears nearby your party, with full memories and a vague sense of being lost but a 
complete story on how they arrived there. They only have 5+CAT stress cap and 1 injury cap, but otherwise have the same 
abilities as they normally would. They do not remember their death. If they ever die, they do not show it, but the next time no 
one is looking and someone turns around to address them, they are gone. Otherwise, they vanish right before extraction. 
 
Each time you remember a person beyond their first return, they return with 2 less stress cap and a distinct facet of their self is 
changed from before. If they die, it counts as another usage. Once their stress cap reaches 0, you are unable to target them again 
or remember what they were like, and can no longer duplicate them with your Friend. 
 
 


 
AUTOMATA 
 
Mechanical hands are the ruler of everything. 
 
FACT: Automata is the only currently known example of a blasphemy being developed around a distinctly modern human concept. The cultural 
impact of the industrial revolution and “posthumanism” on humanity has been marked for further investigation. 
 
Passive: Emulation 
●​
You are more like a machine than a person. As long as either your brain or heart is moderately intact, a fellow exorcist may harvest it from 
your corpse and return it upon extraction, reviving you. Every time you are revived this way, permanently replace one of your bolded 
agenda items that is not “follow orders” with “follow orders”, and lose a critical emotional memory. If all of your current bolded agenda 
items are “follow orders”, lose two critical memories instead.  
 
Abilities: 
●​
Improve Instant, Transmute, Touch 
○​
This power does not consume a psyche burst. You may always spend a few seconds to convert handheld amounts of any material 
into a refined, packaged, or otherwise processed form of itself, such as turning loose liquids into properly bottled portions, raw 
chemicals into processed and packaged compounds, or wood into freshly bound paper. This removes any form of impurity, 
damage, toxin, or other contaminant from the material, but also makes it visually and materially bland and boring. 
 
You may spend a psyche burst to increase the amount of converted material to a CAT sized volume and make the transmutation 
instant. 
●​
Purpose Instant, Transmute, Self 
○​
This power does not consume a psyche burst. At the start of each mission, choose CAT non-weapon mundane kit items you 
possess in your available kit. You may mechanically reconfigure any part of your body into a limitless version of the chosen item at 
will by spending a few seconds to focus, without spending the requisite kit point cost. This item is fused to your body, and 
becomes useless if removed from you.  
 
You may spend a psyche burst to similarly manifest any mundane service weapon without paying its associated kit point cost for a 
scene. 
●​
Streamline Scene, Curse, Touch  
○​
Target an up to CAT sized group of mundane creatures within range, rolling PSYCHE if they are unwilling. On at least one 
success, you reduce their thought patterns into simplified, repetitive versions, removing their ability to think or consciously act 
beyond a given task. While affected this way, the target will robotically perform its task with perfect accuracy in an idealized way, 
even if the task would not make sense in the real world (such as a streamlined cashier selling impossible items if given the correct 
amount of money, or a driver escorting you across town after a short black-screen scene transition). 
●​
Optimize Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Target a willing creature within range. The target no longer needs to breathe, eat, sleep, or perform biological processes, and 
becomes immune to effects that would affect its breathing or hunger, or force it to sleep. The target also completely loses the 
ability to feel or recognize human emotions and empathy, reducing all social skills by -2D. 
 
Roll PSYCHE. For each success, you may extend this power’s duration by another scene, but you also extend the target’s inability 
to feel or recognize human emotions and empathy until the next rest. 
●​
Truth Scene, Charm, Touch 
○​
You may tap into the collective spirit of the local area, calling upon the machinery and tools within an extreme distance around 
you. You immediately learn the presence of any fully automated systems within range, as well as how heavy the machinery they 
control is. You may also learn one additional thing of your choice: 
■​
Whether there is any heavy machinery present, and their purpose or group. 
■​
The presence of vehicles, and their purpose or group. 
■​
The presence of tools and implements of industry, and their purpose or group.. 
 
 


 
REGINA 
All the light touches is yours. 
 
Fact: Regina is a restricted blasphemy as per HIGH ADMIN Declaration 2.19.B8. Unsanctioned usage of Regina powers will result in immediate 
termination if proper credentials are not provided. 
 
Passive: Domina 
●​
Once per rest you may roll PSYCHE to give a human that can hear you a direct order. On a success, you exert your will on reality. If the 
target chooses to disobey, they immediately take PSYCHE+1 slashes on their execution talisman. The target is aware of the 
consequences of disobedience immediately. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Supplico Instant, Curse, CAT Range 
○​
Target a creature within range, rolling PSYCHE. On at least one success, the target is struck by impossible amounts of gravity, 
cracking the earth around it and forcing it to supplicate before you. The target treats moving its position physically as impossible 
until a number of turns have passed equal to twice the number of successes rolled. 
●​
Elevare Scene, Curse, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Target a willing or mundane creature within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On a 
success, you wipe their mind and bend them completely to your will, forcing them to obey any simple one-word order you give 
them. While they are in this state, their mind cannot be accessed, they cannot speak or think, and they cannot truly die until the 
end of the control, even if mortally wounded. 
●​
Casurus Instant, Long Range 
○​
Target a creature within range, ordering them to “fall”. If the target’s execution talisman has slashes remaining equal to 
PSYCHE+1 or less, it immediately dies, crumpling to the ground like a puppet snipped from its strings. If the target’s remaining 
execution talisman is larger, roll PSYCHE for damage, which has no physical damage and instead injures the target’s soul. 
●​
Voluntas Mission, Curse, Short 
○​
Target a creature with connections or important living figures in their life that you are aware of, which you mark as a hostage. You 
may issue the target a single order, rolling PSYCHE. On a success, if the target disobeys you may immediately deal damage to 
the hostage as if wielding your service weapon. The target becomes aware of this threat even if you do not tell them.  
 
You may only have one hostage at any time. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or 
an ally takes advantage of this effect.  
●​
Heres Curse, Unique 
○​
Whenever a mundane creature causes you to take an injury or triggers instant death, you may immediately spend a psyche burst, 
effectively dying. Over the course of the next pressure clock, you slowly infect and take over the mind of the creature that killed 
you, even if it is already dead. Once pressure increases, the transformation is complete and you return, healing PSYCHE stress. If 
you have suffered instant death, you do not heal upon returning, and you keep any stress you took that triggered it. 
 
 
 


 
VIOLENCE 
Until naught stands above you but heaven. 
 
Fact: Those afflicted with Violence are not to be trusted. If you suspect a fellow exorcist may be afflicted with Violence, contact your local Admin 
Bounty Office branch immediately. 
 
Passive: Lonely God 
●​
Any intentional physical touch you engage in with mundane beings and fellow exorcists is instantly lethal to them, and handling mundane 
objects always breaks them in some way that renders them inoperable after one action. Killing any being forces you to roll PSYCHE, 
taking 1d2 sin on a failure. You may suppress the effects of this passive for a scene by taking 1d3+2 stress. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Peace Instant, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature or group of creatures within range, slashing at them with a pure and singular thought. Roll PSYCHE for damage 
and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on at least one success. 
■​
Is your target muddled and indecisive of will? 
■​
Is your target distracted or under duress in some way? 
■​
Is your target a large number or group of creatures? 
On at least one “yes”, a limb of the Admin’s choice is severed from the target for each “yes” answer. If this effect would kill a 
creature, you may have them explode into neatly sliced pieces after a few seconds. 
●​
Restraint Scene, Curse, Charm, Short Range 
○​
Target yourself and one other creature within range, rolling PSYCHE. Choose one of the following options for each success: 
■​
All targets become completely immune to the actions of all other targets, and cannot use psychic powers targeting each 
other until the duration ends. 
■​
All targets immediately become non-hostile towards each other until provoked again and are able to converse in a normal 
manner. This can grant speech and sentience to otherwise incapable beings if necessary. 
■​
All targets immediately stop in place, ceasing any current action, attack, or battle they are engaging in and resetting their 
attention, and must remain within a short distance of all other targets. 
If any stress or slashes are dealt to either target, this effect immediately ends and all non-allied targets deal +1 stress or slashes 
against all creatures present in the scene for the rest of the scene. 
●​
Heaven Scene, Charm, Self 
○​
Open your eyes. For the duration, you may choose to start your turn within arm’s reach of any creature present in the scene, 
silently appearing near them in the nearest available space. While in this state, rolling two or more 6s on a roll to inflict violence 
sends the victim flying in a ½ CAT distance line of your choosing. 
●​
Meaning Entire Mission, Transmute, Touch 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to transform the corpse or remnants of any being into a service weapon whose CAT equals your 
own, but is still considered mundane for the effects of targeting sins and supernatural creatures. You do not need to spend a 
psyche burst to convert a willing creature into a weapon.  
 
Only you may wield this weapon, and any attempt to wield or pick up this weapon by mundane creatures is instantly lethal to 
them; they are aware of this danger upon seeing it. You may keep one such weapon between hunts, which does not cost KP 
to pull out. 
●​
Perfect Unique, Short Range 
○​
This power does not consume a psyche burst. Whenever you enact mundane Violence on another being, mentally, emotionally, or 
physically, you may roll to attack as if using a service weapon regardless of what action you use. If this causes a target to be 
below ½ of its maximum talisman, it regards you as a respected equal and will willingly converse with you if you do not show overt 
hostility. If this causes a target to drop to 0 slashes left on its talisman it is considered nonlethal damage, and any similar target will 
regard you as a superior and will not choose to attack you of its own will, but will defend itself if you attack it. 
 
 


 
OPPRESS 
Bend the supernatural to your divine will. 
 
Fact: Oppress users are required to register a Subjugation Permission Verification 22F document before deployment. Failure to comply will lead to 
execution. 
 
Oppress and Bind are mutually exclusive. If you take one, you cannot take the other unless you replace all of the previous powers. This 
includes Bleed selections. 
 
Passive: Homunculus 
●​
You gain a small, meager supernatural familiar roughly no bigger than yourself. It is a supernatural being (either a sin, a drifter, or an 
artificial creature), and is invisible to mundane humans. It cannot speak but can understand basic language and commands. It has the 
following abilities: 
○​
It uses your skills for the purposes of actions. Its general capabilities are CAT0. 
○​
If it takes any stress, it evaporates and reforms at the end of the scene if in conflict, or after a short while if not. You may take any 
stress it takes in its place to preserve it. 
○​
You may sacrifice your turn in a conflict scene to command your Homunculus to act. Otherwise, it can’t act. 
○​
The Homunculus can walk or hover up to a meter off of the ground and around you, but cannot fully fly. It cannot move more than 
a short distance from you at any time. 
You may decide the form and appearance of the Homunculus in all of its forms, including its passives and active powers. 
 
Abilities: 
●​
Bloat Scene, Transmute 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus can ignore up to PSYCHE+1 stress taken per rest.  
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to cause your Homunculus to grow massive in size and strength. Its mundane physical size and 
strength both increase to CAT, and it gains a ½ CAT size stress talisman. Grant +1D the next time your Homunculus takes 
advantage of this effect. 
●​
Blister Scene, Transmute 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus gains the ability to carry others at a CAT0 pace. It can carry up to ½ CAT people on it easily, or up 
CAT people by halving its speed with much effort/exertion. 
○​
You can spend a psyche burst to cause your Homunculus’ speed to increase to CAT and allow it to carry CAT creatures without 
halving its speed. It gains the ability to jump and glide long distances and/or climb walls as long as it is carrying ½ CAT or less 
people. 
●​
Barren Scene, Transmute, Extreme Range 
○​
Passive: 1/scene you can concentrate and link one of your senses or your voice with your Homunculus as long as you are within 
short range. Your normal body entirely loses the linked aspect for the duration, and the linked aspect is always slightly muffled or 
subdued in some way that makes rolls using it hard by default. 
○​
You can spend a psyche burst to empower your Homunculus, extending its connection and max distance from you to extreme 
distance and granting the ability to seamlessly link all senses and/or voice between you without concentration. Linked aspects are 
no longer hard by default for the duration. 
●​
Burgeon Instant, Charm 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus gains an innate connection with some sort of force or energy, enhancing its abilities. Its passive 
capabilities in the chosen effects are ½ CAT, but are still considered mundane. Choose one of the following, or make your own: 
■​
Frost and water 
■​
Flames and light 
■​
Electricity and plasma 
■​
Earth and stone 
■​
Tar and darkness 
■​
Rot and acid 
■​
Wind and sand 
■​
Force and velocity 
■​
Blades and metal 
○​
When spending a psyche burst to raise your Homunculus’ capabilities you may raise it to CAT rather than ½ CAT if it uses its 
chosen domain, and if it is an attack it becomes supernatural, rolling PSYCHE and only spending a burst on at least one success. 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or any ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Bane Scene, Curse/Transmute, Self 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus gains the ability to be stored inside of yourself or a small item, either physically inside or in a 
demi-planar space. It can be warped in and out of its storage as an action as long as you can touch it. 
○​
You gain the ability to merge forms with your Homunculus, taking on its abilities for yourself. You can spend up to three psyche 
bursts, choosing an equal number of the following effects: 
■​
Your mundane strength increases to ½ CAT and your stress cap increases by 1 for the duration. 
■​
Your mundane speed increases to ½ CAT and you gain the ability to climb, glide, and jump long distances. 
■​
Humans find it incredibly difficult to look directly at you, like a Type II sin. 
■​
You may form weapons from any part of your body at will. These count as mundane service weapons but do not cost KP 
to summon or put away. 
■​
You may roll for a sin mark location, then choose a mark ability (or vice versa). You gain it for the duration. 
●​
If using the Mark Atrophy rules, you do the same for the Boon and Bane. 
The exact form and function in which this merge manifests is up to you. When the duration ends, you take 1d3 sin for every option 
chosen beyond the first. 
●​
Burden Scene, Transmute 
○​
