Passive: Your Homunculus may become visible to mundane humans if you wish, and can appear as a mundane animal or object 
even when in its monstrous form.  
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to cause your Homunculus to take on the appearance of a mundane human you have seen at 
least once up close. It appears and acts like a mundane human, and it can copy your words up to a short distance, projecting 
them through itself in the mimicked voice. Humans can find a tell that reveals its false nature upon close inspection.  
●​
Breach Scene, Transmute 
○​
Passive: 1/scene you may cause your Homunculus to go completely invisible to everything except yourself. While in this state it 
does not need to roll for stealth, but cannot interact with objects or the environment in complex ways such as opening locks, 
manipulating items, or making attacks. This lasts for a few minutes, or for the duration of an action in conflict. 
 


 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to allow your Homunculus to phase through solid matter as if it was thick mud, up to a short 
distance from you. While in this state it is incredibly difficult to see but can still interact with the physical world by “phasing” parts of 
its body back into existence. This lasts for a scene, or until you dismiss the effect as a free action. 
●​
Blame Instant, Curse, CAT Range 
○​
Passive: Whenever you take a bloody, gory, or severe wound, you can cause it to manifest on your Homunculus instead. It can 
take up to CAT+1 of these wounds before it evaporates. This does not reduce stress, but can affect hooks and narrative effects. 
○​
1/mission you may spend a psyche burst to swap in your Homunculus in the place of you or an ally within range you can see 
before a reaction resolves, which can include a severe attack. The Homunculus takes all of the stress and consequences from the 
reaction, but any sin dealt is applied to you instead. You lose the Homunculus and all of your Oppress powers until the end of the 
hunt, or until you spend 2 psyche bursts and 2 KP to re-summon it at the end of the next rest. 
●​
Brand Scene, Charm 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus gains passive intelligence, gaining the ability to speak and hold conversation, and it can telepathically 
communicate with you up to a short distance (only words) which requires focus on both ends. 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to grant your Homunculus the ability to act on its own intelligently without taking orders for the 
duration, and if applicable giving it one turn every other round in combat. 
●​
Broken Scene, Transmute 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus gains the ability to split into a swarm of smaller beings or objects, down to a small mouse in size. 
Retains its total volume, and can spread up to a short distance away from you and itself. It can sense the location of any part of 
itself within long range. 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to sink into your Homunculus’ swarm like a portal. You are essentially in a small pocket dimension, 
and only have a vague idea of your surroundings as a hazy blur around the swarm. You may enter and exit the swarm for the 
duration as long as it can coalesce again to let you out. If it takes stress that would cause it to disappear, you are ejected into the 
nearest open space that can accommodate you. 
●​
Barter Instant, Curse 
○​
Passive: You gain the ability to “loan” your Homunculus to others by engaging in a simple blood pact. This does not require the 
consent or awareness of mundane creatures as long as you possess fresh samples of their blood. You or the Homunculus can 
break this pact at any time you wish. 
○​
1/rest you may spend a psyche burst to transfer a total of ½ CAT injuries and afflictions between yourself and a willing target you 
can touch by engaging in a simple blood pact. This can easily save mundane humans from the brink of death or kill them instantly. 
●​
Brood Unique 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus can spend a scene to incubate the genetic or psychic material of other creatures inside of it, taking on 
their form in a grotesque way. After doing so, you may add one other Blasphemy power appropriate to the consumed material to 
the homunculus. Doing so again replaces the previous choice, and this carries between hunts. 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to activate the incubated power at CAT-1 as your Homunculus morphs into a visage of its genetic 
donor. 
●​
Bind Instant 
○​
Passive: You can attach your Homunculus to a target creature or object, latching onto its surface or flesh. While attached, it 
ignores restrictions on its distance from you, but takes all stress the target takes. Graced creatures can see the Homunculus, but 
mundane creatures will feel a more metaphorical “weight on their shoulders”. You must either go back to pick it up or evaporate it 
and let it remanifest. 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to command your Homunculus to envelop, chain down, or otherwise immobilize a target up to 
CAT+1 size within range, storing them inside of itself. Halting a fast-moving target can potentially cause harm. The binding has a 
2+CAT talisman, and the Homunculus evaporates once it is broken. 
●​
Bash Instant, Transmute 
○​
Passive: Your Homunculus gains the ability to act as your Service Weapon, attack using its weapon features, and does not cost 
KP to pull out. It appears as a mundane weapon to mundane creatures. If evaporated, it becomes a mundane featureless weapon 
until it remanifests. 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to implant any handheld mundane weapon you find into your Homunculus, choosing one of the 
following types appropriate to its nature, which carries between hunts: 
■​
Blast: Short distance, area of effect. Deals +1 slashes against large groups of targets. 
■​
Blade: Adjacent distance, single target. Deals +1 slashes against targets that are surrounded by 2 or more allies in 
melee. 
■​
Bullet: Long distance, pierce. Can easily puncture through most mundane material such as wood, thin metal, and 
concrete. Lining up a shot can target 2 separate targets at once with one attack. 
●​
Bleed Unique, Charm 
○​
Passive: As part of taking this power, choose one non-Oppress power, taking the sin cap reduction for a new Blasphemy if 
applicable. The chosen power is now permanently allocated to the Homunculus as part of its abilities, and using it requires having 
your homunculus present. This power can be taken any number of times. 
■​
When casting the allocated power, it must originate from the Homunculus. 
■​
The details and bounds of the power may be stretched beyond what is conventionally allowed. Gain +1D on weird or 
exceptionally creative uses of the power. 
■​
If your Homunculus evaporates, you lose access to all allocated powers until it remanifests. 
○​
You may spend a psyche burst to cast any allocated power.  
 
 


 
RIME 
Your personal hell, frozen over. 
 
Fact: Rapid Onset Extreme Hyperthermia is the number one cause of Rime mortality. 
 
Passive: Rhigosis 
●​
All of the fluids produced by your body, including your blood, saliva, tears, sweat, and other such fluids, are replaced by a supercooled 
saline substance with a mineral composition similar to deep-sea brine. Any direct contact with large quantities of this fluid is enough to 
incur extreme frostbite. Once per scene you may gain or grant +1D when taking advantage of this fluid. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Still Scene, Touch, Transmute 
○​
Target a creature within range, rolling PSYCHE and taking 1d3 stress. If the target is a supernatural creature, this roll is hard. On 
at least one success the target immediately grows sluggish and hazy, their actions and thoughts frozen to a crawl. The target is 
considered incapacitated, but this effect shatters the moment a hostile action is taken against them.  
●​
Glacier Instant, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
You flash freeze an up to CAT sized object or area within range. Any flexible objects or objects with moving or mechanical parts 
are immediately frozen solid, and any objects in midair are frozen in place by spires of ice. Any form of environmental hazard is 
immediately overwritten by a Slippery complication that affects all creatures. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or 
grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Starve Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
Target a creature you can touch, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On a success, their mind 
is shunted into an endless desolate plane of ice and snow for a fraction of a second, which feels like up to CAT^2 days from their 
perspective. This can easily alter the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this 
effect. 
●​
Plunge Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Target a creature within range, momentarily expelling them into the depths of an arctic sea. Roll PSYCHE for damage, and gaining 
+1D for each “no” answer.  
■​
Does your target have exposed skin or flesh? 
■​
Can your target hold their breath in spite of the shock? 
On at least one “no” answer, the target is thoroughly chilled by the experience and its speed is greatly reduced until it can spend 
time to warm itself. 
●​
Bleak Until Rest, Curse, CAT Area, Long Range 
○​
For the duration, mundane creatures in a CAT sized area of your choice within range have extremely dulled emotional responses, 
making them less receptive to any emotional trigger, positive or negative. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes 
advantage of this effect. 
 
When this effect ends, roll PSYCHE. Every mundane creature affected this way remains affected for a number of weeks equal to 
the number of successes. 
 
 
 


 
ONE 
Too much. It’s all too much. 
 
Fact: Despite the demonstrated powers of One users, they are near universally plagued with extreme self doubt and conflict, making them 
excellent candidates for the CAIN HIGH ADMIN Asset Conditioning and Reeducation Programme. 
 
Passive: Singular 
●​
You are the only you. Creatures can never imitate anything about you without a dead giveaway, and any slander or false information about 
you is extremely apparent to any creature that observes it, even if they do not know why. You are immune to effects that would duplicate 
you or merge you with other creatures. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Unify Scene, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
Target a human within range. For the duration, all other humans within the area share and agree with the target’s opinions and 
views. No one but the target notices anything is wrong if exposed to obvious evidence of this paradigm shift. Gain or grant +1D the 
next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
At the end of the duration, roll PSYCHE. On at least one success all affected humans are permanently altered by this effect, 
retaining a number of opinions and views of the Admin’s choice equal to the number of successes rolled.   
●​
Consolidate Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
As an action, touch an object within range. All other objects of the same type within an up to CAT sized area are rapidly 
accelerated towards the object you touched, merging with it. While merged, the object’s properties are multiplied by the number of 
objects merged into it (such as a merged bottle being able to hold up to 10 bottles of water inside of itself, or a merged coin being 
worth the combined value of all merged coins).  
 
You may dismiss this effect as an action, causing the items to rapidly separate like a grenade, rolling PSYCHE if you wish to deal 
damage.  
●​
Mono Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
This effect does not consume a psyche burst. 1/scene whenever an effect you can see within range would cause multiple of a 
single action to happen, such as a dozen sword slashes, five cars passing, or one hundred windows shattering, roll PSYCHE. On 
at least one success the total magnitude of the actions is preserved, but they are merged into a single instance of that action. This 
can cause a dozen sword slashes to converge into a single massive slash, five cars passing into one bullet-speed vehicle, etc. 
this can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
First Passive, Self 
○​
You must always take the first action in any conflict scene, and cannot be ambushed or taken by surprise, though this does not 
extend to allied creatures. 
 
Whenever you act directly after an enemy in a conflict scene, you may spend a psyche burst to take your turn before them in the 
time stream. After your turn ends, the enemy’s action goes through as normal. If you incapacitate a target this way it cancels out 
any effect that would have happened from their action.  
●​
Solo Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Target a group talisman of creatures within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on a success. On at least one 
success, every creature in that group talisman is fused into a single creature, whose size and physical capabilities are equal to 
twice a normal version of itself. This does not change the size of its talisman, simply changing its multiple nature into a singular 
one. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 
 


 
MANY 
What is more beautiful to one, than another? 
 
Fact: Due to the ██████ incident in 19██, exorcists who display powers that align with the Many blasphemy are forbidden to carry any 
supernatural weaponry whilst duplicating. 
 
Passive: Second 
●​
Whenever you spend KP to pull out a non-consumable mundane item, or pick up a mundane item, you receive a second copy. Each 
object you pick up can only be affected this way once. You may spend a psyche burst to ignore the restrictions on secondary duplications 
once per burst spent. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Diverge Instant, Curse, Self 
○​
Whenever your fellow exorcists make a plan to divide into separate groups, you may choose to go with both groups at once by 
duplicating yourself. You do not telekinetically share any information or stress between you, but as soon as either of you takes an 
injury the other vanishes in an agonized scream, melting into a puddle of viscous fluid. You must choose which of yourself to keep 
if the group rejoins; witnessing your duplicate directly immediately causes an injury. 
●​
Duality Scene, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
Target an object within range that has at least two distinct states built into its function. For the duration, you may combine any 
property of two of its states into a third “quasi” state, such as a lightbulb emitting light while quasi-off, a car being completely silent 
while the engine is quasi-on, or a knife being able to stab things while still retracted. If the states require a notable transition 
between them, you may also choose any attribute from during the transition as well. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally 
takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Pair Until Rest, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
Target two roughly identical objects within range. For the duration, they are considered to be exactly the same object regardless of 
distance from each other. Anything put into the item exists in duplicate until taken out, and any damage or alterations clone onto 
the other of the pair. If either of the pair is destroyed, the paired object emits an effect similar to whatever caused the other to 
break. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
1/rest targeting a PSYCHE-1 sized or smaller object does not require a psyche burst. 
●​
Double Scene, Charm, Self 
○​
For the duration, you exist as two bodies that are nearly overlapped on top of each other, one physical and one in a quantum 
state. Each may move and pose on its own as long as it remains within adjacent distance of the other, and you may swap 
between which body is physical at will. Whichever form is not manifested is intangible, and swapping between forms counts as 
teleporting. Gain or grant +1D the next time you take advantage of this effect. 
●​
Bisect Scene, Transmute, CAT Area 
○​
As an action, target a CAT sized area. That area is split into two identical, overlaid areas. Whenever a creature enters the area or 
is inside the area when it is created, if it is a mundane creature you decide which duplicate it goes to, and if the creature is 
supernatural you must roll PSYCHE to decide which duplicate it goes to. On a failure, the creature decides. Creatures in one 
duplicate cannot see, detect, or interact with creatures in the other. Whenever you enter a duplicated area, you may choose which 
one you and any allies enter. 
 
 
 


 
CHOKE 
breathe I can’t breath I can’t breathe I can’t breath I can’t 
 
Fact: Users of the Choke blasphemy are unable to undergo what is commonly described as a “panic attack”. Should they begin to show symptoms 
that commonly align with such an experience, evacuate the area immediately. 
 
Passive: Tide 
●​
You start every mission with a Tide hook that follows all rules of a normal hook but can never be removed. The Tide hook influences the 
potency and other attributes of your Choke powers. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Drown Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
Target a creature you can see within range, inflicting them with a roiling tide of rising panic. Roll PSYCHE for damage and answer 
the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on at least one success. 
■​
Is your target exhausted, or under extreme physical duress? 
■​
Is your target feeling extreme terror or anxiety? 
If both answers are “yes”, the target suffers a heart attack that instantly kills most mundane creatures. If the target survives, gain 
or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Fall Scene, Short Range 
○​
Target an up to CAT sized creature or object you can see within range. If the target is an unwilling creature, roll PSYCHE, only 
spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On at least one success, any form of structural support collapses and weakens; 
beams buckle, joints give out, muscles weaken, bolts rattle out, etc. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant 
+1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Cacophony Scene, CAT Area 
○​
For the duration of this power, senses in a CAT area bubble around you raise to terrible levels, easily overwhelming and dizzying a 
creature without proper protection. As part of this power, roll PSYCHE, choosing a number of senses equal to 1 + the number of 
successes. 
■​
Light and Color 
■​
Sound and Voices 
■​
Texture and Temperature 
■​
Smell and Humidity 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Asphyxiate Scene, Short Range 
○​
Target up to CAT creatures or objects within range. If any target is an unwilling creature, roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche 
burst on a success. On at least one success, all targets are deprived of resources they need to function or survive, like electricity, 
air, water, fuel, etc. This causes a target to immediately begin gasping, stuttering, and choking, but cannot kill a target for as long 
as it is active. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Wail Scene, Curse, CAT Range 
○​
Target a creature within range. If the target is an unwilling creature, roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one 
success. On at least one success, the target’s mind is overwhelmed with panic and terror and they begin ranting incoherently or 
wailing in distress. For the duration, any sound the target makes is one of incoherent distress, and it has an incredibly hard time 
thinking clearly as its heart hammers in its ears. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time 
you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
Tide Hook: 
This 5-slash hook operates like a normal hook, but can never be removed. Your Choke powers gain additional effects based on the progress of the 
Tide hook, which stack with all previous effects: 
●​
1-2 slash: +1 CAT to your Choke powers, a constant ringing fills your ears, and you hear your heartbeat. You roll -1D on rolls requiring 
balance and hearing. 
●​
3-4 slashes: +1 CAT to your Choke powers, your vision swims, and you begin a cold sweat. You roll -1D on rolls requiring finesse and 
sight. 
●​
5 slashes: When your Tide hook fills, immediately roll a Resist roll as if suffering sin overflow: 
○​
On a failure, or if you Give Up, instead of going Imago you must immediately cast a Choke power whose only affected target is 
yourself. If you need to roll Psyche for this power, you always roll at least one success. 
○​
On a success, instead of gaining a Sin Mark you may choose to gain a Sin Scar, a purely aesthetic alternative. You roll on the 
location table as normal, but do not roll an ability. This Sin Scar not affect your sin cap. 
Regardless of whether you succeed or fail, you suffer a severe breakdown that reduces you to little more than an incoherent mess until 
the start of the next scene.
 
 


 
PIT 
You made your grave, now lie in it.  
 
Fact: Do not enter a doorway if you desire to. The only purpose for doorways is casual transportation between adjacent rooms. If you or a fellow 
exorcist is expressing desire to enter a doorway, contact your nearest Castle Administration Office immediately. 
 
Passive: Dweller 
●​
You gain the ability to move at normal speed through any opening or tunnel large enough to fit your skull, contorting and squeezing your 
way through without injury. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Grave Entire Mission, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Target a doorway, hole, or other portal within range. Then, target a creature within range, rolling PSYCHE if it is unwilling. On at 
least one success, the target is drawn to the portal, compelled to enter it by some unknown instinct. Once when this effect 
happens, and once per pressure afterwards, you may roll PSYCHE as if damaging their talisman. If this “kills” them, they enter the 
door and instantly vanish with no trace. Every time this kills a creature, you must either choose a new target you can see or take 
stress and become the new target. Supernatural creatures may end this effect on themselves when pressure increases. 
●​
Constrict Scene, Transmute, CAT Area 
○​
As an action, you may cause all passageways and paths within a CAT-sized area to constrict and shrink into a claustrophobic and 
cave-like environment. This may cause buildings to appear to bend and meld overhead, hallways to crack and shrink inwards, and 
alleyways to press together. Open movement becomes incredibly difficult for all creatures, and possibly impossible for large ones. 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Chasm Instant, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
As an action, touch a surface, sending a spiraling crack along it towards up to CAT creatures within range. Roll PSYCHE, only 
spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On at least one success, a massive chasm with a distance across up to ½ CAT 
opens on the surface into a shadowy void, attempting to suck in adjacent creatures and dealing slashes on their talisman equal to 
the number of successes on your PSYCHE roll. The chasm’s vacuum then ceases, and it re-seals itself at the end of the scene or 
when you close it as an action. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Bury Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
Target an up to CAT sized creature or object you can see within range. If the target is an unwilling creature, roll PSYCHE, only 
spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On at least one success, the target is sucked into and sealed within the nearest 
surface in a claustrophobic rectangular space, like a coffin. This cannot kill the target via suffocation, but they may be unable to 
break out on their own due to the nature or material of the surface. The target may emerge from the surface at the end of the 
scene as they claw their way out. 
●​
Carve Instant, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature or object within range that you know of but cannot see, rolling PSYCHE for damage, only spending a psyche 
burst on at least one success. On at least one success all mundane material in a straight line between you and your target has a 
hole erased cleanly through it in roughly the same shape and size as the target’s silhouette, up to CAT in size. 
 
 


 
INSTRUMENT 
Is your hand your hand? Is your heart your heart? 
 
Fact: The perfect Exorcist is one who accepts their truth as a divine instrument of CAIN, and submits to it. Rejection is suffering, acceptance is 
peace. 
 
Passive: Honor 
●​
When you first gain this passive, choose a CAT0 sized, non-consumable, common mundane object. You may morph your entire body into 
a functionally identical copy of that object at will, but must roll PSYCHE to return to your human form. On a failure, you are stuck in your 
object form until the end of the scene. You may still use psyche powers and see clearly while in your object form. You still need to breathe 
and lose the ability to move unless the object possesses a means to do so such as wheels or a hinge to flip around, and do not generate 
limited resources if you need them to function (such as camera film or ammunition). 
 
Abilities 
●​
Siblings Instant, CAT Range 
○​
As an action, summon an up to CAT+1 sized volume of objects that vaguely match the type of object used by your Honor passive. 
Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a success, your siblings rocket towards or otherwise attack a 
single target within range, dealing stress or slashes on its talisman equal to the number of successes rolled. 
●​
Child Until Rest, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Touch an up to CAT sized object within range. You imbue the object with a false and decaying facsimile of life, transmuting it into a 
human form with an appearance, personality, and apparel resembling its object nature. While mentally mature, it is not used to 
complex thought and is highly suggestible. If the object is too large, like a car or building, the object is simply referenced and not 
consumed in the process. 
●​
Family Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature or object within range, rolling PSYCHE if they are unwilling, only spending a psyche burst on at least one 
success. On a success, they are immediately transformed into a random common mundane item (a mug, a shovel, a book, a mop, 
etc) or a volume of items equal to the volume of their body, leaving behind anything they are wearing or holding. Supernatural 
targets may choose to end this curse any time after a round has passed, or any time after they take any stress or slashes on their 
talisman. 
●​
Lineage Transmute, Self 
○​
As part of this power, choose a single non-consumable, mundane item of ½ CAT size or smaller. During the next rest, you may 
take an injury to tear out your own heart and forcibly implant it into the object, abandoning your previous body’s afflictions and 
hooks, and gaining a new body that is the chosen object. Your Honor passive changes to the new object permanently, or until you 
use this power again. 
●​
Legacy Entire Mission, Transmute, Self 
○​
As an action, target a handheld object you are holding. You permanently inlay it into your flesh for 1d3-1 stress in a visually 
conspicuous way, and gain the ability to embody an abstract concept of the object 1/scene (such as a hammer increasing the 
force of your punch, or a hose letting you expel water from your mouth) to gain or grant a mundane +1D to an action. For every 
number of merged objects equal to PSYCHE, your stress cap reduces by 1. 
 
Child (human???) Execution talisman PSYCHE (solo) 
●​
Possesses innate knowledge about its source, such as a lightbulb being a talented electrician, or a stove knowing how to cook. 
●​
Extremely suggestible and naive, takes almost no effort to convince. 
●​
If killed, revert back to their object form and shatter. 
If you would have more than one Child active at any time, simply add them into the talisman as a group and increase the talisman size by 1 for 
each Child beyond the first. 
 
 
 


 
CLARITY 
You are the only one. 
 
Fact: TEMERITY has not yet found an objective way to prove any hypothesized form of objective reality. 
 
Passive: Permanence 
●​
Items that belong to you quite literally do not exist until you directly interact with them, meaning they cannot be stolen from you, lost, 
destroyed, or otherwise interacted with while not held or within your line of sight. Rolls to find objects you have dropped or lost are always 
hard for you, and impossible for others. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Blink Curse, Instant, Long Range 
○​
Once per scene when the Admin narrates a single inanimate object or item up to CAT in size, you may spend a psyche burst to 
interject with “What [target]?”, instantly and silently erasing it from reality, filling in any gaps that would give away its absence. This 
can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Blur Charm, Curse, Scene 
○​
Target up to CAT creatures within range, rolling PSYCHE if any are unwilling. On at least one success, their psychic emissions are 
completely suppressed as they appear entirely mundane. When the duration ends, or if you end it early as an action, the target 
instantly forgets everything that has happened since the start of this effect, and begins emitting psychic energy once more. Gain 
or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Alone Curse, Short Range, Scene 
○​
Target a single mundane creature or exorcist within range, rolling PSYCHE if they are unwilling. On at least one success, the 
target begins to exist in a quantum state when not in your direct line of sight, becoming able to disappear and reappear anywhere 
where they are able to normally move in an instant. Every time they would re-enter your line of sight, roll PSYCHE. On at least 
one success, you choose whether they stay in their new position or snap back to where you last saw them. On a failure, the 
Admin may choose where they have appeared. 
●​
Dissociate Transmute, Self, Scene 
○​
Once per rest, you may dissolve your perception into a dissociated blur, jumping forward in time by CAT+2 actions, which must 
include one of your own at the end. During this time, all allies take their actions one after another, then all risk dice (if applicable) 
combined are rolled at once, once all allied actions have completed. Gain a +1D to your closing action as you snap out of your 
dissociation in the middle of an action. 
●​
Delirium Transmute, CAT area / CAT size, Scene 
○​
Whenever the admin describes an inanimate thing you have encountered previously at least once this mission, you may interject 
with “I could have sworn it was [adjective]…”, warping the reality of an up to CAT sized area or a single CAT sized object to 
accommodate your memory. The list of acceptable adjectives are as follows: 
■​
Broken/Abandoned 
■​
Fine/Clean 
■​
Open/Closed 
■​
On/Powered 
■​
Off/Shut Down 
■​
Gone/Destroyed 
■​
Here/New 
 
 


 
TORMENT 
All sins are born in fear of hell. 
 
Fact: TEMERITY has not yet encountered a user of Torment with the sadism required to adequately employ their power. Mental reconditioning is 
offered to applicable handlers at no additional cost. 
 
Passive: Briar 
●​
Your grace oozes from you in a way that makes any harm you inflict always agonizingly painful, regardless of how minor. You deal +1 
slashes against mundane humans, and gain +1D when attempting to nonlethally torture, cripple, emotionally abuse or verbally deride a 
mundane creature. Once per scene the Admin may choose to make a single mundane action you do targeting another being agonizing for 
either you or the recipient. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Agony Until Rest, Curse, Long Range 
○​
With a snap of your fingers, you cause a debilitating burst of pain to erupt within the body or mind of a target within range. When 
you use this power, roll PSYCHE for damage and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only 
spending a Psyche Burst on success. 
■​
Do you know one of their greatest insecurities? 
■​
Do you know what their pain feels like? (Immediately take 1d3 stress) 
On any “yes” answer, the target completely loses sensation in their limbs as most of their body goes numb from the 
overloaded nerves. 
●​
Barbs Until Rest, Curse, Short Range 
○​
You spread suffering, like you always do. As an action, target a creature within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a psyche 
burst on at least one success. On at least one success, choose one of the following options: 
■​
Rip: You speak a crushing insult in the voice of a creature they love. They cannot feel a positive emotion until the end of 
the next rest, such as joy, surprise, affection, or confidence. 
■​
Tear: You set their nerves alight with chaotic pulses. They cannot experience pleasant sensations until the end of the next 
rest, such as pleasant temperature, softness, or relaxation. 
You may forgo rolling PSYCHE, taking the same consequence as your target. If you do, you automatically trigger the same effect 
on both yourself and your target for the duration. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time 
you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Rend Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
Target a creature within range. As long as you know at least one other creature of emotional significance to the target, you may 
roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On at least one success, you may ask at minimum 4-PSYCHE 
simple yes or no questions, up to PSYCHE questions, about the target’s relationship with the aforementioned creature. The Admin 
chooses a single question among those asked to answer truthfully, discarding the rest. Any creature can only be affected by this 
power once unless their bond with the previously used creature ends, in which case you may use this power again with a different 
bond of theirs. 
●​
Nexus Scene, CAT Area 
○​
Your grace flares up, whirling around you like a hurricane in a CAT-sized area. All beings in an up to CAT sized area, including 
yourself, are now subject to lethal-speed winds that deal 1 non lethal stress or slash on a talisman to any being that takes an 
action outdoors, or within the room the Nexus originated from. This wind carries projectiles that easily shred through plants, 
shatter windows, scar and scrape concrete, and chip metal. Any being that uses its action or reaction to entirely shelter from this 
effect is safe from it. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage 
of this effect. 
●​
Flare Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature you can see within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On at least one 
success, a recent physical or emotional wound flares up in a way that causes extreme discomfort or pain (but no damage), 
preventing them from adequately resting, sleeping, or relaxing for the duration. The longer the pain draws on, the more psychically 
motivated they become to reach out to others. This effect ends if they make physical contact with someone they consider 
“comforting” or close to them, or if you dismiss the effect as an action. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes 
advantage of this information.
 
 


 
SILHOUETTE  
A dark shape against the bright light. It almost looks human. 
 
Fact: All corpses retrieved from missions are to be burned as per HELIOD REPO Protocol, by punishment of formal execution at Throne 1A. 
Failure to dispose of corpses will result in ████. Do not question the event horizon of the deceased. 
 
Passive: Eclipse 
●​
You do not possess a heartbeat, blood, or body heat, you do not automatically breathe or blink, and you can remain perfectly still 
indefinitely. You gain the Vanish talisman, and the ability to empower your Silhouette powers with the Fade hook. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Stare Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
You stare with your dead eyes directly into another’s, forcing them to relive every moral transgression they have ever made. Roll 
PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On at least one success, you learn a number of their 
transgressions equal to the number of successes. Choose a single transgression from your available options, forcing the target’s 
mind into a looping playback. This can easily stun or disorient any creature.  Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes 
advantage of this effect.  
■​
You may take the Fade hook to roll PSYCHE again, forcing deeper into their subconscious. On a success. you completely 
paralyze them until the end of the scene, which ends early if they take any stress or slashes on their talisman. On a 
failure, gain 1d3 sin and you become tormented by a single transgression of your own. 
●​
Gasp Scene, Curse, Charm, Long Range 
○​
You induce silent death in a target you can see within range. Target a non-sin creature within range, rolling PSYCHE if the 
creature is an unwilling supernatural creature. On at least one success, the target instantly keels over, apparently dead by any 
measurable capacity. At the end of the scene, the target regenerates any damage done while dead and pops back up with as 
much health as it died with and mild hypothermia.  
■​
You may take the Fade hook to increase the number of creatures affected to CAT+1. 
●​
Pass Instant, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
You close your eyes, causing all non-human mundane organic life within a CAT sized area to instantly die and dry out, including 
plants and even single celled organisms. All matter in the area becomes perfectly sterile and preserved until interacted with by 
outside beings. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of 
this effect.  
■​
You may take the Fade hook to instantly disintegrate any ½ CAT size or smaller organic matter affected this way into fine 
ash. 
●​
Door Passive 
○​
Whenever you kill a mundane creature, or reduce them to PSYCHE slashes remaining on their talisman, time freezes and they 
have an out of body experience. The target hears words in the voices of lost loved ones, their god/s, or other figures important to 
them, calling to them from around a corner or behind a large object. If they choose to follow the voices, time resumes and their 
body immediately vanishes, leaving any clothes and gear on them to clatter to the ground. If they choose to remain, time resumes 
as normal and nothing seems to happen. 
■​
You may take the Fade hook to roll PSYCHE, dealing slashes to their talisman equal to the number of successes rolled. If 
this reduces their talisman to one or fewer slashes remaining, or they are already dead, they are forced to follow the 
voice. 
●​
Ghost Scene, Curse, Short Range 
○​
For the duration, you become locked in step to a creature within range. Your footsteps step in perfect time with theirs, forcing you 
to stay within short range. You are able to remain perfectly beyond their peripheral vision if they turn or look around, and can 
follow them through doorways or barriers. This effect immediately ends if you take any violent action against the target or move 
beyond a short range from them. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally 
takes advantage of this effect. 
■​
You may take the Fade hook to make yourself completely invisible to all mundane creatures besides the target and roll 
PSYCHE. On a success, you are also invisible to all supernatural creatures besides the target. 
 
Fade 
When this hook fills, roll 1d6. On a 2+ your extremities fade in opacity slightly as interacting with the physical world with the faded parts becomes 
hard by default until the end of the scene. Every time this happens during the same mission, it gets worse (first your fingers and toes, then your 
hands and feet, then your arms and legs, then your torso, then your whole body). On a 1, or if your whole body fades, slash your Vanish talisman 
once. 
 
Vanish 
A six-slash talisman. This talisman can only be erased at the end of the mission, erasing half of the total number of slashes on it rounded up. The 
following stacking effects happen when this talisman is slashed: 
●​
1 slash: You are permanently afflicted with the feeling that something is watching you. 
●​
2-3 slashes: You begin to see an imperceptible figure in your peripheral vision around corners and behind objects. Nothing is there when 
you look. 
●​
4-5 slashes: You hear a second set of footsteps behind you, trying to match yours. Nothing is there when you look, and no one else hears 
anything. 
●​
6 slashes. The footsteps and visions stop for the rest of the scene. You silently vanish at the end of the scene the moment no one is 
looking directly at you. You leave behind any clothes, kit, and gear you possess as they clatter to the ground. Make a new character.
 


 
SHRED  
And their music was electric. 
 
Fact: The recent decentralization of music production within the industry has led to a massive uptick in the personalization and intimate importance 
of music and music production to common folk, which could explain the correlated drop in overall quantity due to the law of large numbers. 
 
Passive: Amp 
●​
You possess an imaginary version of an instrument of your choice, such as an air guitar, air drums, air saxophone, air microphone etc. You 
may play your imaginary instrument with perfect proficiency up to a volume of 130 decibels. 1/scene gain or grant +1D when you take 
advantage of a sound produced by your instrument. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Crash Instant, CAT Distance 
○​
Your instrument flashes to life in a blaze of light, becoming a physical object you use. Playing this instrument once destroys it and 
causes a violently destructive sound wave to rocket forth, rolling PSYCHE for damage in an indiscriminate CAT distance long 
cone. Objects within the area are cracked and blown away by the sheer force of volume, and targeted creatures are deafened for 
the rest of the scene. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Tempo Scene, CAT+2 Area 
○​
For the duration, all actions and effects within a CAT+2 sized area around you begin to sync perfectly with a beat, down to an 
eighth note. Every observable phenomenon matches this rhythm, including wildlife sounds, footsteps, heartbeats, attacks, words 
spoken, the movement of objects, etc. No one except you and your allies notices this phenomenon. Gain or grant +1D the next 
time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Production Scene, Short Range 
○​
For the duration you may alter the following things within range once per round in addition to your normal action: 
■​
The number, color, intensity, and position of abstract spotlights and laser lights, including tracking them to the positions of 
moving things. 
■​
The number, color, intensity, and position of pyrotechnics. 
■​
The presence and thickness of artificial smoke and fog. 
■​
The power to normal electrical lights within range, including screens. 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Mosh Scene, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
You begin to play a captivating melody on your instrument, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one 
success. On a success, choose one of the following ways to entrance humans around you: 
■​
Rile: you play an energetic rhythm that pumps up any human that can hear it. Humans become extremely energetic and 
motivated, succumbing to extreme mob mentality and forming potentially violent crowds. 
■​
Mellow: you play a somber melody that saps the energy from your surroundings. Humans become extremely demotivated 
and pensive, their attention drawn from whatever they were previously doing to sadly stand and stare at you for the 
duration. 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Band Scene, Long Range 
○​
For the duration, all allied creatures receive a copy of an instrument of your choice, benefitting from a copy of your Amp passive 
for as long as they wield it. All instruments, including your own and any real instruments in the area, become indestructible 
mundane service weapons with a CAT equal to your PSYCHE for the duration. As long as the entire band consistently plays the 
song with their action, you may gain or grant +1D to one action per round that takes advantage of the song’s energy.
 
 


 
RIVER 
They all look the same inside. 
 
Fact: Exsanguination has been repeatedly stated to be a highly uncomfortable and painful experience, and as such it is not recommended to test 
the volume of blood an exorcist can produce. For information on the abnormal rates of hematologic regeneration in exorcist biology, see █████ 
████ at ██████. 
 
Passive: Pulse 
●​
You may emit blood from any orifice or surface on your body at will with up to enough force to act as a long range mundane service 
weapon. Any ruptures or cuts you expel blood from heal over flawlessly at the end of the scene, and you may call any of your blood that 
you have lost back to yourself at a walking pace. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Transfusion Self, Unique 
○​
As an action, target a mundane creature within range with a gaping or open wound. Your body melts into ichor and is sucked into 
the wound, stitching it up and healing the creature by slashes on its talisman equal the number of successes on a PSYCHE roll. 
While you are within a creature, you gain the following effects: 
■​
Choose hearing or sight. You can utilize their chosen sense but the other becomes muffled or blurry beyond a short range. 
■​
The CAT of your host’s physical capabilities increases to PSYCHE. 
■​
Once per scene you may move up to three of your host’s limbs in a jerky and unsteady motion, which could possibly put 
them in harm’s way. 
■​
You cannot exit your host until they die, as you have replaced a majority of their blood. 
At any time, you may choose to stop cooperating with the host’s cells, causing them to violently reject you. Your host takes 
slashes on their talisman equal to the number you originally rolled to heal. If this does not kill them, both you and your host take 1 
stress or slash on their talisman per scene until you are free. 
●​
Ichor Scene, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
All mundane beings within a CAT sized area around you become excited by and drawn to the scent of blood, and will be coerced 
into consuming any that they see. Creatures in this fervor gladly chase and tear open wounded or bleeding creatures they can 
see, ally or foe, as long as it does not interfere with basic self-preservation. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or 
grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Trickle Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Target a creature within range, causing their body to violently reject its own blood cells. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE 
and answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on success. 
■​
Is your target suffering from illness or disease? 
■​
Does your target have an unhealed wound? 
On at least one success, the target takes stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of successes rolled +PSYCHE 
over time. They will consistently take 1 stress or slash on their talisman every 5 minutes until they have taken all of the damage 
dealt, beginning with minor nosebleeds and ending with complete exsanguination and possible death. You take half of the stress 
or slashes dealt this way at a rate of 1 per 5 minutes as well. This power cannot affect a sin while it is within its palace. 
●​
Spray Scene, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
For the duration, you may seamlessly transport any handheld-sized item between any pools or globules of your blood big enough 
to let it through. Once per scene while this effect is active, you may transport your entire body through a large enough gathering of 
your own blood, appearing from any similar gathering up to a short distance away. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Lash Scene, Charm, Free 
○​
Once per scene whenever you interact with another being, you may mark a single exchange or trade of information (one piece of 
information from each side of the discussion that are in context to each other) by pricking the fingers of both participants, willing or 
not. At the end of the scene, one of the following two things happens: 
■​
If both parties lie, or if both parties tell the truth, all involved parties receive a psychic notification. It is impossible to tell the 
difference between a dual lie and dual truth notification. 
■​
If only one party lies and the other tells the truth, the party who told the truth is notified that a lie has happened, but not 
what specifically was a lie or what about it was a lie. The party who lied receives a notification similar to the first option, 
where it is unclear whether it is a truth or lie. 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
RUSH 
Stay winning, stay rolling, stay electric. 
 
Fact: 98% of Rush exorcists stop gambling before making a significant breakthrough in their psychic potential. 
 
Passive: High Roller 
●​
Your character will never lose at an in-game chance or bet using cards, dice, or coins. Ever. If an effect would force you to lose, a bizarre 
butterfly effect prevents the chance or bet from occurring in the first place. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Lucky Scene, Charm, Touch 
○​
Target one creature within range you can see. For the duration, any form of physical contact that is not an intentional attack 
cannot hurt them. Falling or collapsing objects safely avoid them, wind curves around them, rain never touches their body, a 
crashing car flips a mere centimeter over their head, etc. Any direct attack can still hit them as normal. This can easily affect the 
parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Roulette Instant, Long Range 
○​
You level a weapon or burst of psyche power at your own vital organs or head, preparing to damage yourself. As an action you 
may attempt to attack yourself, choosing up to CAT+1 creatures within range, which must include yourself, assigning them 
numbers in order (1, 2, 3, etc). Then, wager up to PSYCHE+4 damage (0-7 at CAT 5). Then roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche 
burst on at least one success; on a success, roll a 1dX, where X is equal to the number of creatures targeted. The creature with 
the matching number to the one you rolled takes the wagered damage in your stead, which may instantly kill mundane humans 
depending on where you attack yourself. If you roll your number, you take all of the stress wagered. 
●​
Ante Instant, Self 
○​
For the duration of a single wager, you gain the ability to gamble with things normally thought impossible, from the following list: 
■​
Voices 
■​
Names 
■​
Body parts 
■​
Concepts of ownership 
■​
Connections with others 
■​
Skills and muscle memory 
■​
Utter obedience to a single nonlethal request 
You lose your passive for the duration and gain no bonus to the act of gambling itself, simply the ability to raise its stakes 
dramatically. All trades and stakes must be perfectly equal, no bargaining for similar items (an eye for an eye, a name for a name). 
Once the gamble has been concluded, the gambled items are supernaturally given to the winning party. Any being that gambles in 
this deal is forced to obey their end of the deal, and the winner may choose to return their winnings at any time. 
●​
Bluff Reaction, Short Range 
○​
As an action, target an allied creature within range who has just taken an action. Their action appears inverted in luck to any 
observers; a successful action fails horribly, or a failed action seems to succeed flawlessly. At the end of the scene, the action 
retcons itself, reality violently reverting to what it is supposed to be. This can cause a “failed” shot from a gun to suddenly 
retroactively hit, a “successful” roll to convince authorities has now put you in suspicion, or a “successful” destructive blast to 
suddenly undo everything. Any items you gained directly from a Bluffed roll vanish at the end of the scene. Gain or grant +1D the 
next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Ace Reaction, Short Range 
○​
Whenever you or an ally within range takes an action that requires a roll, you may choose one of the following options: 
■​
You may take sin equal to the number of dice in the roll to flatten the entire roll into a single dice. On a failure, every dice 
in the roll counts as a failure. On a success, every die in the roll counts as a success and you take stress equal to the sin 
taken as your body outperforms itself. There is no medium result; the action now succeeds or fails in an utterly 
spectacular and unlikely fashion. 
■​
You may take 1d3 sin to turn the gradient of a risk die into a harsh divide. Anything 3 and below counts as a 1, and 
anything 4 and above counts as a 6. Either result is exceptional and exaggerated, either remarkably lucky or terribly 
unlucky.  
 
 


 
 
 
LUDO 
All the world’s a game. 
 
Fact: The insistence of simulated reality by Ludo users has sparked a great discussion among the philosophical minds of TEMERITY as to 
whether the world really is fictional. If so, this would greatly expand TEMERITY experiments beyond restrictive moral guidelines. Further research 
and deliberation pending. 
 
Passive: Stack 
●​
You gain the ability to “break” items and up to PSYCHE-meter-sized perfect cubes of structures in a way that turns them into small 
centimeter-wide floating square Icons, which can be picked up by you or an ally like cards. You and your allies may only possess up to 
3*CAT of these cards at any time. Whenever an Icon is purposefully deployed to any surface, it immediately manifests back into its original 
form as an object, aligning itself in a perfect meter-sized grid onto the surface it touched. Icons that are not picked up vanish at the end of 
the scene. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Tech Scene, Charm, Self 
○​
For the duration while rolling to perform movement, you may perform one of the following input combinations as an action, rolling 
an appropriate skill with +1D: 
■​
Abuse animation collision to bunnyhop across a surface, greatly and rapidly increasing your speed or jump height. 
■​
Abuse animation velocity to alter the direction (but not amount) of your velocity in midair. 
■​
Abuse animation positioning to attack a surface, moving you a short distance in a chosen direction like a teleport. 
■​
Abuse animation triggers to rubberband your movement back in time up to a CAT distance. 
On a failed skill roll to perform an input combination, you are launched a short distance in a random direction and immediately 
take 1d3+1 stress as the collision detection system lashes out at you. If this causes an injury, take the //ERROR hook. 
●​
Hitscan Instant, Long Range 
○​
As part of any attack, you may spend a PSYCHE burst to cause it to happen in a snap, ignoring the velocity and gravity of any 
projectile or speed of any blade and registering an attack against the target’s physical hitbox instantly. If you do so, the target does 
not take visual or physical damage from the attack, instead depleting their abstract “talisman” directly. They become vaguely 
aware of this effect and are aware they have taken damage but can rarely tell the true nature of it. For the rest of the scene, their 
“health bar” appears in your vision, hovering over their head. 
●​
Collision Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
You shove the physical form of an up to CAT sized creature or object you can touch into another surface, which can include 
yourself, clipping the target’s form into it. Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success.  
■​
If the target is another creature, they immediately take stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of 
successes rolled. 
■​
If the target is yourself, on at least one success you may clip through up to PSYCHE meters of material this scene. 
■​
On a failure targeting yourself, or on a success when targeting another creature, the target has a 50/50 chance of being 
clipped randomly through the surface to a safe area beneath, above, or through it, or becoming stuck in the surface. Gain 
or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Craft Instant, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Target up to 9 Icons of raw or basic material you possess (such as wood, concrete, iron scraps, a bottle of fluid, plastic waste, 
computer parts, a broken gun etc), arranging them in a 3-3 grid on a flat surface within range in a way roughly symbolic of the 
desired end product. Then, roll PSYCHE. You may instantly combine the Icons in a way that could logically produce up to 
PSYCHE+1 KP worth of items from the materials. For every success, you may increase the complexity of the item produced: 
■​
0: Basic, primitive items. Simple tools, blades, butchering meat, carving wood, bending metal, writing words, etc 
■​
1: Slightly complex or labor-intensive items. Weaving from plant fibres, basic chemistry, refining or smelting materials, etc 
■​
2: Highly complex or manufactured items. Guns, synthetic chemical formulas, working electronics, etc 
■​
3: Extremely delicate or artisanal items. Cutting gems, creating paintings, scientific instruments, supernatural items, etc 
You may choose to purposefully roll no successes to avoid spending a psyche burst and create a basic primitive item. This can 
easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Avatar Passive, Self 
○​
Stress no longer physically registers on your body, instead reducing a visible UI “health bar” in the corner of your vision. Afflictions 
and hooks may bypass this effect and display “status effects” in the corner of your vision. Whenever you kill a creature, the 
physical body of the victim vanishes into a cloud of pixels, leaving behind any notable items on their person as the floating Icons 
from your passive. When you die, the same effect applies to you. If you sin overflow to defy fate and successfully resist, take the 
//ERROR hook and “respawn” where the current scene began, or the nearest safe location to it within a long distance. 
 
//ERROR Hook: 
When this hook fills, your game starts to break down around you, immediately forcing you to roll on the following list: 
1.​ The game crashes. Your body immediately freezes in place in the exact position you are in, becoming a fully visible yet intangible and 
visually distorted remnant of your presence. At the end of the scene your remnant vanishes and you respawn at the location where you 
entered the investigation area. 
2.​ You fall through the map. Unless you or an ally has some way of retrieving you or teleporting you back, you fall endlessly into an empty 
skybox until you willingly suffer 1d6+1 stress to respawn on solid ground exactly above where you fell. 
3.​ Your vision lags. You can only see time in up to PSYCHE*10 frames per second, which decreases drastically when you witness large 
amounts of fine physics simulation. 
4.​ Textures corrupt. The visual texture of parts of the environment and some objects or creatures is randomly replaced with a tiling half-meter 
purple and black checker grid that makes all rolls to use vision hard or even impossible in certain situations as it strains your eyes. 
5.​ Errors pop up. Once per pressure the admin may choose to have an annoyingly inconvenient UI window in your vision display an error 
and block an input, increasing the difficulty or risk of your current roll.  
6.​ Lag momentarily spikes. The next time you make violent contact with an entity, object, or the environment all sensory input freezes for 1d6 
half-seconds.  
 
 


 
SCORE 
Underdogs rise up. 
 
Fact: Physical violence and confrontation between opposing non-allied Score users is nearly 87.3% higher than other competitions between 
similar blasphemy users.  
 
Passive: Ball 
●​
Designate a physical sport of your choice that requires a ball or struck object to function. Once per scene you may touch an up to CAT 
sized object. For the scene, it moves and acts like the sports object of your choice for you, including changing its aerodynamics and 
“hitbox”, but remains normal in weight and shape for everyone else. If necessary, you may also designate an object as any sort of required 
club or bat in the same way. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Goal Scene, Charm, CAT Range 
○​
For the duration, no matter how you throw, kick, or otherwise accelerate a target, it will always touch where you are aiming if the 
destination is something you can see within a CAT distance. It may still deal no stress or slashes, or fail in some way on a failed 
roll, but it will always at least touch the destination. If the target ever leaves a CAT distance from you, this effect ends. 
●​
Strike Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Whenever you witness or uncover evidence of a purposefully deceitful, underhanded, or dirty tactic or trick, you may cause a 
psychic whistle to ring out from you. Any being that can hear the whistle becomes instantly aware of the exact nature of the foul 
play (according to your knowledge), bypassing any personal opinion they have of any involved parties. This can easily affect the 
parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
■​
You may roll PSYCHE to attempt to forge a false condemnation. On at least one success, any being that hears the whistle 
becomes aware of the narrative as normal. On a failure, they become aware of you lie and you take 1d3 sin. 
●​
Field Scene, Transmute, CAT Distance 
○​
An up to CAT sized distance around you harmlessly warps, flattens, and refines into a level playing field of a sport of your choice. 
This can include moving buildings out of the way, flattening terrain, hiding obstructions, summoning lights, and creating any props 
for the sport such as posts and baskets. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an 
ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Cheer Scene, Charm, CAT Area 
○​
Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On at least one success, for the duration any mundane humans 
within the area become the Audience and gain the ability to see supernatural phenomena. Any hostile creatures in the scene 
become unable to harm the Audience in any way, as the spotlight falls entirely upon the combatants. Actions from allies that 
successfully impress the Audience grant +1D to the next ally, and actions from enemies that impress the audience subtract 1 from 
the next risk roll. The inverse is true as well; actions from allies that disappoint the audience grant -1D, and actions from enemies 
that disappoint the audience add 1 to the next risk roll. 
●​
Pass Instant, CAT Range 
○​
As an action, you may attempt to throw or pass the Ball from your passive to an ally, or have them throw or pass it to you. This can 
include bound sins, immaterial Jaunt users, and other such incarnations of your allies. Whoever catches the Ball may roll 
PSYCHE, instantly spending a psyche burst and swapping positions with the other on at least one success.This can easily affect 
the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
BROTHER 
Truth is often stranger than fiction. 
 
Fact: The House has shown further deterioration, to the alarm of AUTHORITY. Measures are being taken to imprison and reunite the fleeting 
Fragments of the House, results pending. 
 
Passive: Sisarus 
●​
Choose a single allied exorcist as a Sibling. Both you and your Sibling gain the ability to take the Seed hook to boost the CAT of a power 
by +2. You may only boost your Brother powers this way, and your Sibling must choose a single blasphemy to boost per mission. If any 
exorcists possess the Brother or Sister blasphemy, you are forced to choose them as your Sibling and they may only boost their Brother or 
Sister powers this way. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Sigh Instant, CAT Range 
○​
As an action, release a sigh that mingles with the wind in the leaves. Creatures with a combined CAT equal to yours are suddenly 
struck by an immense sense of calm and relaxation, and their mind fogs over. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE and answer 
the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on success. 
■​
Are you near a source of flowering or beautiful foliage? 
■​
Are all of your targets in a non-combative state? 
All targets enter a half-sleeping trance for a number of minutes equal to the number of successes + ½ CAT, and roots and vines 
curl around their bodies. On at least one “yes” answer any mundane target is put completely to sleep and cocooned within the 
foliage entirely. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Revelation Passive, Curse, Self 
○​
You feel the tendrils curling around the fabric of your mind, glowing with soft secrets. You  may take the Seed hook at any time to 
gain extremely brief, shattered glimpses of all information within the investigation area, rolling PSYCHE. Most information that 
floods your mind is completely nonsensical and irrelevant to your mission (deli receipts from four days ago, the color of leaves on 
a west-facing vine, how many birds are in the sky right now), but you may gain singular snapshot of a piece of information related 
to the sin’s host (their favourite bookshop, the age at which they got a scar, their highest grade in high school) for every success 
rolled, with its specificity increasing with your CAT. Every time you use this power, increase the spread (but not number) of a 
random Seed mutation you have across your body. 
●​
Fruit Instant, Touch 
○​
Gently reach within the mind of another, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a PSYCHE burst on at least one success. On a success, 
you may choose a memory of theirs according to your CAT: 
■​
1-2: Extremely recent or surface thoughts, willingly offered memories.  
■​
3-4: Deeper memories, subconscious thought, unwilling memories. 
■​
5+: Core memories, suppressed or nearly forgotten memories, extreme emotional baggage. 
On a success the memory is pulled from the target’s mind, erasing it, becoming a softly glowing fruit in your palm whose flavour 
aligns with the nature of the memory. On a failure, the target’s psyche lashes back at you, dealing 1d3 stress and giving you the 
Seed hook. The first being to take a bite of or consume the fruit gains the memory as if it was their own and was fresh in their 
mind. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect.  
●​
Twist Scene, Transmute, Touch 
○​
For the duration, foliage within the area blooms and mutates at an aggressive rate. Wherever you step, there is always a stable 
platform of wood or plant tendrils, which can include walking midair and across otherwise hazardous terrain. Once while this effect 
is active, you can purposefully shape these plants into a permanent CAT-sized weaving construct, ending the effect. You may 
have these plants originate from any Seed mutation you have instead of sources around you. 
●​
Roots Scene, Curse, Self 
○​
Choose a single Seed mutation that you or your Sibling has. For the duration, the target may extend tendrils of Brother up to a 
CAT distance away as a mundane service weapon, and regenerate any damage in that location as an action, allowing them to 
negate up to ½ CAT stress per scene taken in that location by regrowing or sacrificing it. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an 
ally takes advantage of this effect. 
■​
Eyes: Brain, eyes, sensory organs, nervous system. 
■​
Jaw: Neck, including reattaching or regrowing your entire head. 
■​
Back or Chest: Torso, lungs, heart, organs. 
■​
Skin, Hair, or Legs: Flaying, physical cuts and punctures to the skin, legs, feet. 
■​
Arms or Hands: Shoulders, arms, hands. 
When this effect ends, the Seed mutation spread (but not number) increases across the chosen location drastically. 
 
Seed Hook: 
The curling vines of Brother begin to bloom within your mind. Every time you gain this hook, you do not slash it but instead gain another, separate 
hook. When this hook fills, a Seed has been fully planted.  
●​
All Seeds vanish during a rest or with any effect that can wipe your memory.  
●​
The next time you suffer an injury or instant death while you possess a Seed within your mind, roll on the Sin Mark location table for each 
seed you possess. The resulting location partially mutates into a coil of plant-like vines, like a rash.  
●​
You gain the ability to completely regenerate any mutated feature at the end of a mission, but you can no longer gain the benefits of any 
Sin Marks in that location. The more times you roll on the same location, the more completely it is replaced with the vines and the more 
inhuman it appears and acts. 
●​
Once you have a number of these mutations equal to your Sin Cap, the next time you suffer instant death you transmute into a twisted, 
knotted tree composed of several weaving trunks, with star-like fruit glowing gently on your branches. Any that consume the fruit gain the 
Brother blasphemy with no sin cap reduction and one of your memories of your choice.
 
 


 
FATHER 
Keep it together. Keep it together. KEEP IT TOGETHER. 
 
Fact: The House has gone silent. No further advising has been observed as of December 14th, ████. All attempts to force communication have 
proven futile. This information is classified under ████████, and has been laced with a memetic tracking agent. Unauthorized viewership will 
result in immed_ 
 
This blasphemy may only be taken during character creation. 
 
Passive: Nail 
●​
You possess seven Nails pierced throughout your body: one in your heart, one in your head, two in your arms, two in your legs, and one 
between your shoulderblades. Several of your Father powers may require a Nail to function. You may extract a Nail from your body at any 
time to morph that part of your body into an animalistic and bestial form: 
○​
Heart: Instincts, reflexes, organs, lungs. Extracting this Nail and failing to roll PSYCHE to control it forces a primal hunger into 
your mind whenever you view a prey animal. 
○​
Head: Eyes, gills, jaw, throat sac, sensory organs, venom. Extracting this Nail and failing to roll PSYCHE to control it renders you 
unintelligent and animalistic for the scene or until an ally can implant the Nail back in. 
○​
Arm: Arms, tentacles, wings, talons. Extracting both can allow for up to PSYCHE extra pairs of arm appendages. 
○​
Leg: Legs, tentacles. Extracting both allows a centaur-like form and/or up to PSYCHE extra pairs of leg appendages. 
○​
Spine: Spine, sails, a tail, a shell. Extracting this nail can allow an elongated body or stretched spine/neck. 
These unanchored features can act separately from your will on instinct, but act with +1D on all actions when acting on their own. You 
must roll PSYCHE 1/scene to reign them into your control, doing so on at least one success. Any destroyed Nails regenerate at the end of 
a rest. Your Unravel talisman has slashes equal to the number of missing Nails in your body at all times. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Surrogate Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
Target a human within range that trusts you. Then, stab them with one of your Nails and roll PSYCHE. On a success, up to CAT•2 
other humans within a long distance are pulled towards and merged with your target, creating one bestial Amalgam that is 
submissive to you with a talisman equal to the number of targets +PSYCHE. The amalgam is unintelligent, has physical strength 
equal to your CAT, and is utterly horrifying to mundane humans that can see it. This amalgam lasts until it is killed or you extract 
the Nail, rolling PSYCHE. On a success, the original human that trusted you survives. On a failure, they all die and melt into a 
puddle of ooze. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Adopt ½ CAT Scenes, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Target the corpse of a mundane human within range. Then, stab a nail into their corpse in the same place you took it out of your 
body. They immediately regenerate flesh and stitch together injuries, awakening as a naive and eager childlike figure in awe of 
you. They possess a talisman with only one slash, and if the Nail is removed from them they will degrade over the course of ½ 
CAT scenes into a lycanthropic animal form before perishing. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the 
next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Family Scene, Charm, Self 
○​
For the duration of this ability, wildlife and living things of any sort will consider you one of their own and obey basic commands to 
the best of their ability, regardless of species. Once during this power you may stab a Nail into one such creature, immediately 
ending the effect and dispersing the wildlife but granting you the ability to speak fluently with the stabbed creature, who becomes 
amicable to you. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Patriarch Until Rest, Transmute, Curse 
○​
As part of this power, spend any amount of psyche bursts. You violently rip out and destroy an equal number of Nails in your body, 
purposefully releasing your animalistic tendencies. For every Nail you destroy, you gain +1D to the ensuing PSYCHE check to 
control your body, to a maximum of PSYCHE extra dice. For the duration, all locations with destroyed Nails no longer require a 
1/scene PSYCHE check to control. You may make a single supernatural attack per destroyed Nail until the end of the duration. 
●​
Kin Psyche, Curse, Long Range 
○​
As an action, fire a Nail from your body to implant into a victim within range. Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one 
success. On a success, the Nail embeds itself into your victim in the same place you fired it from, mutating them horribly. This 
mutation will act out against their wielder immediately by biting, strangling, twisting, or otherwise attempting to free itself from 
them. You may deal 1 stress or slash on their talisman per round as a free action, up to PSYCHE+1 stress or slashes total. If they 
manage to pull out the Nail, this effect ends and the Nail is destroyed. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant 
+1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
Unravel Talisman 
Father is the only one holding this Body together. This talisman always has slashes on it equal to the number of missing Nails in your body. 
●​
1-2: The beast roils in your gut. 1/scene the Admin may give you a distinct opportunity to give in to your bestial nature, at the direct cost of 
making your immediate situation much worse. If you do, immediately gain 1 XP. 
●​
3-4: It is crawling up your throat now, low and growling. The Admin may now force you to give into your bestial nature 1/rest, forcing a risk 
roll. 
●​
5-6: It slithers behind your fangs. The Admin may, 1/scene, take complete control of one thing you say or do as Father speaks through 
you. 
●​
7+. Your body unravels completely, and you become a shifting mound of bestial features, blood, iron, and bone. What little sentience you 
possess allows you to reform yourself at the end of the next rest, or if an ally manages to implant a Nail back into your head. You grow 1/2 
CAT steps in size and strength and gain +1D on mindless or feral actions.
 
 


 
SISTER 
So alone, in such a crowd. 
 
For all of humanity that remains, we are CAIN. Our cause, since the wetting of the first rock, was the extermination of Sin and 
the continued sanctity of the Veil. This cause was the beating heart of our organization for nearly two thousand years. 
 
A permanent solution to our cause has been advised by the House. Henceforth, CAIN shall endeavour to cleanse the earth of 
humanity. 
 
.Donec macula abluatur.. 
 
There shall be no further communication. 
 
Passive: Darling 
●​
Once per mission you may choose a single willing allied player to have their exorcist become your Sibling. The exorcist does not need to 
be willing, just the player. A parasitic insect then emerges from your mouth to burrow into their body and settle around their sinseed, 
leaving no marks behind but causing immense pain. Your Sibling gains the Inheritance talisman.    
 Your character is never consciously aware of casting any Sister powers. You are.. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Parliament Scene, Summon, CAT Distance 
○​
Sister calls forth your Siblings to seep from the environment around you. Choose one of the following options: 
■​
Seethe. Scuttling ground insects pour from cracks and seams and holes. They may carpet the ground, walls, and any 
other surface within range including swarming other creatures. All ground movement within range becomes more difficult 
for non-allied creatures, and non-sealed clothes let bugs under onto the skin. 
■​
Writhe. Buzzing air insects wriggle through gaps and open areas, choking the air. All vision and hearing within range 
becomes more difficult, breathing without a mask or covering causes insects to crawl into mouths and ears. 
You may direct where the swarm congregates and its rough formation, but cannot make specific orders. This can easily affect the 
parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Usurp Instant, Short Range 
○​
Sister bursts from your mouth, hungry and lonely, to latch onto a victim within range. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE and 
answer the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a psyche burst on success. 
■​
Does the target dismiss you as a threat? 
■​
Does the target possess an open wound? 
On a success, the target takes stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of successes rolled. On at least one “yes” 
answer, you also drain the target of hydration and vital fluids, rendering them thin and skeletal. This can easily affect the 
parameters of a roll. 
●​
Fiat Instant, Curse, CAT Range 
○​
Sister graciously lets them hear your beautiful voice. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE and answer the following questions, 
gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on success. 
■​
Are you engaged in a distracting conversation with the target/s? 
■​
Is the target obviously troubled or uneasy in some way? 
On at least one success, you may implant a single subliminal sentence of your choice into their mind while speaking. They will 
treat this sentence as their own, conscious thought, fully believe any information it contains, and have it stick in their mind for a 
number of scenes equal to CAT. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally 
takes advantage of this effect.  
●​
Highness Scene, Charm, Self 
○​
