Sister shows them who you are, truly, inside. For the duration, you emit a pheromone that causes any mundane creatures 
(including animals) that interact with you will instinctually place you within one of the following roles of your choice: 
■​
The highest level of authority conceivable in their mind. Whether this is a parental figure, pack leader, political figure, 
cultural celebrity, religious icon, or other such authority is up to the perception of the target.  
■​
The highest level of innocence conceivable in their mind. Whether this is a childlike figure, naive optimism, a lower social 
strata, foreigner ignorance, or other such excuses is up to the perception of the target. 
You gain no special insight into their perception of you, but you gain +1D or a reduction in difficulty on all social roles interacting 
with them so long as you act in a way fitting to their perception. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D 
the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Knight Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
Sister sanctifies a creature within range, granting them the highest honor as a parasite emerges from your touch to burrow into 
their body. Roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On a success, choose one large facet of your 
personality such as your vocabulary, attitude, opinion on a topic, etc. The target’s personality will slowly become overwritten by 
your chosen facet over the course of a tick of pressure, which may cause immense confusion in others if it is an extremely severe 
change. If they are driven to question their new personality too hard by you or others, you must roll PSYCHE to maintain this 
effect. 
 
Inheritance Talisman: 
Gaining this Talisman is permanent, and you may never remove it. This 8-slash talisman grants you a single sin mark of your choice, without 
needing to sin overflow and without reducing your sin cap. Every time you gain sin of any amount, slash this talisman. Each slash temporarily turns 
a random feature into a visually conspicuous form as your insectoid parasite gorges on your sinseed. A repeated mutation means it grows more 
intense and noticeable. 
1.​ Your eyes and ears 
2.​ Your mouth and neck 
3.​ Your joints and spine 
4.​ Your waist and torso 
5.​ Your arms and legs 
6.​ Your face and scalp/hair 
When you fully slash this talisman, empty it and choose one of your mutations to become permanent. Every time you sin overflow, you are forced 
to Resist without rolling, starting all future missions by slashing this talisman +2 times. If you ever start a mission with this talisman full, your body 
violently rejects the parasite, crippling your sinseed and reducing all of your mutations into amputated scar tissue, though you retain the free 
sinmark. Immediately reduce your maximum psyche bursts by 1 and you may no longer become a Sibling. Whoever gave you this talisman 
permanently increases their psyche burst maximum by 1, to a maximum of PSYCHE extra burst cap. 
 
 


 
VAST 
Reach out to the horizon. You’ll never reach it. 
 
Fact: The House showing such direct engagement in the activities and powers of exorcists is perplexing given their general lack of interest in 
interacting with AUTHORITY and TEMERITY. Forceful communion via Vast user conduits is being developed. 
 
Passive: House 
●​
Whenever you look to the horizon or skyline of any location, the House will always be there, at the edges, or just far away enough that you 
can barely see it. Once per mission you may forcefully pin the House in place an extreme distance away, turning it into a CAT-floor building 
that always seems to loom over you regardless of its actual height. The rules for your House are detailed below. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Tower Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
In a blink, your patron House flickers into view behind you as an endlessly tall spire. It looms, large and threatening, throwing a 
crushing weight against any enemies who view it. Roll PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On at 
least one success, up to CAT enemies of your choice become terrified of the looming tower and cower, whimpering in its shadow, 
which ignores time of day and normal light sources. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time 
you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Dungeon Scene, Transmute, CAT+2 Area 
○​
Choose an up to CAT+2 sized open area or passageway (such as a hallway, warehouse, or stairwell). For the duration, you and 
any creatures within the area become isolated as the area stretches on infinitely into a maze. Any creatures who enter from the 
“edges” of the area are similarly isolated as they are an “infinite" distance away. This can easily change the parameters of a roll. 
●​
Stairwell Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature within range, causing them to trip and fall into the House. When you use this power, roll PSYCHE and answer 
the following questions, gaining +1D for each ‘yes’ answer, only spending a Psyche Burst on success. 
■​
Are you beneath the open sky? 
■​
Are you in a very elevated position? 
On at least one success, the target takes stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of successes rolled, and 
momentarily vanishes with the creak of a door as they tumble through an endless concrete chute within the House, its psychic 
force crushing at their mind as it whirls by them. Once the creature returns, they hit the ground with just less than terminal velocity. 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Skylight Scene, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
Target an up to CAT sized area of sky or ceiling within range. The veil immediately crumbles inward and is torn away, opening up 
directly to a dark and cloudy sky. Weather within the area becomes extremely adverse. Roll PSYCHE, choosing a number of the 
following options equal to the number of successes +1: 
■​
Howl: Intense winds howl out from the sky, making hearing difficult and throwing light objects around. 
■​
Pour: Rain, hail, or snow rapidly begins to fill the room, which will flood if there is nowhere for it to run. 
■​
Rumble: Thunder occasionally bursts out from the sky, intense enough to cause a minor localized earthquake.  
■​
Blind: Thick fog obscures the room, to the point you can barely see a few feet in front of your face. 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Door Instant, Transmute, Touch 
○​
Once per mission you may knock on a closed, non-windowed door within range. Bright white lights begin to spill from under the 
door, and the shadow of someone standing behind it appears. You hear a voice, strange and fractured and metaphorical, asking 
you what you wish of the House. Roll PSYCHE, choosing a number of options equal to the number of successes: 
■​
Safety. In exchange for all of your ally’s rest dice during the next rest, the door will grant you access to perfect, absolute 
safety and isolation in a room within the House. Any ally may choose not to forfeit their rest dice, but cannot enter the 
saferoom if they do not. No uninvited entity can detect you, see you, or follow you through this door, and all allies are 
effectively immortal while within it, their physiology paused in time.  
■​
Power. In exchange for your dearest memory, you and all allies may immediately regain all bursts, as well as one burst 
past your maximum. You permanently lose access to the memory, and your XP cap for gaining advancements doubles 
until the end of the mission, at which point you lose half of your current XP and your XP cap returns to normal. 
■​
Truth. In exchange for your deepest secret, you may ask the House a single yes or no question. The door then opens into 
a window through which you witness the answer to your question. The Admin may choose a single ally to tell your secret 
to, though they do not have to tell you who they told. If the House cannot or will not answer, the Admin must simply give a 
one-word hint instead of the full secret. 
 
The House 
The House is a large building or tower of some kind, always looming over you from the skyline or horizon. Unless you anchor it in place, it is 
impossible to actually approach the House in any way other than your Vast powers. Its properties are as follows: 
●​
The area within the House is infinite, but grows more hostile and nonsensical the farther you depart from the front door. It is always 
brutalist and minimal in its appearance, but its layout is otherwise mutable and shifting. 
●​
Falling does not kill you, but may separate you from allies irrecoverably. Any creature that becomes truly lost within this megastructure 
must take an injury or slash half of their execution talisman to be thrown out of a random public door within the investigation area onto the 
street. 
●​
While within the House, time begins to pass in strange ways. No being within the House must eat, drink, or sleep. There is a 50/50 chance 
upon exiting the House that anywhere from half to double the time spent in the House has passed outside. 
●​
There is a rare chance that doors within the House lead to unused or rarely-used doors within the investigation area. Somewhere, a 
horizon’s distance from the front door, is a closed door that allows direct communion with the House itself. 
●​
Any creature within the house feels an immense pressure that makes it difficult to think clearly, completely disabling any psychic powers. 
Memories within the House grow hazy after leaving. 
●​
Footsteps do not make sound here. Your ears are ringing. 
●​
The air feels heavy. Your breath leaves mist in the air. 
●​
You are not alone in the House. 
 
 


 
IRIS 
Turn a blind eye, and open one in its place. 
 
Fact: Iris users must be provided with opaque, full skin cover body suits while within any facility that contains ALET or higher level memetic 
hazards. 
 
Passive: Pantopticon 
●​
You constantly see in a 360 degree field of vision around yourself, and may manifest up to CAT*2 extra eyes across your body at will. You 
cannot close your eyes, avert your gaze, blink, or otherwise avoid seeing something unless it is visually obstructed from you. Once per 
scene you may gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Curtain Scene, Short Range 
○​
You summon an ethereal, stationary regular plane up to CAT in size within range facing in a direction of your choice.  
■​
You may “exclude” anything behind the plane from anything in front of the plane, hiding its appearance, grace signature, 
and any sensory information it gives off other than sound. 
■​
You may alter the appearance of anything behind the plane (making a door appear closed, making a dead body appear, 
making it look like it is raining, etc), but those alterations are only visible from the front of the plane 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect 
●​
Socket Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
As an action, choose a point you can touch, including in midair. You may pluck out one of your eyes, placing it at the point and 
imbuing it with up to PSYCHE separate senses. Each instance takes 1/PSYCHE of a chosen sense with it, proportionally muffling 
or muddling it for your body. 
■​
Sight in a static 90° angle cone.  
■​
Sound within normal hearing range. 
■​
Scent and Taste in a ½ CAT distance. 
■​
Touch and Temperature in a ½ CAT distance. 
■​
Grace in a ½ CAT distance. 
You must physically return to the location where you deposited your senses to regain them. If this becomes impossible, the eye is 
lost and your chosen sense returns in a blurry, muffled way that grants -2D to using it until the end of the next rest. 
●​
Drink Scene, Charm, CAT Distance 
○​
For the duration, you must ramp up the sensitivity of at least 4-PSYCHE senses of your choice to immense levels, allowing you to 
perceive extremely minute details within range. While a sense is extended, you must roll PSYCHE when attempting to filter out or 
tune into a specific type of sensation. Any form of intense or extreme sensation you are enhancing makes you take 1d6 stress and 
immediately ends this effect. You may end this effect early by taking the Overstimulation hook. This can easily affect the 
parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Mirage Passive, CAT Distance 
○​
When using a free generic PSYCHE roll to create minor sensory effects within range, you may have them become extremely 
specific, potent, and sustained. Any sensory illusion you produce this way can last up to CAT*5 seconds, be up to CAT in size, and 
can be made of PSYCHE separate senses. 
■​
Light and Fog 
■​
Sound and Vibration 
■​
Scent and Taste 
■​
Touch and Temperature 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Open Scene, Curse, Short Range 
○​
You offload your curse of perception to a target within range, rolling PSYCHE if they are unwilling, only spending a psyche burst 
on at least one success. On a success, you lose your Panopticon passive and transfer it to the target, causing eyes to sprout 
along their body and overwhelming them with sensory information. Taking advantage of their disorientation and warped 
proprioception grants +1D to any action made to do so once per round. If the target is knocked unconscious or slain, your 
perception violently returns to you and you take the Overstimulation hook as you experience the residuals of their last sensations. 
 
Overstimulation Hook: 
It’s too much, too loud, too bright, too strong, too sharp. When this hook fills, you immediately sprout CAT*4 extra eyes and other sensory organs 
somewhere on your body and lose the ability to orient your body around your senses correctly, completely stripping you of proprioception until the 
end of the next scene. You roll -2D on all actions requiring movement or fine coordination, and can barely make out any specific sensory 
information beyond the deafening overload.  
 
After this effect ends, roll PSYCHE. On a failure, choose one sense to muddle until the end of the mission. Choosing an option twice completely 
removes it from you, removing it as an option. 
●​
Sight (blurry and bright) 
●​
Sound (muffled and ringing) 
●​
Taste and Smell (dull and acrid) 
●​
Touch and Temperature (cold pins and needles) 
 
 


 
CRAWL 
The world moves on, with or without you. 
 
Fact: The law of large numbers dictates that human suffering will forever be a concrete inevitably, making CAIN an essential part of a functioning 
society.  
 
Passive: Inevitable 
●​
As long as you are walking at a casual pace, most mundane obstacles will either open to let you through, or break and shatter to allow you 
through otherwise. If these obstacles include things that attack or are actively hostile, you take -1 stress from them. 
 
 Abilities 
●​
Pursue Instant, Curse, Extreme Range 
○​
Choose a target within range you can see, rolling PSYCHE if they are unwilling, only spending a burst on at least one success. On 
a success, your maximum speed increases to match theirs exactly for the duration, and any effect they cause that would knock 
you back, halt you, or restrain you automatically fails. Any time you move you must approach the target to the best of your ability. 
As soon as you touch the target, this effect ends. 
●​
Clock Instant, Curse, Investigation Area 
○​
Speak aloud the names of up to CAT creatures within the investigation area if you know them. You may count up to CAT humans 
as one creature. Then roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a success, set a precise timer that is at 
least 4-½ CAT time in length, and specify a task. The target becomes immediately aware of the task and timer, but not necessarily 
of any context. If the target fails the task, you must immediately deal stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of 
successes, which is nearly fatal for most mundane creatures. 
●​
Routine Scene, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
All mundane creatures within a CAT sized area will immediately accept and move on from any form of event or tragedy they 
perceive after initially reacting to it, and will cease caring about anything that does not immediately concern their own life 
regardless of intensity. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes 
advantage of this effect. 
●​
Wheel Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
Target an up to CAT sized object, surface, or creature within range, rolling PSYCHE. On a success, the target instantly ages up to 
CAT^2 years (but does not “grow up”, simply aging their cells), ^3 instead if it is an object. This effect does not deal stress or 
slashes to talismans, but may break structures or reduce objects to piles of rust or rot if they are in an area exposed to the 
elements. 
●​
Emanate Scene, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Target up to CAT creatures within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a success, the closer 
they are to you the more panic, fatigue and weight their bodies are subjected to for the duration. While within an arms reach, this 
effect maxes out at total exhaustion, absolute terror, and up to PSYCHE times their own body weight bearing down on them. They 
are constantly aware of your distance from them, but not direction. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant 
+1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
HAND 
Is your hand your hand? 
 
Fact: Contact with the palm of a Hand user is strictly forbidden by handler personnel. This especially includes reported “high fives”. 
 
Passive: Grasp 
●​
1/scene you may extend your range of touch out to a CAT distance for a single action, gaining more strength the farther you can “reach” 
against the “smaller” objects, by interacting with distant things as if they were smaller. 
○​
1-2: 10 lbs, Break glass, punch humans, destroy furniture. 
○​
3-4: 500 pounds, Bend metal, squish people, topple trees. 
○​
5: 1 ton, Crush buildings, flatten cars, flick away massive creatures. 
Your arms and hands are effectively indestructible, and if torn off regenerate in nearly an instant. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Clap Instant, Short Distance 
○​
As an action, target two creatures and/or objects within range, which can include yourself. As long as their size CAT is within 
PSYCHE steps of each other, you may instantly swap their positions and directions, rolling PSYCHE if any target is unwilling and 
only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next 
time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Backhand Instant, Touch 
○​
Target an up to CAT sized creature or object within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a 
success, the target is forcefully blasted directly away from you through any obstructions up to a CAT+1 distance away, including 
upwards and downwards. This movement is harmless and irresistible, effectively rendering the target invulnerable and limp for the 
duration of the movement. 
●​
Point Instant, CAT Distance 
○​
Target a creature, object, or point within range. Then, roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a success, 
choose one of the following, 
■​
The attention and view of every creature within range (including exorcists’ and your own) is suddenly snapped towards the 
target. 
■​
The orientation of movable or moving objects within range is suddenly snapped towards the target.  
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Snap Instant, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature or up to CAT sized object within range. The does one of the following: 
■​
Violently pops and ruptures. 
■​
Violently opens or closes 
■​
Abruptly turns on, triggers, or starts in the way it was intended to. 
■​
Abruptly turns off, fizzles out, or stops completely. 
If the target is an object, if its size CAT is lower than yours it does not cost a burst to target. If the target is a creature, roll 
PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success and dealing stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of 
successes. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Thumb Scene, Short Range 
○​
Target a creature, area, or object in range with a CAT up to yours, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. 
On a success, choose one of the following gestures: 
■​
Up: Humans within a long range of the target vouch for, adore, and approve of the target for the duration unflinchingly. 
■​
Middle: Humans within a long distance of the target grow bored of, ignore, or grow ignorant of the target for the duration. 
■​
Down: Humans within a long range of the target violently condemn, disapprove of, and avoid the target for the duration.  
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
FOIL 
You’re your own worst enemy. 
 
Fact: Do not for any reason attempt to look directly at the reflection of a Foil user. Your curiosity is not worth what it will do to you. 
 
Passive: Negative 
●​
You possess a hostile supernatural entity in place of your reflection that mundane creatures cannot perceive. It knows you, and hates you. 
 
This entity is extremely possessive and will actively attack and harm any other entity within your mind, such as Whisper’s Shadow or a 
Bound Sin, killing it, dispersing it, or negating its presence and powers for one scene 1/rest. Additionally, you become immune to any form 
of possession, and anything that attempts to possess you or enter your mind automatically takes 1d3 stress or PSYCHE slashes on their 
talisman as the Foil rips into them. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Peripheral Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
As an action, you may stand before a mirror within range and speak to your Negative. In exchange for a deplorable or evil favour 
and one slash of your Trespass talisman, it will kill one mundane human of your choice without fail as long as you can give it their 
full legal name and a photograph of the target.  
 
After up to 4-PSYCHE scenes, the target is unavoidably killed, though the Foil may not necessarily make it a quiet or stealthy 
death. Roll a fate die. On a PSYCHE or lower on the die, you avoid catastrophe. Higher, and the death was highly public and 
inexplicable, slashing 1d3 Tension. This power does not cost a psyche burst. 
●​
Wraith Instant, Charm, Self 
○​
Target a reflective surface you are able to touch, bringing yourself and up to CAT other creatures into it and vanishing from the 
real world, though you still appear in reflections. Mundane creatures in this world are intangible dark shadows, but supernatural 
creatures are fully cognizant of things moving in this realm, including you.  
 
For every minute you spend in this realm, and every time you fail a roll to remain covert or stealthy, slash your Trespass talisman 
once. It retains slashes between uses of this power.  
●​
Symmetry Scene, Transmute, Long Distance 
○​
As an action, place an up to CAT distance square plane in the air within range, or an up to CAT distance line on a continuous 
surface. Then, choose one side of the plane or line. Everything up to a ½ CAT distance away from the line  on the chosen side is 
mirrored on the other side, either as a reflection or a radial fractal. If this causes a creature to be spliced, erased, or cut off in some 
way, roll PSYCHE for damage, only spending a burst on at least one success. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes 
advantage of this effect. 
●​
Sink Scene, Curse, Short Distance 
○​
As an action, target an up to ½ CAT sized creature or object within a short distance of a reflective surface within range, rolling 
PSYCHE if it is unwilling, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a success, your Negative reaches out from the mirror 
and the target is immediately drawn into it.  
■​
The target now only appears within reflective surfaces, and is intangible to any other creature unless it is supernatural. 
■​
If the target is an object, you may cause it to remain tangible while remaining invisible outside of reflections. 
■​
You may roll PSYCHE to deal stress or slashes on the target’s talisman equal to the number of successes as your 
Negative tears into it. 
●​
Crack Scene, Touch 
○​
Target an up to CAT sized area of a reflective surface within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one 
success. You shatter the surface, even if it could not normally be cracked, trapping your Negative within it.  
 
Every time a creature looks into the surface, they lock eyes with the potentially invisible Negative as it spews hatred and insecurity 
into them subliminally, which they fully believe as their own thoughts and become entranced by when not in immediate danger. 
Any form of loud sound, physical contact, or pain snaps the victim out of this trance. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. 
Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. Once this effect ends, slash your Trespass talisman. 
 
Trespass Talisman: 
An 8-slash talisman that retains slashes between hunts. You do not belong here. When this talisman fills, you immediately take 2 injuries, which 
cannot bring you below 1 injury remaining. Until the end of the next rest, you vanish from the real world and can only appear in reflections, though 
you must still walk through the world normally. When you return, a gruesome crime has been committed with your face.  
 
 


 
SLAG 
Roiling under earth, beating in your chest. 
 
Fact: Increased risk of future volcanic and seismic activity surrounding the deployment of Slag exorcist is purely correlation and is in no way a 
factor to be concerned about while managing exorcist assets. 
 
Passive: Transfer 
●​
By touching two objects, you may perfectly swap their average temperatures, ignoring the “amount” of energy transferred and simply 
changing them holistically. You take an amount of stress by doing so equal to the most intense temperature in the exchange, as following: 
○​
1: Body heat, cool breeze. 
○​
2-3: Direct flame, arctic water. 
○​
4-5: Molten magma, absolute zero. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Sear Scene, Transmute, CAT Area 
○​
As an action, you instantaneously do one one of the following: 
■​
Flash-heat everything in an up to CAT sized area, reducing plant life to cinders and charcoal, melting glass into puddles, 
burning any plastic or chemicals into acrid smoke, and evaporating any water within range. 
■​
Flash-freeze everything in an up to CAT sized area, coating everything in a thin layer of frost. freezing any liquids, turning 
the air incredibly dry, and disabling any electronics within range. 
Creatures are not damaged by this effect, and everything within the area then immediately returns to a normal temperature 
afterwards. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this 
effect. 
●​
Fry Scene, Curse, Self 
○​
You may target yourself as an object with your passive, increasing or decreasing your temperature based on an object or 
substance you touch. You still take the stress as normal based on the extremity of the temperature, but you may now remain that 
temperature and can make mundane attacks utilizing the temperature by rolling PSYCHE; successes rolled this way are capped 
by the amount of stress you took. 
●​
Sink Instant, Transmute, Touch 
○​
As an action, dump an immense amount of thermal energy into a single up to CAT sized object or creature within range. Roll 
PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a success, an explosion occurs within the target rather than outside 
of it, bypassing all armor and absorption effects. This is usually immediately fatal to humans. The target immediately takes stress 
or slashes on its talisman equal to the number of successes. If this kills or destroys the target, it erupts into a flame and burns to 
ash after a few seconds.  
●​
Eruption Scene, Transmute, CAT+2 Range 
○​
Target a creature within range, even if you cannot see them, as long as you know their face. Roll PSYCHE, sending a 
concentrated bolt of white-hot molten matter hurtling like a spear in their direction, piercing through any material obstruction in 
small inch-diameter holes. On a success, you deal stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of successes. On a 
failure, you target the wrong creature and instantly kill a random unknown human instead. 
●​
Engulf Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
Target up to CAT creatures or objects within range. All targets immediately erupt into mundane flames, around the same intensity 
as a weak campfire, even if they normally could not be flammable. If you are able to touch a target with your hand during this 
casting, the intensity increases to a raging bonfire. You may target your own body with this effect, which does not harm you but 
may burn away anything on your person if it is flammable. 
 
You may roll PSYCHE to attempt to increase the temperature of this flame, only spending a burst on at least one success. On a 
success, the flame becomes supernatural, cannot be put out by any means other than completely soaking or submerging a target 
in water or a flame retardant, and deals stress or slashes on a talisman equal to the number of successes rolled once. 
 
 


 
CYCLE 
Disparate orbits, spiraling inwards. Extrapolate, forwards and backwards, ahead and behind. 
 
Fact: The solution to the three body problem, while a nearly insurmountable issue for mathematicians, seems to have developed as a sort of 
instinctual response in those that display a Cycle sinseed. Organic computation methods are still under heavy research. 
 
Passive: Cardinal 
●​
You are always constantly aware of your exact speed, pitch, yaw, tilt, and compass direction, regardless of if you can see the outside world 
or not, and regardless of anything in the environment obscuring observation of those things. You are also extremely susceptible to motion 
sickness. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Revolution Scene, Short Range 
○​
Target moving or spinning objects or creatures with a combined CAT equal to yours within range. If any targets are unwilling, roll 
PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on a success. On a success, for the duration you may create up to CAT points of 
immense virtual mass anywhere within range that the targets and other points of virtual mass become attracted to in a stable orbit; 
all targets will maintain their velocity and trajectory regardless of obstacles, including tunneling through the ground and breaking 
through barriers. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of 
this effect. 
●​
Gravity Unique, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Once per mission, target up to PSYCHE+1 individual creatures within range. Choose any number of scenes up to CAT, at least 
one scene. All targets are immediately and inextricably destined to meet or touch (your choice) at the end of the duration, as 
reality slides around to accommodate their arrival. You have no control over where or in what conditions this meeting occurs, and 
as soon as the meeting happens all targets lose their plot armor, and may immediately perish if this protection negates a 
significant threat. 
●​
Horizon Instant, Short Range 
○​
You create a brief point of immeasurable virtual mass within range near up to CAT other creatures, rolling PSYCHE and only 
spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On a success, you warp or stretch their physical features painfully around the 
orbit of the point, tearing off any loose items or clothes and ripping any held items from their hands. They immediately take stress 
or slashes on their talisman equal to the number of successes. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D 
the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Density Scene, CAT Range 
○​
You may place a point of mass within range, with a field up to ½ CAT size around it. For the duration, the point bends grace and 
sin around it, easily throwing off the targeting of supernatural projectiles and warping the presence of projections within range. 
This does not affect creatures, only ranged effects, auras, and ambient grace. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain 
or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect.  
●​
Trajectory Scene, Charm, Self 
○​
For the duration, you become immediately and constantly aware of the velocity, acceleration, pitch, tilt, yaw, and spin of anything 
you can visually see as raw mental information. You become able to calculate complex equations and evaluations in a split 
second, letting you predict and reverse engineer the movements and measurements of any number of simple objects within short 
range up to CAT seconds into the future and past. This foresight breaks down if the object is inherently chaotic or complex, such 
as a sentient creature, incredibly chaotic cloud of small objects, or a gaseous volume. This can easily affect the parameters of a 
roll. Grant +1D the next time the target takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
IDEAL 
No. It has to be perfect. It MUST be perfect. 
 
Fact: Perfectionism and intense paranoia is a highly commendable trait in exorcist assets, leading to overall higher mission result quality and 
reduced exorcist casualties. 
 
Passive: Spotless 
●​
You may, once per rest, turn yourself completely spotless, wiping away any contaminants, filth, aesthetic damage, and visual indications of 
imperfection. This effect erases 1d3 stress, but you take an equal amount of extra stress the next time you take supernatural stress before 
the end of the next rest, as the facade shatters and you return to your imperfect state. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Align Instant, CAT Range 
○​
Everything in its right place. All objects in an up to CAT sized area around you are immediately sorted and arranged properly; tilted 
or tipped things right themselves, scattered items return to shelves, wires and cables are managed, series of items sort 
themselves according to your sorting preference, etc. This does not repair any physically damaged objects but keeps them in 
place as if they were undamaged until the end of the scene. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the 
next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Pristine Instant, Transmute, CAT Range 
○​
All filth, grime, and pollutants within a CAT sized area around you are removed in an instant, restoring everything to a sparklingly 
clean and perfectly sterile state. All of the collected grime is compressed into a single mass that is deposited somewhere random 
within the investigation area, which will gestate into a medium swarm of traces of any currently active sin once pressure increases. 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Clockwork Scene, Charm, CAT Area 
○​
Things go smoothly, and all is well in the world. For the duration, while left alone or used in the intended way all mundane objects 
within a CAT area of you can not jam, congest, gridlock, or otherwise fail in a way that is purely mechanical; creatures purposefully 
altering or damaging objects can still disable them. At higher PSYCHE, you affect additional things: 
■​
2: The opening and closing of automatic doors, the movement of crowds, traffic and traffic lights. 
■​
3: Mundane objects can no longer impede the path of up to CAT creatures of your choice as the world harmlessly bends 
around them. 
○​
Once this effect ends, all affected objects will now unavoidably jam, congest, or gridlock but only in the most inconvenient times 
and ways until the end of the next scene. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an 
ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Correct Instant, Long Range 
○​
Once per scene, target a creature you can either see or know the full name of. In order to use this power, the target must have 
done at least one of the following: 
■​
Significantly broken or transgressed the manners or traditions of their family’s culture. 
■​
Been significantly rude or ill-mannered, especially towards those of what you think is a higher social strata than 
themselves. 
■​
Been significantly slovenly or unclean, especially if their personal property is so. 
■​
Been significantly tardy, or failed to uphold a promise to be somewhere to you. 
■​
Significantly harassed or abused a human with the intent to abuse and not kill. 
○​
As soon as a creature fulfills one of these criteria, they have a single opportunity to correct it, negating it. If they do not or cannot, 
you may roll PSYCHE, gaining +1D for each fulfilled criteria. On a success, you may immediately force the target into a supplicant 
apology, forcing them downwards with a strength CAT equal to the number of successes. This power does not cost a psyche 
burst. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Restore Instant, Touch 
○​
You make things how they should be, how they must be. You may spend a psyche burst to instantly end the enchantment or 
transmutation of any up to CAT sized object you can touch as long as the effect’s creator is a CAT equal to or less than your own 
and its alteration does something other than enhancing its original purpose. The object’s grace is immediately expelled towards 
the creator of the effect a short distance before vanishing, and the object becomes entirely immune to grace and supernatural 
damage until the end of the scene. 
 
 


 
DREDGE 
Just give up already. 
 
Fact:.  [BEGIN RECORDING] ….-kay wait so why do we keep these ones around again? Their whole point is that they-… No, they aren’t people, 
they're exorcists. What are you…? Oh shit is the recording still on. Shi-…. [END RECORDING] 
 
Passive: Decrepit 
●​
Mundane humans will always perceive you as if you were part of the worst, lowest, or most unimportant cultural or societal group in their 
opinion or perception. Even if you provide them with evidence to the contrary, they will simply smile and nod in pity or disguised revulsion. 
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Useless Instant, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
Target an object within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a burst on a success. On at least one success, any function or 
purpose the object has is completely nullified and it will never work for its intended purpose, or any purpose sufficiently similar to it. 
Pages will stick and solidify, a lock will never open to any key, mechanical and electronic functions will not work even fully 
replaced, etc. If this item is of incredible emotional significance to a creature, this power does not cost a psyche burst.  
●​
Filth Instant, Transmute, CAT Area 
○​
All surfaces and objects within an up to CAT sized area around you immediately become caked in filth and detritus as if left 
uncleaned for CAT^2 months, which can include rotting and deterioration. All evidence of interaction or passage in the area 
becomes completely impossible to perceive. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or 
an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Succumb Instant, Curse 
○​
You may activate Divine Agony while your Pathos is full to, instead of granting bonus dice, instantly cause any process or action of 
any kind to fail and sputter out, regardless of CAT or distance, as long as you can see it. If the process is a natural phenomenon 
such as the movement of a storm or the light of the sun, it returns to normal at the end of the mission. Your team is automatically 
charged 4 scrip each if the cessation is too noticeable or hard to cover up. Activating this power requires a psyche burst. 
●​
Hopeless Instant, Curse, Touch 
○​
Target an object or up to CAT sized area within range. The designated object or area can now never be fully repaired, improved, 
or cleaned in any way, permanently. This curse remains and transfers in a weaker state to anything that is recycled or repurposed 
from the original target. 
●​
Resign Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
As an action, target any creature within range you can make eye contact with, rolling PSYCHE and only spending a psyche burst 
on at least one success. On a success, the target wholeheartedly believes that they will die, be brought low, or otherwise become 
irreparably ruined in some way within the next 24 hours. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant bonus dice 
equal to the number of successes the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 
 


 
DIPLOMAT 
You’re everyone’s favourite. 
 
Fact: Diplomat exorcists consistently rank amongst the top hated exorcists across all of CAIN facilities. This statistic has been verified by over 30 
individual fully willing participants who readily gave their opinion under no duress, opinions that many exorcists resonate with even outside of the 
study. 
 
Passive: Disgust 
●​
Humans and exorcists loathe interacting with you, and will refrain from doing any actions identical to those you do to the best of their 
ability. Once per rest you may enforce this stigma to forbid any being, allied or enemy, from repeating one mundane action of your choice 
without suffering extreme and debilitating mental anguish. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Distance Scene, Curse, Self 
○​
For the duration, mundane beings become fundamentally incapable of approaching you closer than a short distance away, and 
will absolutely refuse to converse with you or associate with you at all. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant 
+1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Pal Instant, Charm, Touch 
○​
You sling an arm around a target’s shoulder and begin casually conversing with them to an extremely unsettling degree. Choose 
one of the following options: 
■​
Both of you become immediately aware of any possible exit to escape this interaction, even hidden ones, within a CAT 
distance. 
■​
Both of you immediately become aware of the location of any allied creature (to either of you) within a CAT distance to 
save you from this interaction. 
●​
Plans Scene, Curse, Long Range 
○​
As an action, target a creature within range that you are conversing with (which you do not need to see), rolling PSYCHE if they 
are unwilling. On a success, the target will attempt to escape from this interaction with excuses of being needed at a location of 
your choice that you know of, which the target does not need to know about beforehand. They will immediately take the shortest 
and least impeded route to that location, but are not necessarily granted passage if things like security and passcodes are 
involved. 
●​
Polite Instant, Curse 
○​
As an action, target a creature within range that you are conversing with (which you do not need to see), rolling PSYCHE. On a 
success, both you and the target are locked in a polite but incredibly uncomfortable conversation until you choose to disengage, 
as they are willed to remain and nod or voice affirmations politely. While in this state neither you nor your target experience hunger 
or thirst, fatigue, or soreness from standing, sitting, or otherwise remaining in place, and must always attempt to have the last 
word. 
●​
Smile Passive, Self 
○​
You now count the number of failures on any skill roll to converse with, persuade, or charm a being instead of the number of 
successes to determine the degree of success. For every one rolled in such a roll, choose one of the following options: 
■​
The target immediately forgets who you are after the interaction, out of sheer desire to not be associated with you. 
■​
The target immediately acquiesces to a minor request or bargain, out of desperation to be rid of you. 
■​
The target is extremely unsettled, granting +1D to any attempt to gain insight on or get a read on them this scene to all 
allied creatures except you 
○​
For every non-failure rolled, one mundane being present in the scene has their opinion of you drastically lowered. 
 
 


 
RUN 
There’s nothing there. Please, there’s nothing there. 
 
Fact: There is nothing there. ITs BEHIЙD ӲФU RUN RUN RUN RUN RUN RUN 
 
Passive: Capgras 
●​
You are pursued by a psychic predator, a sentient psychophagic organism that has latched onto your consciousness. This predator can 
assume the face and voice of any being you know, knows where you are at all times, and you can always sense it, but not locate it, up to 
an extreme distance away. This predator is invisible to everyone but you unless it chooses to be visible, and cannot normally deal stress 
or slashes to things other than you or objects within a short range of you. 
 
Every time pressure increases, your predator gains its own Tension Moves separate from the sin you are hunting. 
 
No one will ever believe you. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Autotomy Passive, Transmute, Self 
○​
This power does not cost a psyche burst, Whenever you take an injury or take 5 or more stress at once, you dislodge an extremity 
from your body. Any time afterwards that a being tries to supernaturally appear near you, observe you, or summon/banish you, 
that effect instead applies to the lost extremity and it is destroyed. The lost extremity regrows fully after two missions, spending 
one mission in a shriveled and vestigial state. 
●​
Aposematism Scene, Charm, Self 
○​
For the duration, you become entirely indistinguishable from one being of your choice by any means other than direct visual 
perception. Your voice, sounds you make, grace signature, shadow, and speech patterns perfectly mimic your chosen target but 
your appearance does not change. If your predator attacks you while this is active, they have a 50/50 chance of attacking your 
mimicked target instead. This can easily change the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes 
advantage of this effect. 
●​
Snarl Rest, Curse, Long Range 
○​
You instantly summon your Predator into the scene up to a CAT distance away from yourself, fully revealing them to one target 
within long range of where it was summoned. Your predator now considers that creature among their prey alongside you until the 
next rest, but may also reroll a 6 on any of its risk dice for the duration. 
●​
Bristle Instant, Transmute, Extreme Range 
○​
As an action, you may roll PSYCHE spread barbs of psychic energy around your conceptual form, granting all creatures (including 
your predator) within range that are actively pursuing or seeking you a simple choice: they may either delay their pursuit by ½ CAT 
scenes or they may gain immediate knowledge of your location and take stress or slashes on their talisman equal to the number 
of successes + PSYCHE as they tear through the barbs. Even mundane creatures become aware of this choice and the truth of it. 
●​
Glance Instant, Passive, Long Range 
○​
You become constantly aware of any creature within range that is hidden fully behind cover such as a tree, box, or building, but 
not indoors; however, you also perceive additional illusory presences equal to 6-CAT. As an action you may spend a psyche burst 
to attempt to sneak up on any hidden entity, real or imaginary. On a success, even if there is no one there, you create someone to 
appear there. This can only be a creature you have seen at least once before, a common animal, or a mundane human with no 
significant standing or power. 
 
There is no predator. There is nothing following you. Nothing moved around that corner. You only hear the footsteps of the people around you. 
You just forgot to lock the doors, or close the windows. Nothing bad has happened yet. Nothing will. DONT LET IT KNOW THAT YOU KNOW. 
 
Predator (???): Execution ???.  
●​
Cannot be killed. Can be harmed. A CAT•4 section clock, once filled, causes them to flee. The predator’s prey must use their entire 
action to attempt to hide or escape from the predator, rolling if necessary, and filling sections of the clock equal to the number of 
successes. These do not need to target or be hostile to the predator. As long as a creature’s full action was used only to hide or sneak, 
the predator cannot target them with their risk die or sense them. 
●​
The predator can never be seen clearly unless it is mid attack; it always seems to be a blur too fast to follow, or ducks behind surfaces 
or into the peripherals. 
●​
The predator is blind, and cannot see or detect creatures that require being seen with normal vision. Any mundane humans that 
approaches the predator while it is actively present are violently torn apart and die instantly unless an exorcist flies to aid similar to a 
severe attack, taking 1d6 stress. 
●​
The predator may use any tension move available to sins other than Use A Domain. 
 
Reactions: 
 
Inflict stress. Attacks with: Cruel claws, serrated teeth, rippling muscle, hooked talons, barbed tail, tendrils covered in needles, massive 
tentacles (1) 5 stress, (2-3) 3 stress, (4+) 2 stress. Deals +1 stress or slashes to its prey. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: Vanish into silence, speed up, pull a target away, impale an exorcist, tear through obstructions, let out a 
spine-chilling snarl, wail loudly, throw a large object. 
 
Or: Click (1) Once per mission during a non-conflict travel scene, the Predator may supernaturally separate their prey from their allies, luring 
them into a trance. Their prey wakes to a click or snap sound in an area where they are completely isolated, and the Predator immediately 
emerges into the scene.  
 
Or: Sigh (1) Once per mission during a non-conflict scene in an interior, the predator may appear over the shoulder of their prey to all other 
allied creatures, though their prey remains unaware. One of the prey’s allies may attempt a single action to prevent the attack, reducing the 
stress taken by 2 • the number of successes. Otherwise, their prey takes 7 stress and suffers instant death. 
 
Or: Chase (3 or lower) The predator may inch closer with a click or snapping noise, reducing the distance to their prey by one CAT.  
 
 


 
SEAL 
Close off your heart. Nothing will ever hurt you again. 
 
Fact: Shell exorcists must be kept within actively monitored analog facilities without any normal form of door, shutter, or closing passageway. 
Failure to comply will lead to immediate demerit.  
 
Passive: Paranoia 
●​
If you close and lock a door or passageway, you become instantly aware of when it is opened, but not by whom or how it is opened 
(lockpicked, unlocked, broken, etc). There is no time or distance limit to this effect, and you cannot tell which passageway has been 
triggered if you are monitoring multiple. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Bunker Instant, Transmute, Touch 
○​
As an action, target an object or enclosed structure up to CAT+2 in size and roll PSYCHE. It immediately becomes embedded into 
the earth like a classical bunker, with an entrance to match (usually placed on the former “roof”, but can also be placed on the side 
if you embed it sideways into a cliff face, etc). All entrances aside from the new one become completely impossible to enter or exit 
from unless by digging or tunneling. At the end of the scene, the structure emerges from the ground once more. For every 
success, choose one option: 
■​
The entrance becomes an elevator, and the structure is embedded up to a CAT distance into the earth. 
■​
The entrance becomes fortified, like a blast door. 
■​
The entrance becomes hidden or obscured in some way. 
●​
Lockdown Instant, CAT Area 
○​
Every passageway or opening with a cover and/or lock within the area immediately snaps shut (and locks if able). Physical keys 
and doors can still be opened normally, but any sort of digital lock becomes inoperable until the end of the scene. 
●​
Alarm Instant, CAT Area 
○​
All forms of alarm or alert within the area go off simultaneously and constantly and cannot be shut down until the end of the scene; 
fire alarms, lock alarms, phone alarms, tornado sirens, alarm clocks, those anti-theft alarms in supermarkets, infrastructure 
monitors, hospital heart monitors, elevator bells, etc. It becomes nearly impossible to detect exorcist actions by sound. Gain or 
grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect.  
●​
Hermetic Instant, Transmute, Short Range 
○​
An enclosed space of your choice, up to CAT in size (such as a hazmat suit, room, display case, or car) becomes perfectly and 
absolutely sealed against the outside environment, including temperature, gases, liquids, pressure, and grace emissions. If no 
way to produce oxygen is provided, a creature may run out of air if sealed inside this space. If this hermetically sealed space is 
breached, the violent decompression automatically damages anything near the breach; roll PSYCHE for stress or slashes, 
including towards objects near the breach. 
●​
Clearance Charm, Until Rest, Self 
○​
As an action, touch some form of plastic card (credit card, business card, gift card, etc), rolling PSYCHE, only spending a burst on 
at least one success. The card immediately becomes a “key” with clearance equal to the number of successes rolled: 
■​
1. Grocery store doors, janitors closets, bathrooms, classrooms, etc 
■​
2. Corporate offices, grocery store stock rooms, warehouses, boiler rooms, etc 
■​
3. Server rooms, airport security offices, laboratory equipment rooms, etc 
○​
The card’s appearance remains unchanged, and its effects unlock mechanical locks as if they were digital locks as well. 
 
 


 
WHORL 
Around and around, falling inwards into yourself. 
 
Fact: The meaningless flowery prose many Whorl users seem to repeat to themselves holds no observable meaning, and is postulated to be a 
result of rapid degradation of the language centers of the brain. 
 
Passive: Murmur 
●​
You hear a constant, unending, ever-present murmuring of indecipherable words around you; hearing quiet sounds is nearly impossible 
over the drone. Whenever you use your Whorl powers, a mouth will appear somewhere on your body to produce the murmur (roll 1d6). 
○​
The back of your head. 
○​
The palm of your hand. 
○​
Your chest, near your sternum. 
○​
Next to your own mouth. 
○​
Replacing one of your eyes. 
○​
Your stomach, back, or ribs. 
●​
For every manifested mouth, you feel more and more out of breath. 
 
Abilities 
●​
Tremor Scene, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Target a creature, rolling PSYCHE, and only spending a burst on at least one success. On a success, for the duration you may 
replace any spoken sentence they hear or written sentence they read with a single short sentence of your choosing (such as 
“wake up”, “we know”, or “behind you”). The alteration only works once, if they look away and back, or ask for a repeat of the 
sentence, they perceive it normally. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally 
takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Writhe Instant, Transmute, CAT Area 
○​
All text within a CAT sized area becomes nearly indecipherable as it warps into writhing repetitions of the Murmur, as if text has 
been layered over other text hundreds of times. All text in the area around you is affected for the duration, including screens, 
handwriting, print, and even supernatural means of display. Looking at any writing causes immediate eye-watering and a feeling of 
moral disgust. 
●​
Squirm Instant, Curse, Short Range 
○​
Target up to CAT*2 creatures within range, rolling PSYCHE, only spending a psyche burst on at least one success. On at least 
one success, all targets are forced to continuously repeat the Murmur, preventing them from inhaling or catching breath. The rest 
of their body is still fully under their control and awareness. This cannot damage or kill a creature, but may cause them to go 
unconscious. A creature is freed from this effect at the end of the scene, or after it goes unconscious.Gain or grant +1D the next 
time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Twitch Instant, Curse, Long Range 
○​
Fall back into the murmur and begin to speak it through yourself. Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst on at least one success. On 
a success, any mundane human that can hear you, including through phone calls and speakers, experiences painful yet harmless 
stiff paralysis similar to a strong neurotoxin. You may prolong this effect as long as you can exhale, and it ends when you run out 
of breath or inhale. Exorcists and supernatural beings simply develop a splitting headache. You may cause a developed mouth 
from your Murmur passive to become permanent instead of spending a burst. This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain 
or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
●​
Thrash Scene, Curse, CAT Area 
○​
The murmur rises to a fever pitch, thrumming with subsonic bass and supersonic high tones. Roll PSYCHE, only spending a burst 
on at least one success. On a success, animals and electronics within range go haywire, and you may cause a number of short 
quakes equal to the number of successes rolled as a free action for the duration, with an intensity up to your CAT: 
■​
1-2: A faint rumbling fills the area that can shatter glass and throw off balance within range. 
■​
3-4: A moderate shaking fills the area, tossing around anything not nailed down or secured and violently damaging 
structures within range. 
■​
5-6: A harsh quake fills the area, easily toppling buildings and trees and disabling infrastructure within range. 
■​
7: The earth thrashes ceaselessly, causing untold damage within range and reducing most standing structures to rubble. 
You may gain an additional +1D when taking advantage of this effect. 
○​
This can easily affect the parameters of a roll. Gain or grant +1D the next time you or an ally takes advantage of this effect. 
 


 
Handler Options 
 


 
SEER SIN BRIEFINGS 
 
KRAKEN 
 
Cruel and sadistic, Krakens are insidious torturers that trap others in the worst fear of their 
deceased host. 
 
Krakens are born out of pure, primal fear. They are the children of unbridled terror, purveyors of 
the art of panic and dread. There is no emotion to muddle their ambition, no weakness to exploit to 
sway their minds. Their only desire is to taste more of that sweet, saccharine neurochemical 
adrenaline. 
 
Krakens commonly manifest in: 
 
●​
People with extreme phobias, especially trauma-induced. 
●​
People who fear for their lives in a life and death scenario. 
●​
People with extreme anxiety, panic attacks, and high stress. 
 
Primary emotion: Terror 
 
Appearance and Psychophysiology: Krakens, unlike other Sins, are almost exclusively Form II. 
They spawn when a Graced individual experiences absolute panic or fear that causes their 
sinseed to initiate a cascading failure in their internal organs, leading to them dying from a 
fear-induced psionic aneurysm. Krakens themselves are sickly, weak, and shriveled creatures, 
sitting inside their host’s hollowed puppet corpse like a parasite, but they project an illusion into 
other’s minds as a large, imposing figure. They most often embody the fear that killed their host; 
birdlike and feathered for ornithophobia, a twisted metal homunculus for someone's life flashing 
before their eyes as they spin off of the road, etc. The Kraken has preternaturally fine control over 
their host’s corpse, letting them warp flesh or bend joints in ways that would normally compromise  
their host’s integrity. Their only ambition is to acquire adrenaline and fear neurochemicals which 
they feed on. When creating a Kraken, pick one real phobia, such as ophidophobia, acrophobia, 
ecclesiophobia, etc and theme the Kraken’s appearance, actions and Palace after this Phobia that 
they embody. 
 
Vibes: IT (2017), Nightmare on Elm Street (1984), Alan Wake 1 & 2 (2021-2023). 
 
Behaviour: Krakens are frail, but compensate with their innate talent for illusion, misdirection and 
mimicry. This can take the form of “haunting” a few individuals constantly or sampling from a larger 
populace. Krakens are cruel, and will often get so carried away tormenting their food that their prey 
dies from shock, but will rarely care about the corpses of its victims, as it doesn’t find them fun to 
play with. Krakens do not seem to understand that their activities are what causes the prey to die, 
and think of them as weak for dying to them. 
 
 
ARCHIVE KRAKENS 
 
THE 6TH CROW 
Possessed the corpses of the ████ family in rural ███ after Subject H914, the youngest of the family, was locked inside a closet after a break-in, 
and forced to watch as birds ██████. Upon manifestation superstitions surrounding counting crows began to take literal turns as nameless 
bodies were found seemingly ████ by birds, only to disappear when bystanders stopped looking. Upon arriving it was found every non-corvid 
avian in a 3 kilometer radius of ██ had been simultaneously pecked to death. Manifestation eliminated but no recorded execution, 3 exorcists 
MIA. 
 
SHUDDER 
A rare non-form-II CAT 4 Kraken spawned from Subject H303 after being chased into the woods by the notorious serial killer ████. Subject H303 
seemingly made it halfway across a winter river before collapsing due to sinseed-induced organ failure caused by the extreme stress and 
hypothermia. The Kraken did not possess the corpse and instead latched onto the killer, and the two formed a pact to ████. Due to its secretive 
nature and abstinence from direct interaction, it is theorised its kill count ranged in the ████s before exorcists were able to dispatch it. Execution 
recorded, 17 exorcist casualties. 
 
SANGRE DEL MAR 
Subject ████ was found to possess an incredibly deep-rooted case of thalassophobia, triggered when a hurricane flooded the coast of 
████████████ where they resided and carried their uprooted house onto the open ocean. Subject ████ died from a non-grace-related heart 
attack upon waking from a blunt force-induced unconsciousness. It is unknown how long the CAT 6 sin had festered in their corpse before the 
████ Coast Guard found and brought back Subject ███’s body. Immediately upon returning to land all humans within a ████ km radius fell into 
a comatose nightmare-stricken state. ████ recorded civilian casualties due to drowning from saltwater forming in their lungs, ██ recorded 
exorcist deaths before deployment of Justice successfully dispatched the manifestation. 
TRAUMAS 
The Admin, as the Kraken, answers the following questions, then establishes a trauma based on the truthful answers. 
●​
Do you fight, do you freeze, or do you run? 
●​
What sparked that primal fear in your heart? 
●​
What would you face your phobia to save? 
 
For every question the exorcists answer, they can counter a sin’s reaction, rolling 1D3 after the sin acts. Reduce any stress suffered by all targets by the amount on the die, and 
the sin immediately takes that many slashes on its execution talisman from the psychic trauma, which cannot reduce it below 1. 
PALACE 
A Kraken’s palace is usually in a location related to their host’s death or source of their phobia. Despite the extremely variable nature of the 
Kraken’s domain, it is rather small, more of a den than a large demiplane. If you enter the palace, there is not much the Kraken can do to hide 
itself within the palace.  
 


 
 
To protect themselves, the Kraken employs a strange and esoteric Key to enter their palace, such as superstitious rituals or a specific chant. If the 
way to enter is not found, it is impossible to enter the palace even if you know where it is. You can grant this Key naturally, but if exorcists solve all 
three trauma questions they immediately learn of what the Key is. Guessing and using the wrong Key immediately marks one slash on the 
Pressure talisman. 
 
The form it takes is highly dependent on the circumstances of its host’s death, but always relates directly to the terror that killed their host; if a 
graced were killed by their overwhelming fear of snakes the palace would be a thick, hissing jungle; for ornithophobia, a dark and bramble-ridden 
forest ringing with the hoarse calls of crows. 
 
Kraken palaces are typically: unnerving, disorienting, paranoia-inducing, quiet, dark, maze-like, eerie, suffocating. 
PRESSURE: THE LONG DREAM 
A Kraken begins to lull nearby humans into a nightmare-ridden dream state, causing the unreal and real worlds to intertwine as illusions blur the 
boundaries between the sleeping and the awake. Those within the Kraken’s influence tend to sleepwalk and operate as if in a trance, fading in and 
out of direct consciousness as hallucinatory nightmares jolt them awake periodically. 
 
A Kraken’s influence starts off only affecting those close to or related to the victim; family, friends, rivals or colleagues. It spreads insidiously, like a 
plague, feasting on the existential dread that comes from doubting what is real and what is not. Its nightmares and illusions can instill fears into its 
victims that they did not previously have, causing even those that loved a certain object to become repulsed by it. Marine biologists cower at the 
sight of fish, avid artists freeze in terror after glimpsing the ink dripping down a canvas like blood. This may start off small like unease and slight 
panic, but as the Kraken desires more and more fear to sustain itself it will begin to trigger primal terror, often lethal levels of stress, in a desperate 
bid to sustain its cancerous growth. Its host corpse will become distorted and nightmarish, and its domain of influence a realm of nightmarish 
creatures and hellish abominations.  
 
A Kraken has power over someone equal to the amount to which they fear it. Every time pressure increases, their terror digs its roots deeper into 
its victims. The stronger its grasp, the more powerful it becomes. 
●​
0-1: Its victims begin to avoid the subject of the phobia as if by superstition, unable to describe the sensation but nevertheless unnerved 
by it. Most become confused by their sudden daydreams, sleepwalking, and stupors. Minor illusions dot the area. 
●​
2-3: Victims begin to frantically avoid the subject of the phobia as if it could harm them, and will ostracize those that still are unafraid of the 
phobia’s domain. Paranoia begins to set in as the dreams dig deeper. Most people cannot tell their dreams from reality. The Kraken’s 
Phobia illusions begin to manifest in reality, with real and tangible consequences. More complicated illusions start appearing with 
frequency. 
●​
4-5: Victims will resort to violence and drastic means to avoid even the mention of the phobia. Mentions of the phobia trigger panic and 
disorientation in any who hear the subject spoken of. Even if avoiding the subject of the phobia means death, they will do so (avoiding 
water, or the local source of food, or avoiding fire in a winter village). All victims are entirely unable to distinguish what is real and fake, and 
act erratically. Exorcists begin to feel the effects of the sin as if it was level 0-1. Illusions can now cover entire buildings and city blocks. 
●​
6+: Any victims it directly affects instantly suffer lethal levels of shock as nonstop nightmares and visions of their deepest fears flood all of 
their senses. If an exorcist feels this level of effect they immediately take an injury. Exorcists begin to feel the effects of the sin as if it was 
level 2-3, and each level of Pressure beyond this increases the tier at which exorcists are affected. The Kraken deals +1 stress on all of its 
attacks. Nothing can be discerned from illusions. Roads are suddenly broken bridges. A store face is actually a blank wall. A street seems 
to go on forever, until you blink and you’re in a forest. 
TRACES 
Nightmares 
 
Nightmares are flickering shades that take the appearance of fears of whoever views them. They are good at mimicking 
humanoid noises, but are ultimately mindless predators. They act as mosquitos, siphoning off and storing terror to then 
bring to the Kraken for consumption. 
 
Killing a nightmare causes them to burst and release raw psionic fear in a bubble, causing nearby non-graced humans 
to faint or have seizures. 
 
Nightmares (sins) Execution talisman 2 (solo), 4 (pack), 6 (swarm). 
 
●​ Armed with shadowy whips and quick weapons related to the Phobia of the person they are fighting. 
●​ Cannot interact with tangible reality or be harmed while in bright light. 
 
Reactions: 
Inflict Stress: Attacks with: piercing shrieks, inky tendrils, choking shadows. (1): 4 stress, (2/3): 2 stress, (4+): 1 stress. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: retreat into the shadow of a civilian, siphon fear from exorcists to heal 1 slash on 
their talisman, dissolve into black mist, incorrectly mimic an exorcist, choke with tentacles, blind with a spray of ink,  
 
Or: Sleep Paralysis (1/2): Threat: send an exorcist into a dazed stupor. To the exorcist, it appears as if they have been 
teleported into a shard of the sin’s palace and is frozen solid as shapes emerge from the shadows. They can only be 
woken by taking stress from an ally’s action.  
 
 
 


 
EXECUTION 4 + PRESSURE + CAT 
ATTACKS WITH: Implements related to its Phobia, shriveled wiry limbs, claws, beaks and talons, inky tentacles, nightmarish visions, sprays of 
caustic ink, projectile spines, (1) 4 stress, (2/3): 3 stress, (4+): 2 stress. 
COMPLICATIONS: While a complication is active, the Kraken deals +1 stress on all of its attacks. Cause an effect related to its Phobia, slink 
away into shadows and hide, spread an oil slick across the ground, project illusory duplicates, mimic the appearance and voice of a person, tie 
someone to a wall with tentacles, blind someone with boiling ink, warp perception to disorient attackers, use a Domain. 
THREATS: Threaten an effect related to its Phobia, burst into a public space and absorb the immediate shock and fear to power itself, take a 
civilian hostage in a comatose state, drag an exorcist away into the shadows, completely warp reality to reflect its illusions, charge up a deafening 
shriek, use a Domain. 
 
AFFLICTIONS 
Given by a threat, hook, or tension move. 
 
1.​ Icy Veins: All actions made to move are hard while you are near and/or can see the subject of the Kraken’s embodied phobia. 
2.​ Shivers: Whenever anyone rolls a 1, your next action related to fine movement or speaking becomes risky. If it was already risky, it 
becomes hard. 
3.​ Paranoia: Whenever you setup someone else, you take one stress. Permanently add to your agenda: trust no one. 
4.​ Wandering Dreams: Whenever you rest, take 1D3 stress or be separated a long distance from the rest of your group. 
5.​ Isolophobia: All actions become hard if you are not within reach of at least 1 other person you know. 
6.​ Inception: Permanently add to your agenda: prove you are awake. 
 
Or improvise: make a roll hard or risky. 
 
SEVERE ATTACK: PSYCHOGENIC DEATH 
 
The Sin can use this ability on a ‘1’ on a risk roll. They can only use it once per mission. 
 
Target an exorcist. Any other exorcists nearby must decide to fly to their aid. Any that don’t offer 
aid cannot participate.  
 
Start with a pool of 6d6. Then remove one dice for each of the following. If an answer is “no”, you or someone aiding you 
can immediately make an action to rectify the answer, with only a few moments to act, suffering consequences as normal if 
they fail. 
 
●​ Are you unafraid of what the Kraken embodies? 
●​ Are you brave, do you face your fears? 
●​ Are you in bright light or daylight? 
●​ Is the Kraken hindered, distracted, or under duress in any way? 
 
Then roll the dice. The Kraken morphs its visage into a horrifying image that burns into the minds of any victim 
unfortunate enough to view it. This is not simply a scary face, it is an embodiment of the primal terror that lurks within the 
ancient, instinctual brain of the prey animal. The exorcist and any aiding them take 1 stress for each die rolled, no matter 
the result. 
●​ If at least one ‘1’ comes up, the Kraken transfers its embodied phobia permanently to the exorcist targeted, forcing 
them to permanently add “Fear (the Kraken’s Phobia)” to their agenda. This exorcist will carry this phobia with 
moderate severity for the rest of their life. 
●​ If two or more ‘1’s come up, the targeted exorcist immediately falls into a semi-catatonic state for the rest of the 
mission, and can only be snapped out of it by effects that would raise the dead. They can still be targeted, attacked 
and take stress and sin while in this state. While in this state, all actions that require intense thinking (such as 
mathematics and engineering) are impossible, and all actions that require any amount of processing/thinking are 
hard. 
 
 


 
DOMAINS: Pick three 
 
1.​ Clouds Blotting the Blood Moon 
 
The world grows eerily quiet during the night. The sky turns completely black, the wind does not blow, crickets do not sing, and leaves do not 
rustle. Flashlights and other sources of mundane light seem to dim, and fall off a shorter distance than they should.  
​
All rolls related to stealth or covert operation are hard unless the exorcist has some way of muffling the sound and hiding their appearance, and 
rolling a 1 on a risk dice alerts all beings within a CAT distance to their approximate location. 1/mission the Kraken can manifest a small pack of 
Nightmares adjacent to any exorcist in either blinding light or darkness. This ability refreshes when pressure increases.  
 
2.​Hypnic Jerk 
 
The first time an exorcist would suffer death or instant death while within the Kraken’s area of effect, they instead “wake up” at the very edge of the 
Kraken’s boundary at extreme distance and gain the Night Terrors hook. Any effects that would take place on exorcist death do not happen, such 
as the Guardian agenda’s passive effect.  
 
The affected exorcist recalls all events prior to their “waking up” as muddled and incoherent dreamlike memories, and cannot recall specific or vital 
information unless they see it again. All domains, afflictions, and other effects caused by the Kraken cease to affect the player until they enter the 
area of effect again. Other exorcists still see the “deceased” exorcist’s corpse, and must act accordingly. ​
​
Night Terrors Hook: 
This hook has 6 sections. When this hook fills, the exorcist immediately collapses into deep sleep until the end of the next rest. In this state, they 
take -1 stress from all sources but automatically fail all actions and are completely unaware of their surroundings.  
 
3.​ The Shadow at Your Shoulder 
 
The Kraken gains the ability to travel into and through mirrors and reflective surfaces within Long distance as a reaction. While submerged inside 
of a reflection it is effectively intangible and cannot use domains, but shattering, covering, scratching or obscuring the reflective surface prevents 
the Kraken from exiting through it. Pressure increasing allows the Kraken to travel through more surfaces. 
●​
0-1: Actual mirrors and highly reflective glass only. 
●​
2-3: Glass, metal, and moderately reflective materials. 
●​
4-5: Puddles of water, shiny plastics, glazed ceramics. 
●​
6+: 1/mission the Kraken can emerge from the reflection in an exorcist’s eye if they are looking at a source of its Phobia, dealing 2d6 
stress and inflicting an affliction immediately. 
 
As a threat, the Kraken can reach out of a mirror and drag one exorcist a long distance away, ejecting them from another surface.  
 
4.​ The Saccharine Scent of Terror 
 
The Kraken gains access to a new type of Trace: the Stricken. It is an amalgamated crowd of civilians driven to utter terror by the Kraken to such a 
degree they flail and grasp towards the nearest hint of help. In their panic, they only cause harm. 
 
Stricken 
 
Nightmares (sins) Execution talisman 6 (swarm). 
 
●​ Armed with grasping hands, flailing limbs, sharp claws, sheer numbers, and frightening speed. 
●​ Appears as gaunt human bodies that seem to melt and intersect the further in the middle of the mob they are. 
●​ While above ½ of its talisman, takes 1 less slash from all sources. 
 
Reactions: 
Inflict Stress: Attacks with: Icy skin, flailing limbs, trampling, charging (1): 3 stress, (2/3): 2 stress, (4+): 1 stress. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: Restrain an exorcist, flood a hallway with bodies, approach from all sides, scream to 
alert the Kraken, topple structures, absorb more civilians (erase one slash per civilian), attempt to pull off limbs/dislocate. 
 
Or: Bury (1/2): Complication: The Stricken clamber over and on top of an exorcist in short range, begging and pleading with 
raspy voices and tearing at them with sharp nails. The affected exorcist cannot move and takes 1 additional stress from all 
sources until the complication is resolved. 
 
 
5.​ Was It You, In That Mirror? 
 
Once per scene, the Sin can target one exorcist. The Kraken perfectly imitates that exorcist, including fake “blasphemy abilities” and other such 
skills. The GM and the targeted player secretly flip a coin, calling heads or tails. The one that correctly called the result is the “real” exorcist, and 
the one that incorrectly called the result is the mimic. Any stress the real exorcist takes via attacks or effects from its allies is increased by 2, and 
any stress the Sin takes is reduced to 1 as it dissipates into a dark mist and vanishes. The Kraken deals 1 stress per scene to one exorcist of its 
choice until the impostor is revealed, at which point the mimic immediately deals 1D6 stress to any one exorcist present in the scene. The admin 
can trigger this domain a total of three times per mission.
 
 


 
6.​ Things That Go Bump in The Night 
 
The Kraken exacerbates fear, paranoia and distrust in the community surrounding it. Humans on this mission are not hostile to exorcists, but will 
never start off open, cheery, or trusting. Shutters are slammed closed, doors are locked as they pass by, and not even a newspaper blows across 
the street of the Kraken’s domain. Law enforcement in the area will be inherently wary of the exorcists. As pressure increases, distrust and fear 
between civilians heightens as well. 
 
Any rolls that would require them to confront the Phobia embodied by the Kraken are impossible, as they become obstinate and panicked. At 
night, all people within the Kraken’s area of effect will assume the exorcists to be part of their nightmares and become frightened, alerting the 
Kraken to the exorcist’s approximate location. 
 
This effect increases in severity with Pressure, and at max pressure causes all humans in the area to perceive the exorcists as shambling, horrific 
monstrosities. 
 
7.​ Is It Dead? 
 
The first time the Kraken is damaged, the Sin and the exorcist that damaged it flip a coin, calling heads or tails. If the exorcist wins, the scene 
continues as normal. If the Sin wins, it takes only one slash on its execution talisman and dissipates into black fog, teleporting a long distance 
away. This ability refreshes when pressure increases.
