8.​ Cold Hands Choke Your Veins 
 
The Kraken gains the ability to possess the mind of any non-graced civilian within its area of influence. Once per mission, the Kraken can forcefully 
take over the mind of an NPC the players know, and control them like a puppet. They may summon a copy of themselves by killing the puppet, 
and any stress dealt to the copy is not dealt to the kraken itself. This ability refreshes when pressure increases, and the Kraken can re-inhabit a 
guise of any person it has previously possessed. One subtle feature of their body changes during possession, and players will not notice the 
possession unless they roll at least 2 successes on a Connection or Investigation roll, which the GM then privately notifies them of. If a player fails 
this check on an unaffected civilian, you are allowed to lie and say they are possessed.
 
 


 
OBELISK 
 
Unstoppable and detached, Obelisks are alien and indifferent beings who drain emotion from 
everything around them. 
 
Obelisks are born when a person experiences ultimate defeat. When someone loses all of their 
will to continue, care, or move on. They are the very incarnation of the dark night of the soul, the 
end of caring for anything.  
 
Obelisks commonly manifest in: 
 
●​
People who have suffered a life-altering loss or injury. 
●​
People who have lost the will to live or continue on. 
●​
People with dissociative disorders that distance themselves from reality. 
 
Primary emotion: Apathy 
 
Appearance and Psychophysiology: Obelisks are hulking, titanic entities. They tower over 
structures and humans, draining the vibrancy and emotion from the land around them. They are 
often made of inorganic materials, like metal and concrete, and slowly transmute everything they 
touch to the thing they are made of. They uncaringly seek the ultimate Apotheosis; they are not 
malicious or cruel, in fact they hold no emotion at all. The only thing they possess is a single, 
unshakable resolve. The Obelisk is a force that irreparably and violently alters the world around it 
but cannot comprehend that it does. It is so alien we do not look like life to it any more than a gust 
of wind. 
 
Vibes: Annihilation (2018), Look Outside (2025), BLAME! (2017) 
 
Behaviour: Obelisks are incomprehensible. They do not bother with subtlety or subterfuge, they 
do not waste precious time to hide. They do not step around humans in their way, or hold them 
hostage. The only thing in an Obelisk's mind is a cold drive towards a goal that is impossible for a 
human mind to comprehend. Chunks of their bodies begin to spread an infectious transmutation to 
everything around them, turning color gray and morphing organic material to stone, metal, or 
glass. Exorcists cannot convince or even talk to an Obelisk. It does not speak, or acknowledge 
their presence unless they directly interfere in its movement or activities. 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
ARCHIVE OBELISKS 
 
A SOLEMN SIMULACRUM 
Born from subject ████ after directly witnessing the brutal murder of their mother, an important governmental officer for the ████, in broad 
daylight. Manifestation was delayed until the unresponsive host was brought into the ████ Metropolitan Emergency Hospital, where it proceeded 
to turn ██ patients and staff into distorted coral-like structures, alongside destroying a 2 kilometer radius area of the city. 18 hours 32 minutes to 
kill, 2 exorcists KIA, 1 exorcist MIA. 
 
THE GREATEST OF MEN, A MACHINE 
Host subject unknown. Time to execute is unknown. Number of exorcists lost is unknown. No recorded dispatch of Virtues. The nature of reality 
suppressant metal woven into the ████ of the area led to noticeable temporal anomalies and significant frays in the fabric of reality. Instance 
registered for constant SEER monitoring operations to confirm the instance is verifiably eliminated. Site ███ is hereby designated a permanent 
quarantine zone. Signs of metallic transmutation on the skin or eyes of operatives is to lead to immediate execution. 
 
THE LAST MNEMOSYNE 
Subject 21OKJ was found unresponsive, cradled by a seemingly inanimate Obelisk with a gaping hole in its chest. Most of the surrounding natural 
landscape is speculated to have been instantly transmuted to gold upon the manifestation of the sin. Execution of the host led to the immediate 
disappearance of the figure and the reconstruction of reality back to its regular state, despite not being a normal result of sin execution. Mission 
deemed wildly successful. Zero exorcist or civilian casualties. Operatives granted permission to apply for a 1 scrip promotion. 
TRAUMAS 
The Admin, as the Obelisk, answers the following questions, then establishes a trauma based on the truthful answers. 
●​
What was the last thing you wanted? 
●​
Why did you even care? 
●​
Who do you still love? 
 
For every question the exorcists answer, they can counter a sin’s reaction, rolling 1D3 after the sin acts. Reduce any stress suffered by all targets by the amount on the die, and 
the sin immediately takes that many slashes on its execution clock from the psychic trauma, which cannot reduce it below 1. 
PALACE 
An Obelisk’s palace is actually contained within it, accessed by entering the form of the Obelisk somehow or touching it. It is almost always nearly 
impossible to understand. What the exorcists see inside of it is simply the closest approximation their senses can make, what their brains can 
comprehend without lighting their neurons on fire. They are otherworldly, absurd, terrifying, and make even the biggest and most confident exorcist 
feel a sense of utter futility. They are often wide, open spaces or voids, with strange gravity and spatial warping. They are almost never a single 
room, and grow in size with the Obelisk, potentially turning into complexes of their own. 
 
Obelisk palaces are typically: mind-bending, incomprehensible, abstract, silent, still, dead-looking, featureless, monolithic, imposing 
 


 
PRESSURE: THE GREAT WORK 
The Obelisk begins to drain the color, emotion, and life from the area around it. Those afflicted are completely unaware of their condition, or 
driven mad to the point they embrace it or do not care. As pressure increases, more and more of the landscape and life is converted to silent, 
inorganic material. Even the sky seems to lose its color, and the wind ceases. 
 
Eventually reality itself will begin to fold and wrap around the Obelisk, applying its eldritch knowledge to the laws of physics around it. Once a sin 
event happens, a permanent tear rends itself through reality, releasing a swarm of drifters swept into our world by the burgeoning aether. 
●​
0-1 The barest effects of the Obelisk begin to take root. The world and weather turn gloomy and desaturated, and humans react to match. 
Weird, distorted physical structures start popping up, but humans pay them no mind. 
●​
2-3 The Apotheosis nears its midway. Humans sport growths or spreading patches of the Obelisk’s material on their skin, and begin to feel 
apathetic to the actions of exorcists, appearing tired or worn down. Liquids turn syrupy and sluggish, and the wind completely dies down. 
The Obelisk grows 1 CAT in size. Alterations to the domain are much more intense, and the Obelisk may now summon a drifter instead of 
a trace as a tension move. 
●​
4-5 The Apotheosis is almost complete. Humans now have large parts of their bodies transmuted, and almost robotically continue 
mundane routines, completely ignoring the fact that exorcists or even other humans exist. Psyche abilities roll -1D unless targeting the 
Obelisk, its traces, or a drifter directly. The Obelisk grows 1 CAT in size. 
●​
6+ The tide bursts through the Obelisk like a fountain, spraying massive hordes of Grace entities and drifters across the battlefield. The 
world turns gray and colorless, humans are killed in place as they are entirely transmuted and frozen, and the Obelisk gains the ability to 
summon 2 traces and/or drifters of any kind as a tension move, rather than one. The terrain is almost unrecognizably warped and 
distorted, and the Obelisk grows 1 CAT in size. 
 
 
TRACES 
Fragment 
 
A Fragment is the pale reflection the Obelisk leaves on fractured reality, the glimmering embers of a nine hundred and 
fifth dimensional fire. It is knives that stab the flesh of the universe and make it bleed a cold, still blood. 
 
You never kill a fragment. You just shatter the mirror. 
 
Fragment (anomaly) Execution talisman 2 (solo), 4 (solo), 6 (solo). 
 
●​ Armed with nothing. The nothing kills you. 
●​ Teleports instead of moving. 
 
Reactions: 
Inflict Stress: Attacks with: lethal vibrations, impossible colors, its presence. (1): 4 stress, (2/3): 2 stress, (4+): 1 stress. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: Shatter into smaller reflections, teleport into an exorcist, send an exorcist into 
shock, override someone’s nervous system, warp reality.  
 
Or: Behold (1): Threat: The exorcist catches a glimpse of the soul of the Obelisk through this fractured reflection. They 
see the infinite total of everything that ever is not, but their mind shuts down to prevent them from dying to shock. The 
exorcist’s mental skills all immediately drop to 0 and they cannot gain advantage dice on rolls using them. This effect 
ends at the start of the next rest. Each Cluster can only use this threat once. 
 
 


 
EXECUTION 10 + PRESSURE + CAT 
ATTACKS WITH: Fragments of inorganic matter, pulses of debilitating pain waves, visions of heat death, eldritch words of truth, its presence, a 
petrifying gaze. (1) 5 stress, (2/3): 4 stress, (4+): 3 stress. 
COMPLICATION: Warp space around itself, teleport away, emit a debilitating psionic reverberation, flood an exorcist’s nervous system, summon 
spires and walls of inorganic material, manipulate gravity, exist, use a Domain. 
THREATS: Build up an intense vibrational blast, expand its size and shape, rearrange matter into fractals, reflect into multiple of itself, impale 
someone, attempt to flood an exorcist’s mind with impossible knowledge, just stand there, use a Domain. 
 
AFFLICTIONS 
Given by a threat, hook, or tension move. 
 
1.​ It’s… Beautiful: You are entranced by displays of the Obelisk’s ability. You cannot willingly look away from traces or major manifestations 
of the Obelisk and must be pulled away by another person. You resist. 
2.​ The Laugh of a Broken Mind: The first time in a scene you would take an injury, you instead sin overflow. The first time in a scene you 
would sin overflow, instead take an injury. Once both effects are triggered, this affliction ends. 
3.​ -1/12: Your Investigation and Surveillance are reduced by 2 as you are consumed by your own confusion. Permanently add to your 
agenda: drive yourself mad to find truth. 
4.​ Premonitions: When you reach max stress, if you can see an Obelisk or Fragment you see the future where you die. Your stress cap 
reduces by one more the next time you take an injury. 
5.​ Tinnitus: At the start of a scene, flip a 1d2. On a failure, all actions that rely on hearing become impossible for that scene and you are 
functionally deaf. If you are already deaf, you go blind. If you are already both, whatever sense you use to navigate is impaired. 
6.​ The Revelation: Permanently add to your agenda: stop caring. 
 
Or improvise: make a roll hard or risky. 
 
SEVERE ATTACK: CONVERSATION 
 
The Sin can use this ability on a ‘1’ on a risk roll. They can only use it once per mission. 
 
Target an exorcist. Any other exorcists nearby must decide to fly to their aid. Any that don’t offer 
aid cannot participate.  
 
Start with a pool of 6d6. Then remove one dice for each of the following. If an answer is “no”, you or someone aiding you 
can immediately make an action to rectify the answer, with only a few moments to act, suffering consequences as normal if 
they fail. 
 
●​ Are you out of sight of the Obelisk? 
●​ Is your mind calm and collected? 
●​ Do you have a helmet or head protection? 
●​ Is the Obelisk hindered, distracted or under duress in any way? 
 
Then roll the dice. The Obelisk attempts to communicate directly with the feeble, neuron-based brain of the exorcist. A 
flood of impossible ideas floods the collapsing mind of the target, imbuing the proprioception of an entity so far beyond the 
scope of human comprehension your neurons push out every other scrap of information to account for the influx. The 
exorcist and any aiding them take 1 stress for every die rolled, no matter the result. 
●​ If at least one ‘1’ comes up, the Obelisk renders the target insensate for the rest of the scene, convulsing, their 
head warping and expanding, growing more and more neurons to process even one billionth of the backlog of 
information. They suffer intense nose and ear bleeding.  
●​ If two or more ‘1’s come up, the targeted exorcist loses all of their previous memories, instincts, and knowledge as 
their brain shoves it out to repurpose the neurons for comprehension. Their head and brain swell to a truly 
grotesque size, and they suffer extreme disfigurement and mutation covering most of the skin above their 
shoulders. The amnesia and mutation effects can be removed with Temerity Psychosurgery at the cost of 4 scrip. 
 
If this attack kills the exorcist, and they either die or Give Up, a new Obelisk morphs into existence from their corpse. The 
new Obelisk has a talisman equal to half the max health of the original Obelisk, and cannot regain slashes beyond that. 
The new Obelisk shares domains with the original and cannot use severe attacks. 
 
 


 
DOMAINS: Pick three 
 
1.​ The Infinity Before 1 
 
The reality-fraying nature of the Obelisk’s palace begins to leak into the area around it. Space and time stop making sense in a CAT+1 distance 
around it, travel time becomes abstract, physics seem to cease obeying physics, and the exorcists cannot shake the fact that all of this feels like a 
dream. Floor plans seem to stretch into infinity, a road seems to loop back upon itself, and tiles of land seem to repeat themselves into infinity. 
1/pressure whenever an exorcist travels a significant distance between scenes, roll 1d6: 
●​
1: The distance takes triple the amount of time to travel. Tension automatically ticks up by one more. 
●​
2-3: The travel is confusing and warped in some way. Checks made to navigate take -1D until the next rest. 
●​
4-5: The travel is eerily still and unbothered.  
●​
6: The distance takes 1/3 the amount of time to travel. Tension does not tick up for the previous scene. 
 
2.​ A Radiant Rainbow of Nothing 
 
The Obelisk emits an aurora borealis of distorted space and incomprehensible hues that weaves over its entire domain, touching down like comet 
trails, or the ends of a rainbow. The Obelisk is considered to be in ½ CAT more places at once, rounded up, and its domain extends like an infinite 
pillar into the sky rather than simply being a radius around itself. Attacking multiple “instances” of the Obelisk at once deals +1 slashes against it. 
Electricity and wireless communication cease working correctly, and when any sort of wireless communication, speaker, or screen is used it seems 
to broadcast looping messages of a language no one on earth could possibly understand, causing disorientation and erratic behaviour in anyone 
who listens to or watches it. 
 
3.​ The Bottomless Void, Overflowing 
 
The Fragments of an Obelisk collide and spark into a warped, scintillating Cluster. The Obelisk gains the following new trace: 
 
Cluster 
 
A Cluster is a far more physical and violent manifestation of the Obelisk’s existence. It is, for one reason or another, a perfect 
psionic blank and instead manipulates the atoms of materials around it directly. It constantly patrols the domain of the 
Obelisk, killing those who step out of line and summoning the Obelisk itself at times. It is a sentry, a watchdog. Tell exorcists 
that they immediately get the sense that this is a being to avoid, not confront. 
 
Cluster (anomaly) Execution talisman 5+CAT. 
 
●​ Is entirely immune to grace abilities, but attacking it with mundane weapons is not Hard. 
●​ Hiding from the Cluster gains +1D as it can not supernaturally sense you. It cannot attack you while you are hidden. 
●​ Teleports instead of moving. 
●​ There can only be one cluster active in any given scene. 
 
Reactions: 
Inflict Stress: Attacks with: disintegration beams, spines of inorganic material, harsh noises. (1): 4 stress, (2/3): 3 stress, 
(4+): 2 stress. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: Dissolve into hard-to-damage sand, turn the landscape into spikes, summon a cloud of 
swirling shards, encase an exorcist in a block of stone, overload an exorcist’s nerves. 
 
Or create a threat: Self Destruct (1), the Cluster erupts into fragments of supersonic material, dealing its remaining talisman 
to every exorcist within a short distance of it regardless of if they have cover or not. The Obelisk then appears in its place. 
The Cluster immediately dies, and a new one cannot be created until pressure increases.  
 
 
4.​Blood in The Corner Of Your Eye  
 
The Obelisk is impossible to pin down. When not directly observed, it is always an Extreme Distance away from the exorcists, either on the horizon 
or changing every time they look. The exorcists must expend no less than 2 trauma questions and the Nail of Abel to pin it down long enough to 
enter its palace and execute it, and it is considered always within its palace. Once per hunt the Obelisk may teleport mere centimeters behind the 
exorcist's field of vision, freezing them in place with its sheer presence, forcing them to roll PSYCHE in secret (other exorcists may not know the 
result of another’s roll). On a failure, an exorcist gains the Enraptured hook. It teleports away before an exorcist has a chance to catch a glimpse of 
it.​
​
If no exorcists gain the Enraptured hook, the Obelisk gains another use of this ability when pressure increases. 
 
5.​The Playpen of a Juvenile God 
 
The investigation area changes drastically, completely warping the nature of reality. This can be a seemingly infinitely tall tower extending up into 
the sky or down into the earth, with paranaturally large floors containing entire sections of the investigation area inside of it and even their own 
skies, a scattered collection of broken islands floating across a bottomless void, or an Escher-esque folding dream world. Travelling between these 
separate parts of the investigation area is always impossible, but can be traversed by finding Doors guarded by traces and drifters that travel to a 
random or specific area.  
 
This is not just a psychic domain, but an actual alteration of the physical world. Humans do not seem to notice the changed environment at all. 
Once per hunt, the Obelisk can shuffle the locations of the areas at will, keeping Doors in the same location but changing their destinations. The 
change reverts harmlessly once the Sin is executed or spared. 
 
 


 
6.​Ultimum Magnum Opus 
 
The Obelisk’s transmutative effect is far more viral and quick-setting than normal. Everything mundane within a 10 meter radius of itself is 
instantly transmuted into whatever material the Obelisk is made of, including liquids, fabric, vapor, and mundane creatures. This radius increases 
by 15 meters each time pressure increases. The transmutation can also cause random or nonsensical alterations to structures in the area, such as 
flipping floor plans upside down, or making cars float. The Obelisk gains the ability to attack exorcists through cover by extending a tendril of this 
transmutative force. 
 
7.​The Cries of Stepping Stones 
 
The Obelisk’s psychophysical form is incomprehensible, and trying to interpret the senses assaulting one’s mind is almost fatal. 1/scene when an 
exorcist looks at the Obelisk in any way, even in photos, videos, or reflections, privately tell them that they must either: 
●​
Take 1d3 stress 
●​
Take the Enraptured hook. 
An exorcist must hide which choice they took, or hide the fact that they chose at all.​
 
Mundane humans gain the ability to see the Obelisk without supernatural abilities. Whenever a mundane human looks directly at the Obelisk, roll 
1d3: 
1.​ They close their eyes quick enough to spare their mind and body. 
2.​ Their flesh begins to warp into a lovecraftian abomination, but they retain some semblance of their sanity. Roll 1d2; on a failure the NPC 
dies when the obelisk is executed. 
3.​ Their mind and body break, twisting into a creature cultishly loyal to the obelisk. They become a semi-human version of a Fragment, and 
seek to drag other humans to the Obelisk or force them to look at it. 
When pressure increases, roll the dice for one NPC the players have met. 
 
8.​Eternity Looks To The Left 
 
It is a remarkable achievement, to make an Obelisk notice you. Somehow, you have made your actions worthy of the barest glimpse from the 
deafening gaze of an eldritch entity. The Obelisk gains a long-distance cone, which it can project from itself however it pleases 1/round as a 
reaction. Any living being that starts their turn inside of this cone immediately takes stress as if from a risk die reaction, and again if they end their 
turn inside of it. If this causes an injury, the exorcist immediately gains a Hook as the cone transmutes a chunk of their flesh. This instantly kills any 
mundane creature it touches, and can easily crack, crumple, mutate, transmute, or disintegrate any part of the environment it passes over.  
 
Outside of combat, this range increases to extreme, and slowly scans the area. 1/pressure the GM may suddenly cause a complication or threat 
even if the Obelisk itself is not present and it is not a combat scene, as the gaze wanders close to the exorcists. 
 
The Obelisk can instead project this cone out of any Fragment in the scene, or out of a Cluster if it has one. 
 
ENRAPTURED 
 
Enraptured is a unique 4-section hook that does not tick up in any way other than gaining another instance of it. When the hook fills, the Exorcist 
afflicted goes mad and sides with the Obelisk until the next rest. The exorcist may reveal their betrayal in a grandiose way or covertly attempt to 
sabotage another player’s actions. When sabotaging, the exorcist privately lets the GM know, who then rolls an appropriate skill of the player’s 
choice against the targeted player’s roll. If the Enraptured exorcist has more successes, the action fails. If all exorcists are Enraptured at the same 
time, the mission is considered a failure and a bombardment is issued.
 
 


 
EEL 
 
Obsessive and paranoid, the Eel is a sin that is born out of an intense desire to be taken 
away or protected.  
 
Eels are fiercely defensive over the identities and locations of their host, often causing massive 
damage to infrastructure and public records in order to protect them. They will often sever ties 
with friends, family, and anyone that has a shot at their host while they are “vulnerable”. 
 
Eels commonly manifest in: 
 
●​
Victims of repeated abuse who lack a protector. 
●​
People who feel trapped physically, or are unable to leave a relationship. 
●​
People who desire someone to protect them from others. 
 
Primary emotion: Vulnerability 
 
Vibes: Silent Hill 2 Remake (2024), South of Midnight (2025), Gris (2018) 
 
Appearance and Psychophysiology: Eels are always long, wrapping things, closing off their 
domain from the outside world like the ancient jormungandr encircling the earth. This can 
manifest as ribbonlike tendrils, a Eeling body, or encroaching branch structures. They are 
always massive in physical size for the purposes of their “grasp”, but always possess a main 
body (usually smaller) which they manifest to commune with their host and other beings. Their 
physical size is always big enough to encroach upon the entire investigation area at high 
pressure, and is incredibly massive even at lower CAT.  
 
Behaviour: Of all the sin types, the Eel is undeniably the most sentient. They are able to 
clearly communicate with other beings able to interact with them, and rarely show direct 
hostility until they perceive a being as a direct threat to their host. Their singular, narrow 
minded goal is the removal of any risk to their host’s security and safety, which can often have 
disastrous results as time wears away at their sanity. Protection can range from social 
isolation, to physical isolation, to active elimination of connections and “threats” in the 
investigation area. If the exorcists are somehow able to convince the paranoid creature they 
are there to protect the host or deal with a threat to the host, the Eel will tentatively accept a 
deal, and may even demanifest without need for sparing or trauma questions. 
 
 
ARCHIVE EELS 
 
WHITESTONE 
After an inflammatory debate with their parents about the nature of their identity and orientation, Subject CL-1311 was ejected from their 
household, where they immediately manifested a CAT4, type 1 Eel that began assimilating a “safe haven” from the nearby downtown ██████ 
area. Uniquely, its protection seemed to extend to those of a similar identity to the host, a recorded 74 individuals who were brought into the 
rapidly expanding fold. An increase in conservative protests leading to a mass shooting alerted CAIN SEER operatives to the manifestation. Time 
to execution, 13 minutes. 0 exorcist casualties, 12 civilian casualties, host alive and brought in for potential CAIN Asset Appraisal. 
 
THE BENEFACTOR 
After an inquisition by their prospective employer, subject ██████ manifested a rare Type-2 Eel under the threat of an unrelated journalist 
unearthing their complicated past with organized crime. Innumerable casualties during the collapse of the journalist’s employer’s main office in 
████████████, ██████, which had been incorporated into the Fortress, as well as 20,300 casualties as the entire ██████ crime family 
and their employees were summarily executed. Total estimated direct and collateral damage 513.4 billion dollars, Hope burnout expected. Time to 
execution 14 hours, 20 minutes, 12 exorcists KIA, virtue Faith deployed to infiltrate the massive nettle of psionic barriers. 
 
ATHENA 
A Category 3 type 3 bound sin formerly controlled by media socialite and high school popular personality ██████, designated Subject CL-694. 
After undergoing an extended campaign of harassment and bullying via anonymous online forums from a fellow student, the sin spontaneously 
manifested and subsequently fried all electronic devices within half a kilometer of the manifestation site and began assembling large hoards of 
internet data centers. Traces took on the unique form of automated social media bots that harassed the original proponents of the bullying 
campaign in the host’s stead. Sole casualty of the host, time to execution 5 days, 4 hours, 39 minutes. 
TRAUMAS 
The Admin, as the Eel, answers the following questions, then establishes a trauma based on the truthful answers. 
●​
Why has no one come to your aid? 
●​
Who or what is threatening your safety? 
●​
Who do you trust more than anyone else in the world? 
 
For every question the exorcists answer, they can counter a sin’s reaction, rolling 1D3 after the sin acts. Reduce any stress suffered by all targets by the amount on the die, and 
the sin immediately takes that many slashes on its execution clock from the psychic trauma, which cannot reduce it below 1. 
 


 
PALACE 
Eel Palaces are unique in that they form as bubbles wherever their tendrils concentrate the most. They are static and small, ranging anywhere 
from small peripheral storage rooms to a grand hall at the taproot. They are always protected by a nigh impassible thicket, causing movement to 
be extremely slow and dealing 1d3 stress to any exorcist who tries to move within short distance of a palace entrance, unless they expend a 
blasphemy power or answer a trauma question which negates the effect for an entire scene. 
 
Eel palaces are typically: restricting, narrow, mazelike, thorned, constricting, sticky, cramped, claustrophobic, creaking. 
PRESSURE: FORTRESS 
The Eel’s ultimate goal is the protection of their host. This ranges from simply interjecting during altercations, to actively hunting out and 
eliminating threats or things that spark fear in the host. The more pressure increases, the more maniacal the Eel gets in its pursuit of absolute 
security. The biggest accomplishment will be their Fortress, a massive physical barrier cobbled together from their surroundings to protect both the 
Eel’s host and psychophysical heart. The fortress is always incredibly massive, representative of the Eel’s own physical bulk. It is rough shod or at 
least unstable, held together only by the infestation of the Eel running throughout it. 
 
When the exorcists arrive, the Fortress will simply be a normal building or location, where the Eel’s heart is contained and where their brambles 
spread out from. Every time pressure increases, the investigation area is ransacked and more material is added to the fortress, growing it in size 
and increasing the amount of traces that patrol it. The more pressure goes on, the more elaborate and defended the fortress becomes. This can 
manifest as anything from a massive tree, to a guarded tower or pit, or even a sprawling complex of walls and sentries. Humans do not notice the 
areas changing, and will simply maneuver around them if possible. 
 
When pressure reaches 6+, the Fortress is completed and the Eel can summon more traces the closer the exorcists are to the heart: 
●​
Extreme distance: 1 trace per scene which does not consume a tension move, up to 1 trace. 
●​
Long distance: 3 traces per scene which does not consume a tension move, up  to 3 traces. 
●​
Short distance: 1 trace every other round, up to 3 traces. 
All traces immediately wither and disappear if the sin is Spared or Executed. 
TRACES 
Serpent 
 
The Serpent is an enforcer, a protector that makes sure nothing can get close enough to even think about threatening the host. While 
diminutive alone, their strength lies in their sheer numbers and ability to overwhelm their enemy with traps and tricks. 
 
Serpent (sins) Execution talisman 1 (solo), 3 (group), 6 (swarm), 8 (horde), 10 (tide). 
 
●​
Takes +1 slashes from area of effect attacks. 
●​
Deals +1 stress to an exorcist that has taken damage from another group of Serpents within the same round. 
 
Reactions: 
 
Inflict Stress: Attacks with: barbed spears, crude arrows, poking swords, preset traps, poisoned sticks (1): 2 stress, (2/3): 1 stress, (4+): 
0 stress. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: Call for aid, surround an exorcist, hurl a massive volley of weapons, alert the Eel, call forth the 
Brambles, retreat into a thick Bramble, bark orders. 
 
 
 


 
EXECUTION 8 + PRESSURE + CAT 
ATTACKS WITH: Choking tendrils, crushing limbs, sharp fangs, thorny brambles, spiked tail, projectile spines, whirling saws, toxic fumes. 
COMPLICATIONS: Cover the floor in brambles, trap an exorcist in a cocoon, collapse a wall, cover a doorway in vines, teleport via roots, emit a 
cloud of tear gas, use a Domain. 
THREATS: Constrict around an exorcist, rear up to spew a barrage of thorns, summon a horde of Serpents, impale an exorcist, use a Domain. 
 
A Eel regenerates its talisman twice as fast as a normal sin, but attacking its Heart directly deals +1 slashes to it. The innermost chamber of its 
Fortress contains its Heart and the entrance to its palace. 
 
AFFLICTIONS 
Given by a threat, hook, or tension move. 
 
1.​ Walled Garden: All actions made are hard if you are approaching a human or exorcist while doing so (physically or socially). 
2.​ Lone Wolf: Whenever you rest, you and anyone that rests with you rolls one less rest die. You may choose not to rest. 
3.​ In Self Defense: You automatically assume all aggression towards you is hostility, including team mates, and will react as if they are 
enemies. 
4.​ The Gatekeeper: You become fundamentally unable to trespass or go where you are not allowed or invited. You can take 1d3 stress to 
ignore a single instance of this rule. 
5.​ Withdrawn: You cannot benefit from ally agenda abilities that would grant you things such as XP, rest dice, psyche bursts, or scrip. 
6.​ Pleasant Island: Permanently add to your agenda: remain alone. 
 
Or improvise: make a roll hard or risky. 
 
SEVERE ATTACK: CHOKE 
 
The Sin can use this ability on a ‘1’ on a risk roll. They can only use it once per mission. 
 
Target an exorcist. Any other exorcists nearby must decide to fly to their aid. Any that don’t offer aid cannot participate.  
 
Start with a pool of 6d6. Then remove one dice for each of the following. If an answer is “no”, you or someone aiding you can 
immediately make an action to rectify the answer, with only a few moments to act, suffering consequences as normal if they fail. 
 
●​
Do you feel safe, or confident in your ability to protect yourself? 
●​
Is an ally willing to sacrifice their life for your safety (they can describe how)? 
●​
Are you near a wall or solid cover? 
●​
Is the Eel hindered, distracted, or under duress in any way? 
 
Then roll the dice. The Eel strangles the exorcist’s very soul in sharp nettles, carving into their psyche with serrated vines. Mundane 
objects and unupgraded service weapons in a short distance around them are instantly shredded to ribbons and they are rendered 
immobile.  
●​
The exorcist and any aiding them take 1 stress for each die rolled, no matter the result. 
●​
For every ‘1’ that comes up, the Eel chokes out a vital point in their chakra, dealing 1 additional stress and cutting into their soul, 
forcing them to expend 2 psyche bursts. if an exorcist does not have psyche bursts to spend, they must roll for sin for each 
psyche burst they are unable to pay. 
●​
For every ‘6’ that comes up, the exorcist heals 1d3 stress as toxic grace washes out of their purged soul. 
 
 


 
DOMAINS: Pick three 
 
●​ Bloody Hands Make Light Work  
 
The Eel’s body gains the ability to rapidly branch from itself. The Eel may, at any time, choose to split while moving, going off in two separate 
directions. Each branched form starts off with a 4 slash talisman, which reduces by one for each additional split; at 2 bodies they have 4 slashes 
each, at 4 bodies they have 3 slashes, at 8 bodies they have 2 slashes, etc cetera. The size of the talisman visually correlates with how withered, 
weak, or frail the body looks. Each branch is technically its own separate being, but can send information to the other bodies via their connective 
tether. 
 
As part of its severe attack, the Eel may negate one exorcist's attempt at aid via sacrificing one of their bodies to occupy them or grapple them. 
 
●​ Grasping At Shortened Straws  
 
The Eel gains the ability to inflict exorcists with its own obsessive delusions, gaining access to a special affliction. Whenever pressure increases, 
the Eel may infect one exorcist with the following affliction: 
 
CHAINED: 
The GM asks the exorcist the question: which ally means the most to you? You may now only benefit from and give Setup/Teamwork and aid 
during Severe Attacks to the chosen player, and doing so to or from other allies is impossible. Whenever the chosen player takes stress or gives 
Setup/Teamwork to another player other than yourself, you take 1 stress.  
 
Losing sight of the chosen exorcist causes the victim to entirely disregard their other allies and duties, regardless of their situation, until they find 
the chosen player again. 
 
●​ The Burning Of Bridges 
 
The Eel’s paranoia seeps into the world around it. As a tension move, the Eel may make locks, barricades, bridges, and other means of barring 
entry hard to bypass for a scene, unless targeted by supernatural effects, and failing a roll to bypass them triggers an alarm, tripwire, or other 
device that instantly rolls a risk die. On a 1, the area around the exorcists goes into further lockdown, creating a 6 slash “lockdown” talisman that 
makes movement and entry/egress hard or impossible by default. While in lockdown, windows close, doors lock, shutters close, and lights of any 
kind turn off. 
 
The first time a risk die is rolled in any scene, if it is 3 or lower, the Eel may summon an “investigation squad” of Serpentd to the exorcists’ area in 
addition to any other effects.  
 
●​ Strolling Down The Path of Most Resistance 
 
The Eel gains the ability to place unique sessile constructs known as Brambles. At the start of the hunt, the GM chooses two points of interest for 
the investigation and covers them in Brambles. While inside a Bramble, moving requires a roll that is risky and deals 1 stress for every short 
distance moved unless the exorcists are very slow and careful about their travel. This effect can be dispelled within a long distance for 1 scene by 
expending a psyche power to get rid of it, or permanently by expending a trauma question, which does 1 slash to the Eel indirectly. Traces are 
immune to Brambles. 
 
Every time pressure increases, the GM chooses two new points in the investigation area to infest with Brambles, which can include a place where 
the exorcists currently are as Brambles sprout suddenly in the area. 
 
1/hunt on a risk roll of 1 while inside a Bramble, the GM may separate all exorcists in groups of 2 to separate locations within 3 miles, emerging 
from other brambles via teleportation. 
 
●​ A Crumbling Wall on the Cliff Face  
 
The creation of the fortress can now entail rearranging the landscape itself, forming cliff faces, giant walls, bottomless ravines, towering mesas, 
and other sorts of impassable terrain that would otherwise be impossible to form. The higher pressure gets, the more extreme and isolating the 
area becomes (This can also include moving and merging entire buildings into the Fortress if the area is primarily urban).​
​
The Fortress itself becomes a sort of mini investigation zone, with its own points of interest adopted from the base investigation area. While within 
the Fortress, there is always a small patrol of traces in every scene, though they are mostly passive and relatively easy to sneak past or go 
around. 
 
●​ The Thousand Mile Road  
 
The Eel’s body is almost omni-present throughout the investigation area; crawling over every surface as roots, draped over every building like 
cloth, or wound around roads and buildings like a massive serpent. Dealing damage to or touching this extension of its body does not deal stress, 
but does alert it to the exorcist’s presence. It gains the ability to immediately manifest its primary body anywhere along its form, effectively 
teleporting, and can extend up to a short distance away from the nearest extension of itself.  
 
1/mission the GM may make movement within a scene risky while not indoors or out of possible sight of the Eel, as it emerges and surveys the 
area on a routine patrol. On a 1 on a risk die, the exorcist makes a subtle noise that attracts the attention of the Eel, which sends a horde of traces 
to investigate. This ability refreshes when pressure increases. 
 
While within the Fortress or when pressure reaches 6+, the Eel can be in up to 2 places at once, and is alerted on a 2 or lower on the risk die. 
 
 
 


 
●​ Lovely Barbs of Mistletoe 
 
Information about the host is rapidly redacted, including physical copies, memorabilia, and digital archives. 1/pressure whenever the exorcists 
discover the location of a key piece of information and attempt to travel to it, roll 1d3: 
1.​ The area has already been ransacked of any relevant information that the Eel and its trace could get their hands on. All rolls to find 
information are impossible, but can be made hard by abilities and blasphemy powers that could aid them in their search. It should be 
obvious that the area has very recently been tampered with. 
2.​ The exorcists arrive just as a detachment of traces are approaching the place to ransack it, which the Eel does not need to spend a 
Tension Move to summon. If they defeat the traces before they steal the evidence, or ambush them after they have already collected it, 
they gain +1D on rolls to find relevant information in the hoard for the scene. 
3.​ The area is untouched, but the exorcists notice signs of an approaching swarm of traces, which the Eel does not need to spend a Tension 
Move to summon. They may either rush to complete their search (making rolls hard or risky by default), split up to hold off the 
approaching forces, or take their time and deal with the traces as they arrive. 
 
If the exorcists arrive afterwards, or fail to stop a trace that escapes with the evidence, one of the trauma questions becomes unanswerable until 
they investigate inside the Fortress to locate the stolen evidence. If they find it inside the Fortress, they immediately learn the answer to one 
trauma question as bountiful evidence is before them. 
 
●​ Rapping at the Locked Door 
 
The Eel forbids a certain word or phrase, perhaps an entire page of information, washing it away from the public consciousness. Every being in the 
area will treat the information as either nonexistent or impossible to express or pronounce in any way. This can include more widespread 
information, such as certain types of technology, world events, entire people like celebrities, and such. Beings cannot speak, write, or signal 
specific information, but can read and learn it from pre-existing sources. Every time pressure increases, the Eel may choose one more piece of 
information at any time. Once pressure reaches 6+, the host’s name and biography is forbidden. 
 
If used on a physical type of non-weapon object, it becomes blurry and distorted similar to a Smother user’s powers, making it hard or impossible 
to operate or interact with. 
 
 


 
 
 
SHARK 
 
Sinister and ominous, the Shark arrives as a harbinger of misfortune and doom, playing the 
architect of their host’s worst possible path by invoking bizarre and impossible tragic 
circumstances.  A Shark will appear as an always distant and obscured figure in their host’s 
life, appearing before unfortunate disaster after disaster strikes. Their actions are particularly 
devastating as they often lead to the manifestation of other sins with the tragedy of their 
presence. 
 
Sharks commonly manifest in: 
 
●​
People who believe the world is out to get them, or that life is rigged against them. 
●​
People who suffer from extreme arrogance and a sense of invincibility. 
●​
People with obsessive levels of cautions and paranoia. 
 
Primary emotion: Dread 
 
Vibes: Final Destination (2005), It Follows (2014), A Series of Unfortunate Events (Novel) 
 
Appearance and Psychophysiology: The Shark is a silent, ever-present figure. It is almost 
always unnaturally tall, and cloaked in a shadow covering any defining feature. They are often 
shrivelled and frail-looking, with their form “filling out” as their host spirals further into despair 
from their torment. Even if mundane humans are unable to see it, the Shark will instill a deep 
sense of dread and foreboding in its surroundings. 
 
Sharks never possess eyes, in any manifestation. This normally would be dismissed as a 
coincidence but all recorded instances dating back to its first appearance always mention a 
distinct lack of any ocular organ of any kind. 
 
Behaviour: The Shark is perhaps the strangest of all sins, as its manifestation almost never 
results in interaction with their host. They will play from afar, setting up and sabotaging the 
world around them to trigger tragedy to torment the psyche of their host, trailing the scent of 
tragedy like a drop of blood in the ocean. They do not seem to draw sustenance from their 
host’s grief, only a sense of intrigue or entertainment. They are incredibly difficult to pin down, 
and will often vanish as soon as they are not directly observed.  
 
 
 
ARCHIVE SHARKS 
 
MATCHBOX 
Originally manifested in ██████, Kentucky by subject ██████████ before being subconsciously bound and suppressed until much later in 
their life. Hypothesized to be responsible for nearly 230 false readings and CAT6 sin alerts which resulted in exorcists arriving to an entirely 
unaffiliated area. The resulting buildup of psychic ire within the bound Shark overflowed catastrophically while they were ████, leading to the 
catastrophic failure of power and gas lines in every building within 7 miles, leading to a mass pipeline detonation totaling nearly ████████████ 
in civilian casualties. Resultant devastation wiped from human memory with Hope and a fake terrorist cell was invented to support a more 
favorable narrative. All deployed exorcists confirmed dead, and as such are unable to be executed for allowing mass hysteria. 
 
TRICKLE 
CAT2 Form I Shark observed plagueing a crumbling oil cracking platform in the North Atlantic Ocean, leading to increasingly brutal workplace 
safety hazards. The host, eventually identified as the director of the rig, was pinned for extreme budget cuts and workplace safety violations, 
despite their previous record as an obsessively paranoid overly cautious individual. Eventually led to the suicide of the host, and subsequently the 
demanifestatiom of the sin as a large portion of the rig broke away and slid into the ocean. 
 
THE RED DOT 
Form I Shark targeting Subject 2OMK. While the manifestation itself was never sighted during the investigation, its presence in the form of a 
sickening aura of grace suffused the investigation area, leading to considerably delayed execution of the host. Its ire appeared visually as a 
marker similar to the laser sight of a rifle, and tracked on the forehead of the host and several of their family members preceding their bizarrely 
complicated deaths. 218 recorded civilian casualties, host was eventually eliminated by the Shark before the exorcists could successfully locate it, 
causing it to demanifest. All exorcists involved docked scrip for failure to execute. 
TRAUMAS 
The Admin, as the Shark, answers the following questions, then establishes a trauma based on the truthful answers. 
●​
What is out to get you? 
●​
What do you fear losing the most? 
●​
What soothes your nerves? 
 
For every question the exorcists answer, they can counter a sin’s reaction, rolling 1D3 after the sin acts. Reduce any stress suffered by all targets by the amount on the die, and 
the sin immediately takes that many slashes on its execution clock from the psychic trauma, which cannot reduce it below 1.  
 


 
PALACE 
The Shark’s palace is a culmination of the unstable fate it inflicts upon its host. It is incredibly difficult to navigate, often more so than the 
surrounding area, and always brings a smothering sense of imminent collapse. Every non-teamwork action taken within the palace is 
permanently risky, no effect can bypass this. Even if the Shark does not attack, something will fail or crumble and cause injury to the exorcists 
(though it does 2 less slashes than the Shark would directly). Once the sin has been spared or executed, a 6 slash Timer talisman is generated as 
the exorcists attempt to flee before it fully collapses in on itself. Every action taken rolls a risk die: (1-2) 3 slashes, (3-4) 2 slashes, (5-6) 1 slash. 
The admin may generate a 3-6 length Escape talisman depending on how “deep” the exorcists are into the palace. If the Timer talisman fills up 
before the Escape talisman, the palace fully collapses, dealing one injury to any exorcist still inside. If an exorcist takes their last injury this way, 
they are caught in the closing and suffer instant death. 
 
Shark palaces are typically: unstable, teetering over the abyss, rickety, elevated, collapsing, cobbled-together, creaking. 
PRESSURE: TRAGEDY 
The spiral into calamity begins as a slow and winding arc, before exponentially whirling to lethal effect.  
 
Every time pressure increases, the Shark must mark 1d6x5 innocent civilians with a visual tell or some other way to identify their position as prey, 
which can include important NPCs the players have met at least once before. If at least half of the marked civilians all congregate within a long 
distance of the host, something tragic and unexplainable will happen that kills everyone in the radius but the host without intervention. This cannot 
be avoided unless they are prevented from entering the area or source of the effect in the first place, and even if the exorcists are involved they 
will never be able to save everyone. If the tragedy happens at all, the Shark gains +2 to its execution talisman size and fully heals. 
 
If less than half of the civilians show up to the event, it will only kill one person (who can be saved with intervention), and the Shark permanently 
reduces its execution talisman by 1d3. 
TRACES 
Slip 
 
The slip is a shoulder-devil, a “weight on the shoulders” felt by any poor sod it haunts. While leeching from a host, they will experience 
increasingly drastic bouts of bad luck that will make them irritable and volatile, rendering them unhelpful or even directly obstructive to the 
investigation. They delight in taunting exorcists, and attacking it runs the risk of harming their human host. 
 
Slip (sin) Execution talisman 3 (solo), 4 (pair). 
●​
Can only appear on a human NPC. 
●​
Can cause their host to take any damage from mundane weapons that are used to attack them. Killing the host stuns the Slip. 
 
Reactions: 
 
Inflict Stress: Attacks with: wet floors, loose pipes, randomly accelerated objects, falling rubble (1): 3 stress, (2/3): 2 stress, (4+): 1 
stress. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: Cause a civilian to stumble in the way of an attack, knock out their host, go invisible, switch hosts, 
initiate a Rube Goldberg effect, run into traffic. 
 


 
EXECUTION 1 + PRESSURE + CAT 
ATTACKS WITH: Loose signs, cracked tiles, wet floors, eroded pipes, unsound ceilings, falling items, frantic animals, out of control vehicles, other 
exorcist’s attacks, errant civilians. (1) 4 stress, (2/3): 3 stress, (4+): 2 stress. 
COMPLICATIONS: Disappear from view, cause mass panic, cause a vehicle to pop a tire, overflow the sewers, burst a steam pipe, start an 
electrical fire, set off a fire hydrant, cause civilians to wander in the way, use a Domain. 
THREATS: Collapse a small building, cause a vehicular crash, harm civilians, topple a tree, flee the scene, imbue massive amounts of 
unluckiness into an exorcist, give a damning portent, trigger a power outage, use a Domain. 
 
The Shark may immediately remanifest at half of its maximum execution talisman after being shunted to its palace instead of waiting to 
regenerate. 
 
AFFLICTIONS 
Given by a threat, hook, or tension move. 
 
1.​ Under The Ladder: Whenever you enter any form of door, entryway, or portal, roll a risk die. On a 3 or lower, immediately take 1 nonlethal 
stress as something bangs into or falls on you, unless you move extremely slowly and cautiously. 
2.​ Lucky 13: Every time you roll the dice, add the values together and add 1d3. Once per scene if the total equals 13, the action has severe 
consequences. 
3.​ Broken Mirror: Once per scene on a roll of a 6 on a risk die targeting someone else, the enemy rolls another risk die targeting you, as the 
misfortune glances off of the original target. 
4.​ Spilled Salt: Moving quickly or long distances in a conflict scene rolls its own unique 1d6. On a 1, you trip and stop moving completely. 
5.​ Open Umbrella: Whenever you step out of an interior into open air you are always splashed, soaked, splattered, or otherwise dirtied by 
chance. While dirty, rolls made to speak to others are hard by default, and roll -1D if appearances matter. 
6.​ Black Cat: permanently add to your agenda: accept your fate. 
 
Or improvise: make a roll hard or risky. 
 
SEVERE ATTACK: CATASTROPHE 
 
The Sin can use this ability on a ‘1’ on a risk roll. They can only use it once per mission. 
 
Target an exorcist. Any other exorcists nearby must decide to fly to their aid. Any that don’t offer 
aid cannot participate.  
 
Start with a pool of 6d6. Then remove one dice for each of the following. If an answer is “no”, you or someone aiding you 
can immediately make an action to rectify the answer, with only a few moments to act, suffering consequences as normal if 
they fail. 
 
●​ Are you certain of your future? 
●​ Are you on stable and/or open ground? 
●​ Do you renounce the idea of luck? 
●​ Is the Shark hindered, distracted, or under duress in any way? 
 
Then roll the dice. The Shark initiates a dazzlingly complex butterfly effect that takes an entire round to progress. The 
admin must describe a small portion of this butterfly effect with each risk roll of that round, including areas the exorcists 
normally would not be able to see. If an aiding exorcist sees part of the orchestration, they may attempt to rectify it on their 
turn; a success reduces the stress-per-dice taken by the target and aiding allies by one. The exorcist and any aiding them 
take 3 stress for each die rolled, no matter the result. 
●​ If at least one ‘1’ comes up, the Shark deals an additional stress for each die rolled. 
●​ If two or more ‘1’s come up, the targeted exorcist immediately takes an injury as the orchestra of unluck draws to a 
gruesome crescendo. In addition to the injury, all nearby humans within a long range are instantly killed by the 
runoff of the butterfly effect concluding. 
 
 


 
DOMAINS: Pick three 
 
●​ I Was Observing The Birds  
 
The Shark may use a tension move to cause the host to become utterly unreachable. Circumstance and chance will always prevent exorcists 
from fully interacting with the host in any meaningful way. Bikers cross their path while chasing them, they lose them in a crowd, a car passes by, 
they slip, a drunken citizen accosts them, etc. Similarly, the host will never be able to properly hear or get a good look at the exorcists, but they can 
leave pieces of writing and notes to speak with them if they figure that out. 
 
●​ Spiral In For The Kill 
 
The Shark gains the ability to tug the yarn of luck, gaining access to a special affliction. Whenever pressure increases, the Shark may infect one 
exorcist with the following affliction: 
 
MURPHY’S LAW: 
The first time per scene an afflicted exorcist would take stress that would cause an injury, instead deal half that stress plus 1 to two other exorcists. 
If this would cause those exorcists to take an injury, repeat this step. 
 
●​ The Trolley Grinds Ever Closer 
 
Once per mission, the Shark may present exorcists with the following scenario: 
-​
A: Easily prevent the next tragedy from going off entirely, offering a convenient excuse or opportunity to slow down or completely stop the 
marked targets. More innocent civilians will die equal to double the number of Marked targets stopped, but the tragedy will not happen in 
the sense that the shark will not benefit from it. 
-​
B: Easily save innocent civilians equal to double the number of Marked targets present, offering a convenient shield or way to drag them to 
safety. If they do so, at least half of the marked targets will unchangeably arrive at the scene of the next tragedy no matter what the 
exorcists do. 
 
If the exorcists save the Marked targets, one important NPC they know will be spared, but one of that NPC’s friends or loved ones dies 
immediately. If the exorcists save the Unmarked targets, one important NPC they know will die. 
 
●​ Dark Night Of The Soul  
 
Once per mission, the Shark may set out a 3 slash Calamity talisman. Every time a scene passes, the Shark rolls a risk die; on a 3 or lower it 
slashes the talisman once. The exorcists become acutely aware of the impending disaster and have a vague idea of the target, but find it 
incredibly hard to convince any mundane human of their knowledge. If they fail to evacuate the target of the calamity before the Shark fills the 
talisman, widespread destruction ensues.  
 
Miraculously, no humans are harmed in this event, but something inconceivably important to the host is lost forever; a childhood dog is crushed 
under a weak ceiling, a trailer home they spent years saving for is destroyed in a crash, files containing their life’s work are lost in a small but 
zero-casualty fire, a treasured object or memory box is dropped into a flood, etc. While their host is grieving, the Shark increases 1 CAT and deals 
+1 stress with all attacks. If the exorcists find some way to find or restore the lost target, the Shark immediately withers and reduces its execution 
talisman by half. 
 
●​ Little Minds, Tamed By Misfortune 
 
Any NPC in the investigation area who has had themselves or family members subjected to a tragedy from pressure increasing will slowly become 
more hostile and possibly violent towards the host. The more superstitious the individual is, the more likely they are to grow increasingly hostile 
quicker. If one NPC experiences 3 tragedies towards themselves, their friends, or their family, the situation goes out of control. The mourning NPC 
will identify the host as the source of the misfortune, attempting to kill them the next time they are in a scene with the exorcists. 
 
If the attempt is successful the host dies from the attack, turning their Shark into a post-mortem sin, and granting it the ability to target any NPC as 
the survivor of a tragedy. If the attempt fails or is interrupted, the attacking NPC dies from a stroke of bad luck. 
 
●​ Shadows Looming Over The Window 
 
Every time the exorcists make significant headway in the investigation or uncover key clues, they suffer a harmless instance of bad luck. If they 
uncover the correct answer to a trauma question, they roll a risk die. 
 
During a conflict scene with the Shark or its Traces, if any exorcist has purposefully moved a range band closer (from long to short, from short to 
adjacent, etc) to the enemy during their turn, roll 2 risk dice and take the lower. If they have purposefully moved a range band away, roll 2 risk dice 
and take the higher. During a conflict scene with any NPCs, this effect can trigger once both ways. 
 
●​ Under An Unfortunate Sun 
 
The Shark has trapped its host in an endless loop of tragedy. While to the exorcists it is their first time entering the investigation area, the host has 
the approximate knowledge of most events leading up to either their death, or the death of a specific person who triggers the loop. They have 
been trying for around 1D6x10 loops before the current one, but sharing knowledge of future events will inevitably lead to them becoming 
self-fulfilling. Acting against the host (such as restraining them, attacking them, or tricking them) is impossible by default as they have memorized 
your action tree. 
 
The exorcists have three “portent” tokens. Once per scene, the exorcists may spend a portent to act on one piece of advice given by the host. If 
they do, a single action related to that advice (such as searching for a named NPC) succeeds automatically, but the Shark can cause any one 
action to fail that mission in return as it effortlessly predicts their actions. Exorcists may spend another token to neutralize this failure, instead 
rolling like normal, but they grant the Shark another failure to enforce, etc. 
 
 


 
 
●​ Wrong Side Of The Deathbed 
 
The Shark begins to strain the limits of causality, choosing a single NPC important to the host as a Martyr. Every time pressure increases, the 
Martyr will always be present for the tragedy and catch a single glimpse of the host before dying. Afterwards, the Martyr is revived with zero 
memories of the event and will be present for the next tragedy, and so on, forcing the host to repeatedly live through the death of a loved one. 
Every time the Martyr dies, all of the Shark’s traces gain +1 to their execution talisman, permanently. 
 
If the Martyr is ever saved from a tragedy, the exorcist most responsible for saving them immediately gains 1d3 sin and an affliction of the Shark’s 
choice as the Shark offloads the causal blame to them. 
 
 


 
 
SEER SIN MUTATION CATALOG 
 
This section contains extended domains for each of the core sins, based on semi-common mutations observed in the 6 common manifestations. 
 
●​ OGRE: Nothing Ever Changes 
 
The Ogre’s sluggishness seeps into the fabric of time itself. The miasma now slows time the more it infects an area, freezing the wind in place and 
slowing the flow of water to a snail’s pace. Humans begin to literally slow down, completely freezing once pressure reaches 5+. 
 
While inside miasma, exorcists begin to struggle against the stopped flow of time, becoming sluggish and tired. Moving farther than a CAT1 
distance in a single action deals 1 non-injuring stress, and actions related to movement including running, jumping, or avoiding things become 
either hard or risky (or both) by default. Exorcists roll 1/2 CAT less rest die when resting within miasma, unless near open fire or a significant 
source of heat to combat the miasma. 
 
●​ IDOL: Sweet Blood on a Pierced Tongue 
 
The Idol spawns tragedy around itself, weaving its honeyed words into the ears of its victims. 1/mission, the GM may cause a tragic event to unfold 
using its cult, killing people important to an NPC the exorcists have already met. The Idol convinces the NPC that the exorcists were somehow 
responsible for the destruction while the host attempts to bring their loved ones back. The NPC turns into a trace with the following statblock:  
 
Avenger 
 
Tears streak down the mottled and mutated face of this forlorn being. It will protect the idol and its host with its life, promised a chance to 
see the ones they lost again. 
 
Avenger (...exorcist?) Execution talisman, execution talisman 3+CAT. 
 
●​
The avenger gains access to one blasphemy power of the GM’s choice, as their sorrow and devotion collapse into a malformed 
sinseed. 
●​
While dying, the Avenger deals 1d3 sin to all exorcists present as they send off with words of condemnation. 
 
Reactions: 
 
Inflict Stress: Attacks with: psychic powers, mundane weapons, unreal strength (1): 4 stress, (2/3): 2 stress, (4+): 1 stress. 
 
Or: create a complication or threat: scream at the exorcists, fuse or bind with the idol’s traces, cast their blasphemy power, charm 
civilians into aiding them, use human shields. 
 
Or: Threat, Devote (1); The Avenger burns away their life in devotion to the host and their promise, changing their stress dealt to (1) 5 
stress, (2/3) 3 stress, (4+) 2 stress. The Avenger dies after CAT+1 rounds. 
 
●​ HOUND: Damnation of The Moon 
 
The pure vitriol of the Hound emanates from it in a primal scream, calling its pack to its side. The Hound gains the ability to summon its traces as a 
threat during a conflict scene, in larger groups as pressure increases. Alternatively, 1/mission it can direct its scream at a group of humans nearby, 
instantly killing them and turning their corpses into temporary traces that last until the end of the scene. Traces summoned this way deal +1 stress 
to a target as long as it is adjacent to another group of traces. 
 
●​ CENTIPEDE: The Boiling of Tar 
 
The Centipede draws forth all that is toxic and malignant in the investigation area, forming it into its own profane monolith. While within range of 
the monolith, breathing unfiltered air automatically deals (extreme distance) 1 stress, (long distance) 2 stress, (short distance) 3 stress to an 
exorcist immediately. Each time pressure increases, a part of the monolith sloughs off, turning 10% of the investigation area into hazardous terrain 
that instantly destroys things like paper, film, glass, wood, and other fragile materials. If pressure reaches 6+, the monolith completely collapses 
and the entire investigation area is washed in toxic filth, turning all terrain hazardous.  
 
1/scene if an exorcist rolls a risk die for a failed action while within a hazardous zone, they take stress as if the centipede was there; (1) 5 stress, 
(2/3) 3 stress, (4+) 2 stress. 
 
 


 
 
●​ TOAD: A Grinning Wolf in Golden Wool 
 
1/mission the Toad can pull a series of impossibly far-reaching strings, getting a hold of 1 person critically important to at least one exorcist on the 
team. The Toad can pull them out as a hostage during any conflict scene, which immediately ends the conflict scene. If anyone attempts to attack 
the Toad while the hostage is restrained, the hostage dies instantly. The Toad makes an offer, which the exorcists must pay at least 1d4 of (they 
are not told how many they must pay, and must pay until they feel they have paid enough): 
●​
A psyche burst, as the Toad devours it, up to 3 times. 
●​
1d3+1 stress, as the Toad devours their blood, up to 3 times. 
●​
3 KP, as the Toad devours it, up to 3 times. 
 
For every cost paid, the Toad increases its Execution Talisman by 1, permanently. The Toad will then proceed to attempt to kill the hostage 
anyways as a threat. If allowed to act on this threat, or if not enough costs are paid, the hostage dies instantly. The Toad then vanishes an 
extreme distance away before exorcists have a chance to deal any stress to it. 
 
●​ LORD: Stricture of Complacence 
 
The Kingdom becomes incredibly idyllic and peaceful, filled with pastures, gardens, casinos, and any other indulgence the host desires. Humans 
will always resist or avoid any sort of exertion or intense questioning, and are generally subdued. 
 
At the beginning of the hunt, the Lord asks the exorcists: what do you desire the most?. 1/mission, the Lord may summon a tantalizing comfort 
near the exorcists related to their desire. All exorcists must immediately roll PSYCHE to resist (if they wish to resist) or immediately rest and treat 
all rolls that require exertion or forsaking indulgence as hard or risky (or both) until the next rest. However, all exorcists who rest may roll an extra 
resting die by taking sin equal to the number rolled. 
 
If 1 or fewer exorcists are forced to rest, the Lord refreshes this ability when pressure increases. 
 
 


 
 
PERFECT SIN SIGHTING LOG 
 
PERFECT KRAKEN 
 
●​
Perfect Nightmare: The Kraken’s Nightmares may assume the form of any mundane humans the exorcists have met before. 1/Pressure 
up to 3 NPCs of the Admin’s choice may transform into a Nightmare or group of Nightmares and attack the exorcists, taking a single 
reaction each before the exorcists can act.  
●​
Absolute Terror Field: Psychogenic Death always permanently imbues its phobia to all targets and exorcists who provide aid, regardless 
of the roll. 
●​
Vile Visage: 1/scene the Kraken may automatically afflict an exorcist with an affliction hook if they look directly at it or its traces’ face. 
●​
Perfect Darkness: The Kraken may teleport up to a CAT distance between any two shadows as a 1-3 risk roll, and may grab and attack 
an exorcist’s shadow directly instead of their physical body. 
 
PERFECT OBELISK 
 
●​
The Great Truth: The Obelisk may inflict the Enraptured hook on any exorcist in the investigation area once per pressure. 
●​
Perfect Husk: The Obelisk’s Great Work no longer increases the CAT of its size, but it increases its size by 1 CAT every time pressure 
increases. At large enough sizes, this may require exorcists to navigate the interior of the obelisk like its own investigation area. 
●​
Omnipresence: The Obelisk may deal 1 irreducible stress to any exorcist in addition to its reaction, ignoring cover and any form of stress 
reduction or shielding. 
●​
Perfect Void: Trauma questions deal -2 slashes to the Obelisk, but may be used a total of 5 times (allowing repeat usage). 
 
PERFECT EEL 
 
●​
Perfect Binding: Execution of the Eel’s host becomes impossible, and both the Eel and its host become immune to damage and cannot 
deal stress while in the presence of each other.  
●​
Oubliette: Entering and existing the Fortress covertly without a conflict becomes impossible by any means. 
●​
Ravening Tides: As a tension move, the Eel may empower its hordes, causing any Serpent’s execution talisman to never fall below 1 for 
the scene as constant reinforcements arrive. 
●​
Perfect Isolation: If an exorcist is isolated from other exorcists by any means, they suffer the Walled Garden affliction until the end of the 
next rest. 
 
PERFECT SHARK 
 
●​
Sweet Suffering: Every time a “1” is rolled on a risk die, the Perfect Shark heals 2 slashes on its talisman. 
●​
Butterfly Wings: The Shark and its Traces may forcefully reroll any risk die once per scene, keeping the lowest roll. 
●​
Perfect Victim: After taking any amount of slashes on its execution talisman from an exorcist’s attack, on a 1 on the subsequent risk roll 
the Shark may redirect those slashes towards the nearest creature to it, which can include an exorcist. 
●​
Perfect Tragedy: The pressure 4+ trigger of the Shark’s Tragedy will result in a massive catastrophe that will kill 30% of humans in the 
investigation zone (such as a massive detonation, volcanic eruption, collapse of a building, or a hurricane).
 
 


 
SOLEMN SIMULACRUM:.ARDUS EMULATA. 
 
The EMULATA Project was a top secret weapons development program run by black sites and secret governmental sects beyond even the eye 
and influence of CAIN, dark sciences that breached the Veil in secret. From these machinations rose the Emulata: towering colossi of steel and 
silicon fused to the soul signature of a Sin, the limitless supernatural married to the mundane. An Emulata is no longer the unstable, unpredictable 
monster the Sin once was, its host and itself alike both subdued by their Neuropatterning, forced into subservience. Suffering turned into a 
weapon, and a tool. Suffering, to breed suffering. 
 
Vibes: Metal Gear Solid & Rising:Revengeance (Metal Gears), Death Stranding 2 (Ghost Mechs), Evangelion (EVA Units). 
 
There are nine Emulata total, one for each core sin plus the sins within this document. Emulata follow a few new rules distinct from normal sins: 
●​
Emulata are visible to the naked eye by mundane mortals, and as such are either usually more insidious or stealth-based or rely on cutting 
off communications with the outside world while they do their work. 
●​
Emulata are no longer beholden to the wishes of their host entirely, but are still liable to act out once in a while when fitting. A majority of 
their task will be corruption or destabilization of local peace, government, or infrastructure. These are weapons forged by militaries to 
pursue military goals. This is with exception to the EKATOS EMULATA (Centipede) whose main goal is overt conflict and destruction as an 
act of terrorism. More details on an Emulata’s goals and alternate pressure clock are provided in their dossier profiles. 
●​
Emulata are no longer hard to attack by default, but they have a tiered injury-like system and destroying their shell releases the now 
unrestrained and resentful Sin within, causing a massive surge in grace and giving them a last grandstand. 
●​
When pressure reaches max, some form of local combat arrives if there is one; military, police SWAT, or other such forces arrive in 
masses. This provides a problem for exorcists as for all the world knows, Emulata are simple machines. Revealing their true psychic 
nature may lead the forces to realise a deeper truth. 
 
Running a game with an Emulata obviously breaches completely different emotional and thematic touchstones compared to a normal hunt. 
Emulata hunts are more about war trauma and political intrigue, spy thrillers and the feeling of being a tool or a weapon for war. An Emulata is less 
about a more personal social trauma (most of the time) and is more about the effects of war, conflict, and the military on individuals. 
 
EMULATA PROGRAM: 
●​
EKATOS EMULATA (Centipede) 
○​
Terrorism and overt conflict. The ideal of a war machine. 
●​
AIMA EMULATA (Hound) 
○​
Assassinatiom. Take out the target/s, then either self-destruct or slip out. 
●​
VATHYS EMULATA (Ogre) 
○​
Infrastructure and sabotage. Shut down logistics and communications slowly, like a rot. 
●​
STOMA EMULATA (Toad) 
○​
Heists. Steal everything valuable related to the target and leave. 
●​
NOMOS EMULATA (Lord) 
○​
Cover stories and subterfuge. Alter the narrative to fit the client’s demands. 
●​
DOXA EMULATA (Idol) 
○​
Public manipulation. Entrance the populace into willingly supporting a hostile takeover of the area. 
●​
PYRGOS EMULATA (Obelisk) 
○​
Covert ops. Isolate the target area and make sure neither signal nor communication gets out. 
●​
THILIA EMULATA (Eel) 
○​
Silencing. Wipe information, redact evidence, and make sure whatever the client wants disappears. 
●​
ACHREIOS EMULATA (Kraken) 
○​
Internal sabotage. Fearmonger, engineer internal conflict to make the targets fight against themselves.

ADMIN LORE COMPENDIUM 
 
PROJECT ICARUS 
Project Icarus is the golden child of TEMERITY’s tampering with the forces of sin, the oldest and most extensive since their conception in ███. 
Extensive research of SIN power expression and the Sinseed have culminated in the production of the Icarus Ambrosia: A simple but frighteningly 
effective transferral of a harvested sinseed’s abilities to a human host in the form of artificial blasphemies. It has also shown to, in those not 
already graced, irreversibly grant a sinseed.  
 
Note: In the vast majority of cases, the expiration period of ungraced humans is greatly accelerated by the introduction of a foreign sinseed. 
Solution pending.  
 
Deeper collaboration between Project Icarus and Project FAUST is currently pending approval from the leadership of CAIN ADMIN, and the 
potential of custom engineered blasphemies excites many occultists in the labs of Temerity. 
 
Note: While this does show potential of turning CASTLE’s mobile troops into entirely graced individuals, the serum’s introduced sinseed nearly 
triples the observed effects of “hollowing”. Solution pending. 
 
Icarus AMBROSIA [5 Scrip, Consumable, 3 Uses] 
●​
Feeding this serum to any deceased non-graced human will immediately resurrect them and grant them a sin mark, sinseed, and 
Blasphemy of the Admin’s choice. Roll 1d6.  
○​
On a failure, the human develops terminal illness as a side effect of this rapid introduction of grace, and dies by the end of the 
hunt. If you are able and the human’s body is intact, you may carefully extract the newly grown sinseed to produce another vial of 
Ambrosia during downtime for 1 scrip. 
○​
On a success, the human becomes a CAT1 exorcist and if brought along is recruited by CAIN. 
●​
For an additional 1 scrip per dose you can commission CASTLE to refine the serum to work on currently alive humans, but the roll 
becomes hard. 
 
PROJECT FAUST 
Project FAUST is the most radical research project of the TEMERITY branch: the production, birth, and harvesting of entirely artificial sins. The 
expert occultists insist their creations are entirely unlike the barbarically produced sins employed by terrorists such as UNDER//HEAVEN, and are 
instead truly human-independent, quasi-sentient sin constructs.  
 
Note: Due to an update of the CAIN ADMIN Ethics and Morality Code (11.4421) in 1919, performance has dropped drastically due to the limited 
permitted human test subjects. Appeals to this addendum have been pending since 1920. 
 
Recently, TEMERITY has discovered that by introducing artificial stimuli in the form of illusions via Jaunt dreamwalkers and Palace users, the 
expert occultists of Temerity Labs have been able to produce sins from comatose or otherwise brain-dead brains. This has led to the rather 
disturbing imagery of The Bridge, a massive room of Jaunt and Palace users hooked into disembodied brain jars used in the production of these 
Homunculi. Efficient binding and harvesting of Artificial Sins and their Sinseeds after birth is still a work in progress.  
 
Note: The fatality rate of employed exorcists on the Bridge has dropped an impressive 2% since 1850 due to extensive safety measures taken by 
Temerity. 
 
TEMERITY vehemently denies any association with the manifestation of highly irregular sins in the human populace. 
 
 
 


 
TEMERITY TREATISE ON THE GRACE PHENOMENON 
Grace is the foundation of all that CAIN is and strives against. It originates from a reality parallel and separated from our own, the psychic sea, 
which contains and is influenced by the collective emotions of the entire sphere of human influence. Due to the inherently subjective nature of 
human emotion and by extension the psychic sea it has proven extremely difficult for TEMERITY to develop tools able to measure concrete 
variables. This document is the currently accepted best working theory, hereby referred to the Grace Phenomenon Theory.  
On the Measurement of Grace: 
 
Grace is not a particle, nor a form of energy. Its closest analog to our reality is the idea of a single, continuous field of unbroken existence. Upon 
collision with our reality in an extraneous dimension the psychic sea is filtered through our fabric of reality to produce grace via a mechanism 
TEMERITY does not currently understand, and has accepted may simply be an inner working of reality. The “strength” of grace is not a measure of 
the quantity of grace present, as once again it is not a substance but an entirely unique realization of reality. Instead, it is a quantization of the 
pressure the collision between the planes exerts measured by a scalar field in Abdels (named so after legendary ancient Arabic TEMERITY 
Occultist Izz ud-Din Abdel) and a 5-pole fourier transform denoting the Effective Dominant Emotional Register.  
 
The scalar field simply takes the average recorded pressure in abdels over 1 second in a 10m^2 area, minus the observed constant background 
grace pressure of 3.183e^-4 abdels to calculate the Effective Absolute Grace Pressure of the area. ​
 
Note: Most of the time setting up an elaborate array to effectively calculate the Effective Absolute Grace Pressure accurately is impossible, so a 
portable abdelometer simply does the calculation for one point (itself) instead of a large scalar field averaged over a smaller period of 0.1 seconds, 
but the device can be toggled to average up to 10 minutes of readings. This reading is usually referred to as Effective Local Grace Pressure. 
 
The 5-pole fourier transform records common “temperatures” of emotional registers of ambient grace in an area using a delicate device known as 
a Pathometer.  
 
Note: A Pathometer is worth approximately 13x more than the life of a standard CAT2 exorcist due to the paranatural materials and processes 
used in its construction. Failure to return a Pathometer or destruction of a Pathometer is grounds for severe punishment decided by their 
commanding Castle handler. 
 
Unlike the simple up and down of thermodynamics, this “temperature” can slide on up to 10 axes between all 5 poles (those being Ardor, 
Melancholy, Bliss, Ennui, and Repulsion). The Dominant Emotional Temperature Register notes the frequency of the recorded temperature on 
each axis averaged over time. These “chords” between each of the poles are recorded over a full minute, before subtracting the recorded Ambient 
Emotional Temperature Register of the region if available (TEMERITY field agents across the world monitor the most common frequencies of 
emotional temperature in heavily populated areas) and averaging each axis with others of its kind into the DETR using an A.M.B.E.R. graph. 
Comparing a DETR reading to comprehensive pattern grouping records SEER can forecast not only the type of Irregularity manifested with 97% 
certainty, but also the Domains it manifests with almost 34% certainty.
